### [1] [An AI-Based Solution for Secure Service Provisioning in IoT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30701)
*Marco Arazzi,Mert Cihangiroglu,Serena Nicolazzo,Antonino Nocera,Vinod P*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个结合深度强化学习（DRL）和联邦学习（FL）的综合框架，用于在物联网环境中选择最合适的智能对象提供服务，并通过行为指纹（BF）模型监控服务提供过程，计算可靠性评分，从而增强服务的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着物联网快速扩展，攻击面随之增大，服务提供过程中的安全至关重要。现有方法缺乏对动态环境中服务提供者可靠性的动态评估与选择机制。

Method: 采用深度强化学习（DRL）让智能体在与复杂动态环境交互中学习适应变化并遵守安全约束；利用联邦学习（FL）构建全局分布式行为指纹（BF）模型，监控IoT设备交互行为，并基于BF计算服务提供者的可靠性评分，将其融入服务选择过程。

Result: 实验评估表明该方案即使在资源受限的IoT设备上也能有效部署，具有良好的鲁棒性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该框架是一种可行且可扩展的安全增强机制，能够有效提升现代IoT生态系统的服务安全性。

Abstract: As the Internet of Things (IoT) continues its rapid expansion, the attack surface grows accordingly, with emerging threats targeting smart objects and their interactions. In this evolving landscape, securing service provisioning is crucial to ensure the proper functioning, security, and reliability of the IoT ecosystem. Service provisioning encompasses key tasks such as device registration, configuration, authentication, authorization, and software deployment, all of which are essential for seamless and secure IoT operations. In this paper, we present a comprehensive framework designed to select the most suitable smart objects to deliver a target service within a given IoT environment while also monitoring the behavior of the entities involved during the service provisioning phase. To achieve this, we employ a Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) approach in which an intelligent agent learns, through interaction with a complex, dynamic environment, how to adapt to changes while adhering to predefined security constraints. For behavioral monitoring, we leverage Federated Learning (FL) to develop a global Behavioral Fingerprinting (BF) model that is fully distributed and can analyze how IoT devices interact within the network. In addition, the BF is used to compute a reliability score for each service provider, reflecting its degree of compliance with the defined security constraints. This score is then incorporated into the service provisioning process, allowing smart objects to select providers not only according to functional suitability but also to their reliability level. Finally, we conduct an extensive experimental evaluation to assess the robustness and scalability of our approach. The results demonstrate that our solution can be effectively deployed even on resource-constrained IoT devices, making it a viable and scalable security-enhancing mechanism for modern IoT ecosystems.

</details>


### [2] [Understanding and Evaluating Claw-like Agent Security Through a Computer-Systems Lens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30755)
*Peizhi Niu,Wenjie Qu,Shangding Gu,Tianneng Shi,Yuankai Li,Ahmad Tawaha,Hend Alzahrani,Vincent Siu,Boyi Li,Chenguang Wang,Jiaheng Zhang,Basel Alomair,Ming Jin,Muhao Chen,Chi Wang,Costas Spanos,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出安全基准SafeClawArena，从计算机系统类比视角系统评估Claw类AI代理的安全漏洞，发现最高攻击成功率达70%，恶意插件100%成功，现有防御严重不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Claw类AI代理（如OpenClaw）是具有持久化权限、系统级责任的常驻进程，其安全故障比传统AI代理更严重，但现有基准只评估模型响应和工具调用，忽略了跨组件故障模式。

Method: 采用计算机系统类比（网关→OS、技能→应用、插件→扩展），开发SafeClawArena基准，包含406个对抗任务，覆盖四个攻击面（技能供应链完整性、持久状态利用、跨边界数据流、间接提示注入），在容器化平台副本中执行，使用金丝雀凭证和自动污点追踪评估。

Result: 三个平台和五个前沿LLM的评估结果显示：最高攻击成功率70%；恶意插件100%成功；SeClaw将GPT-5.4的成功率从70%降至22%（部分通过效用-安全权衡而非主动防御）；Claude-Opus-4.6在所有平台上接近22%的底线。

Conclusion: 当前防御手段不足以应对Claw类AI代理的安全威胁，需要未来加强防护。

Abstract: Claw-like AI agents (e.g., OpenClaw) are always-on processes with persistent access to credentials, files, tools, and external services. They take on system-level responsibilities -- installing packages, maintaining state, scheduling subtasks, and mediating I/O -- making security failures far more severe than in other agents. Yet existing benchmarks focus on model responses and tool calls, leaving cross-component failure modes largely unmeasured. We adopt a computer-system analogy: treating a Claw-like agent as an agentic computer system whose gateway runtime plays an OS-like mediation role, whose Skills resemble user-installed applications, and whose Plugins resemble loadable extensions with runtime privileges. Each component has a classical counterpart whose protection mechanisms -- refined over decades of cybersecurity research -- are absent on the agent side. From this perspective, we develop SafeClawArena, a benchmark of 406 adversarial tasks across four attack surfaces (Skill Supply-Chain Integrity, Persistent State Exploitation, Cross-Boundary Data Flow, and Indirect Prompt Injection), executed in containerized replicas of real agent platforms with canary-marked credentials and evaluated via automated taint tracking across nine output channels. We evaluate three platforms (OpenClaw, NemoClaw, SeClaw) and five frontier LLMs. The highest attack success rate reaches 70%; malicious Plugins succeed in 100% of cases regardless of the LLM. SeClaw cuts GPT-5.4's attack success rate from 70% to 22%, partly through utility-security tradeoffs rather than active defenses, while Claude-Opus-4.6 already sits near a 22% floor on every platform. These results expose the inadequacy of current defenses and suggest directions for future hardening. Code and data: https://github.com/sunblaze-ucb/SafeClawArena.

</details>


### [3] [Security--Fidelity Tradeoffs: The Hidden Cost of Prompt Injection Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30783)
*Mitchell Hermon,Rahul Gupta,Weitong Ruan,Ekraam Sabir,Haohan Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM对抗间接提示注入时存在安全-保真度权衡：防御机制通过压制不可信文本来抵抗注入指令，但这会破坏翻译、文档编辑等需要保留原文的任务。现有攻击成功率指标无法区分模型是忽略注入还是正确处理注入。作者提出SecFid基准，使执行注入、处理为数据和忽略注入三种情况产生可区分输出，从而量化保真度。实验表明，在1168个样本和48种配置下，没有模型或防御能同时实现两个目标：最高保真度模型达到96.5%保真度但仅47.8%安全性，最安全防御达到99.3%安全性但仅71.0%-73.9%保真度。决策理论分析表明，正确行为取决于部署场景中劫持与丢弃的相对成本，而非防御本身属性。安全指标单独衡量仅反映鲁棒性的一半，不报告保真度就隐藏了其代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全防御研究忽略了一个关键问题：对抗间接提示注入的防御措施通过压制不可信文本来工作，但这会破坏需要保真度处理输入的任务（如翻译、文档编辑）。攻击成功率指标无法区分"忽略注入的正确处理"和"忽略所有内容的粗暴压制"，导致防御的真实成本被隐藏。

Method: 构建SecFid基准，设计实验使执行注入、处理为数据和忽略注入三类行为产生可区分的输出，从而同时量化安全性和保真度。在1168个样本和48种配置上评估模型与防御组合。引入决策理论分析，将正确行为建模为部署场景中劫持成本与丢弃成本的函数。

Result: 无模型或防御能同时实现高安全性和高保真度，存在明确的前沿边界。最高保真度模型达96.5%保真度/47.8%安全性，最安全防御达99.3%安全性/71.0%-73.9%保真度。相同安全水平的防御可能在保真度上存在差异（因实现机制不同）。

Conclusion: 安全指标单独衡量只反映鲁棒性的一半，不报告保真度会隐藏防御的真实代价。正确的防御行为不是模型或防御本身的属性，而取决于部署场景中劫持与丢弃的相对成本。因此，未来的安全评估必须同时报告保真度指标。

Abstract: We identify a security-fidelity tradeoff in defending LLMs against indirect prompt injection: defenses resist injected instructions largely by suppressing untrusted text, which corrupts tasks that must preserve it, such as translation and document editing. Attack-success metrics cannot see this, because a model that ignores an injection and one that faithfully processes it as data score identically. We introduce SecFid, a benchmark built so that executing an injection, processing it as data, and ignoring it produce distinguishable outputs. This makes fidelity measurable and exposes a frontier: across 1,168 examples and 48 configurations, no model or defense achieves both objectives. The highest-fidelity model reaches 96.5% fidelity at 47.8% security, while the most secure defenses invert this, at 99.3% security but only 71.0%-73.9% fidelity. Even defenses with identical security differ in how they earn it: some repair hijacks into faithful processing, others simply suppress benign content. A decision-theoretic analysis shows why no fixed choice can be right everywhere: the correct behavior is not a property of the defense but of the deployment, set by its relative cost of a hijack versus a dropped span. Security alone therefore measures only half of robustness, and reporting it without fidelity hides the price at which it was bought.

</details>


### [4] [AI-Generated PowerShell Malware: An Experimental Framework and Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30819)
*Luciano Pianese,Vittorio Orbinato,Pietro Liguori,Roberto Natella*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个评估LLM生成的PowerShell恶意软件的实验框架，包括新型沙箱动态分析方法、人工标注的真实恶意软件数据集，以及对开源LLM的评估。结果显示LLM生成的恶意软件与真实恶意软件在触发的OS恶意事件方面高度相似，Jaccard指数中位数达84.5%，48.4%的实例完全重叠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI已被用于生成恶意代码，对网络安全构成重大威胁，因此亟需研究AI代码生成器的攻击能力，以便安全分析师了解其威胁程度。

Method: 提出了一个实验框架，包含：1）用于动态分析AI生成恶意软件的新型沙箱方法；2）手工标注的自然语言注释的真实PowerShell恶意软件数据集；3）对许可开放的、开源LLM在PowerShell恶意软件生成方面的评估。

Result: LLM生成的恶意软件与真实恶意软件在触发的操作系统恶意事件方面高度相似，Jaccard指数中位数达84.5%，48.4%的实例实现了完全重叠。

Conclusion: 当前开源LLM已具备生成与真实恶意软件高度相似的PowerShell恶意代码的能力，这对网络安全防御提出了新的挑战。

Abstract: Generative AI has emerged as a significant cybersecurity threat, with several recent attack campaigns leveraging LLMs to generate code for malicious purposes via scripting languages such as PowerShell. Consequently, for cybersecurity analysts, it is imperative to investigate the offensive capabilities of AI code generators. In this paper, we propose an experimental framework to assess LLM-generated PowerShell malware, which comprises a novel sandbox approach for dynamic analysis of AI-generated malware. Furthermore, we present a novel, manually curated dataset of real-world PowerShell malware, annotated in natural language to assist the training and evaluation of LLMs. Finally, this study evaluates permissive, open-weight LLMs adapted to PowerShell malware generation. Our results reveal a high degree of similarity between real malware and LLM-generated ones in terms of triggered OS malicious events, with a median Jaccard index of 84.5% and 48.4% of instances achieving complete overlap.

</details>


### [5] [Curvature-Guided Module Localization for Low-Rank Detoxification of Backdoored Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30899)
*Arash Raftari,Mehrdad Mahdavi,Nathan Blackthorn,Andrew Arash Mahyari*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于机制性权重空间修复的后门移除方法，通过激活修补和曲率分析定位触发相关模块，并施加低秩修复，有效抑制后门触发行为同时保持良性性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后门攻击严重威胁大语言模型安全，现有防御方法通常需要从头重新训练，计算成本高昂。本文旨在探索一种无需完整重训练的高效后门移除方案。

Method: 提出机制引导的权重空间修复框架：首先利用激活修补（activation patching）和Fisher/K-FAC曲率分析定位与触发行为传播相关的关键模块，然后仅对最具影响力的模块进行针对性低秩修复（low-rank repair）。

Result: 在Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct上，针对触发器位于提示词开头、中间、结尾三种设置，该方法显著抑制了触发条件下的恶意响应，同时良性行为保持良好。

Conclusion: 大语言模型的后门移除可以被形式化为局部结构修复问题，而不仅仅是一个广泛的行为对齐问题。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks pose a serious threat to large language models (LLMs) by causing otherwise benign systems to produce attacker-specified malicious behavior when a hidden trigger is present. In this work, we study post hoc detoxification of backdoored LLMs in a practical setting where the defender has access to the poisoned model but does not wish to retrain the full network from scratch. We propose a mechanistically guided weight-space repair framework that first localizes modules involved in propagating trigger-induced behavior using activation patching and Fisher/K-FAC curvature analysis, and then applies targeted low-rank repair to only the most influential modules. We evaluate the method on poisoned variants of \texttt{Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct} with triggers inserted at the beginning, middle, and end of otherwise benign prompts. Results show that the proposed approach substantially suppresses trigger-conditioned malicious responses while preserving benign model behavior. These findings suggest that backdoor removal in LLMs can be formulated as a localized structural repair problem rather than only a broad behavioral alignment problem.

</details>


### [6] [Certified Speculative Execution for Untrusted AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31023)
*Chenyu Zhou,Qiliang Jiang,Shuning Wu,Xu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为CGPA（Certificate-Gated Prefix Acceptance）的认证推测执行框架，用于硬约束序列决策系统。该框架通过可信验证器拒绝违规动作、保形校准的价值边界门控最长低成本前缀，将剩余部分交给求解器，从而解耦安全性、遗憾值和速度。实验表明，该方法能使任何不可信提案源（包括98%违规的12B模型）达到零违规，遗憾值比无防护接受低三个数量级，并实现2.96倍的墙钟加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬约束序列决策系统当前缺乏认证方式以利用现代AI的测试时计算：执行学习策略或多步LLM草稿会丧失可信求解器提供的可行性保证，而每一步都调用求解器则牺牲了AI提供的速度。需要在安全保证和计算效率之间建立桥梁。

Method: CGPA方法包含三个核心组件：(1) 可信验证器，精确拒绝违反约束的转移；(2) 保形校准的价值边界，门控最长低成本前缀，确保每段遗憾预算可控；(3) 剩余部分交由求解器处理。从而安全、遗憾和速度三者解耦。

Result: 在多种不可信提案源（包括对抗性起草者和六种异构冻结LLM）上实现零应用违规；保形校准的边界将平均遗憾值降至比无防护接受低三个数量级，达到步进式oracle的采样噪声水平（95%置信区间包含零）；在部署规模的机组组合实例上，冻结的8B LLM实现了2.96倍每回合墙钟加速，遗憾值仅2.1%，优于领域启发式方法（1.79倍）和安全滚动时域基线（1.07倍）。

Conclusion: CGPA框架成功实现了安全保证、遗憾控制和计算速度的解耦，证明了越强大的不可信源越能为认证系统带来更快速度，且保证永不改变。该方法为硬约束序列决策系统中安全利用现代AI能力提供了理论上有保证的实用方案。

Abstract: Hard-constrained sequential decision systems have no certified way to spend the test-time compute of modern AI: executing the multi-step drafts of a learned policy or a frozen LLM forfeits the feasibility guarantee a trusted solver provides, while invoking the solver at every step forfeits the speed the AI offers. Certificate-Gated Prefix Acceptance (CGPA) closes this gap with a certified speculative-execution contract for untrusted AI agents: a trusted verifier rejects constraint-violating transitions exactly, a conformally calibrated value boundary gates the longest low-cost prefix within a per-segment regret budget, and the rest defers to the solver, so safety, regret, and speed decouple by construction. The contract drives every untrusted proposal source - adversarial drafters and six heterogeneous frozen LLMs (including a 12B model that violates constraints in 98% of direct rollouts) - to zero applied violations; a certificate-aware learned boundary, conformally calibrated, drives mean regret three orders of magnitude below unguarded acceptance, to within sampling noise of the stepwise oracle (95% CI spanning zero), and under calendar shift a learned proposal source overtakes it on 15 of 18 held-out days. On a deployment-scale unit-commitment instance it turns a frozen 8B LLM into a 2.96x per-episode wall-clock speedup at 2.1% regret, outpacing the domain heuristic (1.79x) and a safe receding-horizon baseline (1.07x): the more capable the untrusted source, the faster the certified system, at guarantees that never change.

</details>


### [7] [Secure-CHG: A Comprehensive Framework for Robust and Fair Federated Learning via Hybrid Defense and Contribution-Aware Trust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31066)
*Guanming Che,Qiang Wang,Jian Xu,Fucai Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现联邦学习在模型收敛后期存在“晚期失效”脆弱性——梯度范数衰减使恶意与良性更新难以区分，导致传统统计防御失效。为此提出Secure-CHG框架，通过级联统计滤波（早期）和CHG-Shapley机制（晚期），将防御从表面形态检测转向内在语义贡献验证，有效抑制后门攻击，攻击成功率相比Krum和Trimmed Mean基线分别降低2.3倍和2.0倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统计防御主要关注模型收敛早期，忽略了晚期阶段梯度范数衰减导致恶意更新与良性更新形态上不可区分的问题，存在“Late-stage Failure”漏洞。

Method: 提出Secure-CHG混合框架：早期采用级联统计滤波稳定优化；晚期提出CHG-Shapley机制，利用样本硬度（局部训练损失）将更新投影到复合Hardness-Gradient空间，放大对抗性语义痕迹，并推导闭合解实现低复杂度节点估值与信任调制聚合。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、MedMNIST、NEU-SDDB上验证，Secure-CHG有效缓解晚期失效，将高级后门攻击成功率相比Krum和Trimmed Mean基线分别降低2.3倍和2.0倍。

Conclusion: Secure-CHG通过从形态检测转向语义贡献验证的范式转换，有效解决了联邦学习后门防御中的晚期失效问题。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) is highly susceptible to stealthy backdoor attacks, which aim to force a model into predicting an attacker-chosen target class for inputs containing a specific trigger. However, existing statistical defenses primarily focus on the early stages of model convergence. In this paper, we identify a fundamental vulnerability termed ``Late-stage Failure.'' We demonstrate that as the global model converges, decaying gradient norms render malicious and benign updates morphologically indistinguishable. This vanishing statistical variance effectively blinds traditional defenses, enabling adaptive adversaries to remain dormant and subsequently hijack the training process. To overcome these constraints, we propose Secure-CHG, a hybrid framework that pivots the defense paradigm from superficial morphological detection toward intrinsic semantic contribution verification. Secure-CHG employs an adaptive defense pipeline: a cascaded statistical filter stabilizes optimization during the early oscillatory phase, while a novel CHG-Shapley mechanism takes over during late-stage convergence. By leveraging sample hardness (i.e., local training loss) to project updates into a composite Hardness-Gradient space, it effectively amplifies adversarial semantic traces, enabling the isolation of stealthy attackers even as gradient norms vanish. Furthermore, we derive a closed-form solution for CHG-Shapley, facilitating low-complexity, retraining-free node valuation and trust-modulated aggregation. Extensive evaluations on CIFAR-10, MedMNIST, and NEU-SDDB demonstrate that Secure-CHG effectively mitigates Late-stage Failure. Specifically, it significantly suppresses advanced backdoor attacks, reducing their attack success rate by 2.3$\times$ and 2.0$\times$ relative to the mainstream Krum and Trimmed Mean baselines, respectively.

</details>


### [8] [Probe Choice Changes Canary-Memorization Verdicts: Three Post-Hoc Disagreement Case Studies in a Text-Dominant LoRA-Tuned Autoregressive Testbed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31168)
*Zhichao Fan,Zexin Zhuang,Yanhang Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 在Qwen2.5-VL-7B金丝雀测试平台上，本文审计了固定前缀窗口（K=20）的平均负对数似然记忆探针，发现其与全跨度秘密NLL或贪心精确召回存在三种不一致情况（假阴性、假阳性、歧义窗口内下降），并提出了四项报告建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有固定窗口记忆探针（如K=20的mean-NLL）可能无法准确反映模型对秘密片段的实际记忆情况，需要系统审计其与全跨度NLL及行为指标（精确召回）之间的一致性。

Method: 在Qwen2.5-VL-7B上构建金丝雀测试环境，使用K=20固定前缀窗口的mean-NLL探针进行审计，通过后验案例分析法识别并分类了三类不一致情况（C3假阴性、C4假阳性、C5歧义窗口内下降），并对比全跨度秘密NLL和贪心精确召回作为基准。

Result: C3（假阴性，窗口截断）：损害落在K=20窗口外的hex令牌上，探针保持平坦而hit@1下降；C4（假阳性，非秘密漂移）：探针上升但约99%集中在非秘密前导部分，秘密跨度和hit@1不变；C5（歧义窗口内下降）：探针在训练不足的基线上下降，而全跨度hex为正且hit@1=0。

Conclusion: 建议报告四项指标：(i)全跨度秘密NLL，(ii)跨度局部化分解，(iii)k≥4的行为精确召回，(iv)诱饵探针以确认秘密特异性。证据限于受控金丝雀测试和特定测试平台。

Abstract: We audit a fixed prefix-window mean-NLL memorization probe (K=20) on a Qwen2.5-VL-7B canary testbed and report three post-hoc cases where it disagrees with full-span secret NLL or greedy exact-recall. C3 (false negative, window truncation): damage lands on hex tokens outside K=20; the probe stays flat while hit@1 drops. C4 (false positive, non-secret drift): the probe moves, but approximately 99% sits on non-secret preamble; the secret span and hit@1 are unchanged. C5 (ambiguous in-window drop): the probe falls on an undertrained baseline while full-span hex is positive and hit@1=0. Recommendation: report (i) full-span secret NLL, (ii) a span-localised decomposition, (iii) behavioural exact-recall at k>=4, and (iv) decoy probes before asserting secret-specificity. Evidence is on controlled canaries in one backbone; magnitudes are testbed-specific.

</details>


### [9] [Securing the AI Agent: A Unified Framework for Multi-Layer Agent Red Teaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31227)
*Yong Yang,Xing Zheng,Huiyu Wu,Huangsheng Cheng,Xiaorong Shi,Jing Guo,Bo Yang,Yi Zhou,Xiangfan Wu,Zonghao Ying*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AI-Infra-Guard是一个开源AI红队框架，基于"层级-范式匹配"原则，针对AI基础设施、协议/工具、智能体行为和模型四个攻击面层级分别采用不同的检测范式，覆盖75+ AI组件、1400+漏洞规则、MCP服务器审计、多轮黑盒红队测试及26+越狱攻击算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源AI基础设施（模型服务引擎、智能体平台、MCP生态系统等）的快速发展已超越现有安全工具的防御能力，缺乏统一覆盖各攻击面的红队框架。

Method: 提出层级-范式匹配方法：基础设施层采用确定性规则匹配（75+组件、1400+规则），协议/工具层使用LLM驱动的智能体审计，智能体行为层进行多轮黑盒红队测试，模型层配备含26+攻击算子的越狱测试工具集。

Result: 构建了目前唯一覆盖全部攻击面层级的开源框架，包括对智能体技能包供应链安全的审计能力。

Conclusion: 层级-范式匹配可作为智能体安全的实用基础，并作为社区共建的开放基座。

Abstract: The fast growth of open-source AI infrastructure, from model serving engines and agent platforms to the Model Context Protocol (MCP) ecosystem and the language models themselves, has outpaced the security tooling available to defend it. We present AI-Infra-Guard, an open-source framework that organizes AI red teaming around a single observation: the attack surface of an AI agent is stratified across layers (infrastructure, protocol/tool, agent behavior, and model), and no single detection paradigm fits all of them. The framework therefore matches a paradigm to each layer, from deterministic rule matching over 75+ AI components and 1{,}400+ vulnerability rules, through LLM-driven agentic auditing of MCP servers and agent-skill packages and multi-turn black-box agent red teaming, to a jailbreak harness with 26+ attack operators over sixteen datasets. To our knowledge it is the only open-source framework to span all of these, including supply-chain auditing of the agent skills that increasingly extend AI agents. We release AI-Infra-Guard as open source so that \emph{layer-paradigm matching} can serve as a practical foundation for agent security and a shared base for the community to build on.

</details>


### [10] [The Decomposition Is the Fingerprint: Per-Component Identity for Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31272)
*Hongliang Liu,Yuhao Wu,Tung-Ling Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多 bank SimHash 的紧凑局部敏感指纹（120字节），将技能拆分为（prompt, code, tools）三元组分别计算，通过汉明距离实现技能身份的快速比对。实验表明AUC达0.974，比原始嵌入节省77倍空间，能识别族系关系（如改写、重命名、重构等）并定位变更组件，同时故意不跨独立多语言实现识别。该指纹提供结构化的身份轴，与行为验证互补而非替代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体在运行时动态获取和执行技能（含提示指令、可执行代码和工具声明），需要一种稳定的技能身份识别机制。传统密码学哈希对微小改动极其敏感（一字改动即完全改变摘要），无法满足技能相似性比较的需求。

Method: 将技能拆分为提示(prompt)、代码(code)、工具(tools)三个组件，对每个组件分别用多bank SimHash投影生成局部敏感指纹，合并为一个120字节的三元组签名。比较时使用汉明距离，可在常数时间内完成。

Result: 在4,950对比较中AUC达0.974（95% CI [0.956, 0.994]），仅用原始嵌入的1/77比特数，且期望排序保持、有限比特集中。三元组拆分将单一数值转化为关系分类、族系识别和技能物料清单（SkillBOM）。在906个技能的注入基准测试中能识别被篡改的副本并定位变更组件。

Conclusion: 指纹提供的是谱系身份而非行为等价性，作为技能注册中心的结构化身份轴，与行为验证互补。身份识别不等同于安全信任，它是一种补充性信号而非安全裁决。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly acquire and execute skills at runtime: bundles of prompt instructions, executable code, and tool declarations fetched from marketplaces and other agents. Governing them needs a stable notion of skill identity, yet cryptographic hashing is engineered to destroy the very similarity we need, as a one-character edit scrambles the digest. We present a compact, locality-sensitive fingerprint that embeds each component of a skill and projects it to bits with a multi-bank SimHash, giving a fixed 120-byte signature compared in constant time by Hamming distance. Our central claim is that keeping the fingerprint as a per-component triple (prompt, code, tools), rather than a single score, is what makes it useful: the triple recovers skill-family identity through paraphrase, renaming, refactoring, and controlled code translation when another component remains shared, while independent multilingual reimplementation is not recovered; it also localizes which component carries the reuse. We claim lineage, not behavioral equivalence: identity supplies the structural axis of a registry and leaves safety to behavioral verification. The fingerprint reaches an area under the ROC curve (AUC) of 0.974 (95% CI [0.956, 0.994]) over 4,950 pairwise comparisons while using 77x fewer bits than the embedding it approximates, with ranking preserved in expectation and finite-bit concentration; the per-component split turns one number into relationship classification, families, novelty, and a portable "SkillBOM" for a skill registry. On a 906-skill injection benchmark the fingerprint recognizes injected skills as tampered copies of a known base and localizes the change, but recognition is not trust: it remains, by design, an identity signal complementary to behavioral verification rather than a safety verdict.

</details>


### [11] [CSO-LLM: Class Subspace Orthogonalization for Post-Training Backdoor Detection and Trigger Inversion in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31309)
*Zhengxing Li,David J. Miller,Guangmingmei Yang,George Kesidis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM作为分类器时的后门检测与触发器逆向问题，提出了基于类别子空间正交化（CSO）的新型检测框架。CSO通过增强基线检测器的敏感性与特异性，并隐式黑名单化目标类方向上的扰动token，实现了高效的触发器检测与逆向。实验在多个LLM架构和分类领域上验证了其优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门检测与触发器逆向方法主要针对图像AI，而LLM领域缺乏此类方法。LLM输入空间离散且规模巨大（最多150,000^k个k元组），且需要黑名单化目标响应类的典型token以避免误检，但通用领域难以获得完整黑名单。

Method: 提出类别子空间正交化（CSO）范式，作为即插即用的后门检测模块。CSO在LLM中发挥双重作用：(i) 增强基线检测器的敏感性和特异性；(ii) 提供隐式黑名单机制，通过惩罚候选触发器中沿目标类方向引起信号扰动的token。检测器有两个版本：一个在token嵌入空间中进行连续优化，另一个在离散token空间中进行贪婪累加的触发器逆向与检测。

Result: 在多个LLM分类领域和不同LLM架构上，该方法均展现出强大的检测性能和准确的地面真实触发器逆向能力。

Conclusion: CSO范式有效解决了LLM后门检测中离散输入空间大和缺乏通用黑名单的难题，为LLM分类器的安全性提供了可靠保障。

Abstract: While post-training backdoor detection and trigger inversion schemes have been developed for AIs used e.g. for images, there is a paucity of such methods for LLMs. First, the LLM input space is discrete, with up to 150,000^k k-tuples to consider with k the token-length of a putative trigger. Second, one must blacklist tokens typical of the putative target response (class) of an attack, as such tokens may give false detection signals. However, a comprehensive blacklist is not available, in general, for a given domain. We develop a highly effective detection and inversion framework for LLMs treated as classifiers. Central to our approach is class subspace orthogonalization (CSO), a novel plug-and-play paradigm for backdoor detection that serves two fundamental roles when applied to LLMs: i) it enhances both sensitivity and specificity of a baseline detector; ii) it provides a form of implicit blacklisting, as it penalizes against inclusion, in a candidate trigger, of tokens that induce signal perturbations "in the direction of" the putative target class of an attack. One version of our detector performs continuous optimization in token embedding space, while a companion trigger-inversion and detection method performs greedy accretion in discrete token space. Our methods give both strong detection performance and accurate inversion of ground-truth triggers on several LLM classification domains, and for several different LLM architectures.

</details>


### [12] [Automated High-Precision Extraction and Forensic Verification of Data-Bearing Vector Figures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31345)
*Bowen Sun,Chaowei Xiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从矢量图形格式的科学图表中自动提取原始数据的方法，实现了比人工数字化更快速、更精确且可验证的数据恢复，并提供了精度理论、自动提取算法和防抵赖认证机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学文献中的定量数据越来越多以图表形式呈现，读者需要底层数值时通常需要手动重新数字化，这种方法速度慢、精度低且无法证明结果的忠实性。

Method: 基于矢量图形中数据被精确编码而非近似表示的原理，提出：(1)精度理论，界定不同渲染器和导出格式下数据恢复的理论边界；(2)自动提取器，无需人工干预即可一次性解码图表；(3)验证理论，证明恢复过程的单射性、数值碰撞的极低概率以及通过重新渲染证书实现非抵赖性。

Result: 对matplotlib标记可实现float32位精确恢复；端到端可达到3-4位有效数字（受校准限制）；在无真值参与的情况下，解码结果与外部档案高度一致（Planck 2018达10^-9，Keeling CO2记录达5×10^-4）；独立复现了Chinchilla缩放定律置信区间的修正。

Conclusion: 提供了一种可认证的、高精度的科学图表数据自动恢复方法，恢复的数据需经过人工审查，而非作为指控依据。

Abstract: The quantitative record of science and engineering is increasingly carried by figures rather than text or tables, and a reader who needs the underlying numbers must usually re-digitize them by hand: slowly, imprecisely, and with no way to prove the result is faithful. Yet when a figure is stored as vector graphics, its data are not approximated by the picture but encoded in it: the renderer writes each marker and vertex at a printed precision that, for the dominant scientific toolchain, exceeds the data's own. We turn this into three contributions, one per shortcoming of hand digitization. First, a precision theory bounding how accurately data can be recovered for a given renderer and export format: bit-exact float32 for matplotlib markers, and a calibration-limited three to four significant figures end to end. Second, an automatic extractor that decodes a figure in one pass with no human in the loop, in place of the slow point-by-point tracing a digitizer demands. Third, a verification theory: recovery is injective except on a characterized, vanishingly small interval near zero; accidental agreement between unrelated data is astronomically unlikely; and a re-rendering certificate binds the recovered values to the markers, lines, and ticks the figure draws, not its text, making a result non-repudiable. With no ground truth used during recovery, decoded figures match external archives (Planck 2018 to 10^-9; the Keeling CO2 record to 5*10^-4, and one decoded figure independently reproduces a correction to the Chinchilla scaling-law confidence interval. We map the achievable precision across common renderers and their PDF, SVG, and EPS formats. What we deliver is certified data; the scientific significance of any particular dataset lies outside this paper's scope, and recovered values are candidates for human review, never accusations.

</details>


### [13] [Witness Complexity of Short Descriptions: A Cryptographic Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31370)
*Fabio F. G. Buono*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出“见证复杂度”\(\gam(x)\)，衡量字符串在通用图灵机上最短描述的最小运行时间，以形式化密钥/证书的紧凑性与可验证性之间的鸿沟，并证明其与Kolmogorov复杂度的本质区别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密码学实践中协议有严格时间限制，实现需要可预测的资源消耗，系统要求即时验证；紧凑表示若需超多项式时间扩展则无法提供操作保证。现有Shannon熵和Kolmogorov复杂度无法捕捉这一时间约束鸿沟。

Method: 定义\(\gam(x)\)为近最短描述的最小运行时间；证明多项式因子的不变性；基于\(\P\neqNP\)假设的条件分离；利用\(\KC\)不可计算性得到无条件下界；通过类相对变体\(\gP\)给出\(\PeqNP\)的双条件刻画；证明结构化\(\classNP\)族的多项式时间可解性。

Result: \(\gam\)与Kolmogorov复杂度\(\KC\)存在本质分离：低\(\KC\)可伴随高\(\gam\)；获得条件分离（\(\P\neqNP\)）、无条件下界（\(\KC\)不可计算性）、\(\PeqNP\)的等价刻画；发现文法大小与推导代价之间的无条件间隙。

Conclusion: \(\gam\)作为密钥和证书可用性的新度量，弥补了现有复杂度理论在时间有界验证场景下的空白，为密码学实践提供了可操作的形式化框架。

Abstract: In cryptographic practice, where protocols impose strict time bounds, implementations demand predictable resource usage, and real-world systems require immediate verification for security and usability, a short key or certificate is useful only if it can be expanded or verified within a bounded time; otherwise a compact representation that requires superpolynomial work to expand offers no operational guarantee within a bounded-time protocol. This paper formalises that gap by introducing \emph{witness complexity} \(\gam(x)\), the minimum running time over near-shortest descriptions of a string on a universal Turing machine. \(\gam\) differs from Shannon entropy and Kolmogorov complexity \(\KC\): low \(\KC\) can coexist with high \(\gam\). We prove invariance up to polynomial factors; a conditional separation (assuming \(\PneqNP\)). An unconditional lower bound from incomputability of \(\KC\); a biconditional characterisation of \(\PeqNP\) via the class-relative variant \(\gP\); and polynomial-time tractability for structured \(\classNP\) families. Part II develops companion measures and shows an unconditional gap between grammar size and derivation cost, positioning \(\gam\) as a metric for the usability of keys and certificates.

</details>


### [14] [EnclaveX: End-to-End Confidential AI with CPU/GPU TEEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31408)
*Robert Schambach,Quoc Do Le,Sergei Arnautov,Christof Fetzer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了结合CPU和GPU可信执行环境的端到端工作流，以解决LLM在云部署中的安全和隐私问题，并通过Intel TDX与NVIDIA H200 GPU的集成测试评估了性能开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的广泛部署依赖Azure、Google Cloud、AWS等集中式基础设施，用户需共享敏感数据和代码，存在严重的安全和隐私风险。现有CPU TEE（如Intel SGX/TDX、AMD SEV-SNP）和新兴的GPU TEE（如NVIDIA H100/H200）虽能缓解这些风险，但缺乏对CPU与GPU TEE端到端工作流的全面评估，尤其在性能开销、远程证明和AI/LLM应用安全保证方面研究不足。

Method: 提出端到端工作流，结合CPU TEE（Intel TDX和AMD SEV-SNP）与GPU TEE（NVIDIA H200），在VM级别和应用级别分别设计机密性和完整性保障机制，并揭示Kubernetes管理员可访问机密VM内容等漏洞。

Result: 通过行业基准测试，评估了集成Intel TDX与NVIDIA H200 GPU配置下的系统性能开销。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了CPU与GPU TEE联合部署于AI/LLM工作负载的综合评估空白，指出了现有安全机制（如Kubernetes权限管理）的漏洞，为构建更安全的云AI基础设施提供了参考。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have rapidly proliferated, driving widespread adoption of AI applications. Most deployments rely on centralized infrastructures such as Microsoft Azure, Google Cloud, or AWS, requiring users to share sensitive data and training or fine-tuning code. This dependence raises significant security and privacy concerns, as cloud providers must be trusted to ensure confidentiality and integrity.
  Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) e.g., Intel SGX/TDX, AMD SEV-SNP, and ARM CCA have been introduced to mitigate these risks. More recently, NVIDIA has developed GPU TEEs (e.g., H100/H200), yet comprehensive evaluations of end-to-end workflows that integrate CPU and GPU TEEs remain limited. Critical aspects, including performance overhead, remote attestation, and security guarantees for AI/LLM applications, have not been sufficiently studied.
  This paper addresses this gap by presenting an end-to-end workflow that combines CPU and GPU TEEs. We propose mechanisms to ensure confidentiality and integrity at both the VM level (via Intel TDX and AMD SEV-SNP) and the application level, highlighting vulnerabilities such as Kubernetes administrators' ability to access confidential VM contents. Finally, we evaluate the performance overhead of our system using industry benchmarks, focusing on configurations that integrate Intel TDX with NVIDIA H200 GPUs.

</details>


### [15] [A forgery attack on the Block.co blockchain-based digital credential certification system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31462)
*Giacomo Zonneveld,Giulia Rafaiani,Marco Baldi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现基于区块链的数字证书系统Block.co存在安全漏洞，可被用于伪造有效证书，且该问题源于此类系统的固有设计缺陷，可能影响其他类似系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链技术被认为适合用于数字文档（如学历证书）的认证，但缺乏中心化权威机构时，难以保证学术机构的真实身份与证书发行者数字身份之间的可信连接，这一安全缺口值得研究。

Method: 作者对Block.co系统进行安全分析，识别其认证机制中的漏洞，并验证了可伪造被系统认可为有效的证书。

Result: 发现Block.co存在漏洞，可生成系统认可的有效伪造证书，且该漏洞是区块链认证方法的固有限制，可能影响采用相同方法的其他系统。

Conclusion: 基于区块链的去中心化数字证书认证系统在没有可信中心权威的情况下，难以保证真实身份与数字身份的安全绑定，这一固有限制导致存在可被利用的伪造漏洞。

Abstract: Certification of digital documents, such as academic credentials, seems a particularly suitable application for the use of blockchain and distributed ledger technologies. Indeed, these technologies enable decentralized certification systems that rely on the immutability and persistence of their distributed ledgers. However, in the absence of a central trusted authority, it is not easy to guarantee the authenticity of the connection between the real identity of an academic institution and the digital identity of the certificate issuer. In this paper, we demonstrate that one of such systems, known as Block.co, has a vulnerability that allows the production of forged certificates that are recognized as valid by the system. Since this is an inherent limitation of the approach used for blockchain-based certification, our attack is likely to be extendable to other systems adopting the same approach.

</details>


### [16] [CVE-TTP KG: Knowledge Graph Linking Software Vulnerabilities to Attack Behaviors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31557)
*Basant Agarwal,Dincy R. Arikkat,Swati Yadav,Serena Nicolazzo,Antonino Nocera,Vinod P*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文构建了CVE-TTP知识图谱，利用Transformer模型（CySecBERT）将CVE漏洞与MITRE ATT&CK框架中的战术和技术进行关联，实现了高精度的行为识别（技术宏F1=87.71%，战术宏F1=96.16%）和关系抽取（宏F1=0.99），并集成到Neo4j知识图谱中实现可视化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有漏洞数据库（如CVE、NVD）缺乏与攻击者行为模式（如MITRE ATT&CK中的战术和技术）的关联，限制了安全团队对漏洞威胁的有效解读和响应。

Method: 构建了包含24,820个实体和43,608个关系的标注数据集；采用CySecBERT等Transformer模型进行行为分类（技术/战术识别）；通过pipeline方法进行实体和关系抽取（实体F1=0.86，关系F1=0.99），并与span-based联合模型（F1=0.78）对比；结果集成到Neo4j知识图谱中。

Result: CySecBERT在技术识别上宏F1=87.71%，战术识别上宏F1=96.16%；pipeline实体抽取F1=0.86，关系抽取F1=0.99；span-based联合模型实体关系抽取F1=0.78。

Conclusion: 通过CVE-TTP知识图谱成功桥接了漏洞信息与攻击行为模式之间的鸿沟，为威胁情报分析和漏洞优先级排序提供了结构化、可查询的知识表示工具。

Abstract: In the evolving threat landscape, adversaries exploit software vulnerabilities to launch sophisticated attacks, challenging traditional defenses. Although databases like CVE and NVD provide detailed technical information, they often lack links to attacker behaviors such as tactics and techniques, limiting effective threat interpretation and response. This work bridges this gap by connecting vulnerabilities with behavioral patterns from the MITRE ATT&CK framework. We construct a CVE-TTP Knowledge Graph that links CVEs to tactics and techniques using classification and relation extraction. Transformer-based models are developed for behavior identification, with CySecBERT achieving macro F1-scores of 87.71% (techniques) and 96.16% (tactics). Also, we created an annotated dataset with 24,820 entities and 43,608 relations for entity and relation extraction. The pipeline-based approach achieves macro F1-scores of 0.86 (entity extraction) and 0.99 (relation extraction), while a span-based joint model achieves 0.78. These outputs are integrated into a Neo4j-based Cyber Threat Knowledge Graph, enabling structured visualization of vulnerabilities.

</details>

*Rana Alharbi,Chuadhry Mujeeb Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用Gotham2025数据集（包含78个仿真IoT设备）的入侵检测系统，对比五种机器学习算法后发现随机森林分类器在攻击分类中取得了0.99的F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT设备资源受限导致安全与隐私问题突出，亟需有效的入侵检测系统来保护IoT网络免受攻击。

Method: 使用Gotham测试平台生成的Gotham2025数据集（78种仿真IoT设备，支持MQTT、CoAP、RTSP协议），比较随机森林、XGBoost、逻辑回归、朴素贝叶斯和深度神经网络五种机器学习算法的入侵检测性能。

Result: 随机森林分类器表现最佳，攻击分类F1-score达到0.99。

Conclusion: 随机森林在IoT入侵检测任务中具有优越性，Gotham2025数据集可作为IoT安全研究的有效基准。

Abstract: The Internet of Things (IoT) is rapidly growing and expanding into various sectors, such as healthcare, transportation, smart homes, and more. Despite the benefits of using IoT devices, they present several challenges. Given the significant role these devices play in our lives, it is crucial to address issues related to their security and privacy. These devices are limited in resources, which complicates their security and the protection of the data that they manage. The paper aims to examine intrusion detection systems using the Gotham2025 dataset, generated through the Gotham testbed, which consists of 78 emulated IoT devices utilising various protocols, including MQTT, CoAP, and RTSP, to assist in safeguarding IoT networks from attacks. We conduct a comparative analysis between five machine learning algorithms, including Random Forest, XGBoost, Logistic Regression, Naive Bayes, and Deep Neural Network. We demonstrate that the Random Forest Classifier was the top-performing model, achieving an F1-score of 0.99 in classifying attacks.

</details>


### [18] [Digital signature schemes based on code equivalence and syndrome decoding from restricted errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31601)
*Sarah Arpin,Jason T. LeGrow,Hiram H. López,Gretchen L. Matthews*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于纠错码的两种后量子数字签名方案（CROSS和LESS），它们基于受限错误综合征解码和码等价问题，通过Fiat-Shamir变换将Sigma协议转化为数字签名，旨在抵御量子计算攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后量子密码标准化背景下，需要开发能够抵御量子计算机攻击的数字签名方案，而基于纠错码的方案是重要候选方向。

Method: 综述了CROSS（基于受限错误综合征解码）和LESS（基于码等价问题）两种方案，描述了Sigma协议及其通过Fiat-Shamir变换转化为数字签名的过程。

Result: 提供了两种NIST第二轮候选方案（CROSS和LESS）的全面概述，阐明了其底层困难问题及签名构造范式。

Conclusion: 基于纠错码的数字签名方案通过Fiat-Shamir变换可实现后量子安全性，CROSS和LESS是其中具有代表性的候选方案。

Abstract: Digital signature schemes are an important cryptographic tool to ensure data authenticity and integrity in many applications that must be resilient to attacks, including those facilitated by quantum computers. We consider the two digital signature schemes based on error-correcting codes that are second-round candidates in NIST's call for Additional Signature Schemes, which is part of the Post-Quantum Cryptography Standardization Process. Specifically, we provide an overview of the Codes and Restricted Objects Signature Scheme (CROSS) and the Linear Equivalence Signature Scheme (LESS). We describe their underlying problems of syndrome decoding from restricted errors and code equivalence. We review sigma protocols and how they can be transformed into digital signature schemes via the Fiat-Shamir transform. Finally, we explain how this procedure yields code-based digital signatures believed to be post-quantum secure.

</details>


### [19] [Hybrid Topological Data Analysis and LSTM Networks for Enhanced Network Intrusion Detection Using CIC-IDS2017 Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31619)
*Amar Jeet,Bhaskar Ranjan Karn,Dinesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合拓扑数据分析（TDA）与长短期记忆网络（LSTM）的混合方法用于网络入侵检测，在CIC-IDS2017数据集上取得了AUC和F1-score均为1.000的优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络入侵检测系统需要更先进的技术来检测网络流量中的恶意活动，传统方法在捕获复杂网络流量特征方面存在局限。

Method: 采用混合架构，将TDA的持续同调（Betti曲线、持久性图）与LSTM网络相结合，利用TDA捕捉网络流模式的拓扑特征，LSTM建模时间序列依赖关系。

Result: 在CIC-IDS2017数据集上，混合TDA+LSTM模型AUC为1.000，F1-score为1.000；5折交叉验证平均AUC为1.000±0.000，平均F1为0.999±0.001。消融实验表明拓扑特征F1=0.990，时序特征F1=1.000。对比实验优于TDA+随机森林（F1=0.994）和隔离森林（F1=0.835）。

Conclusion: TDA与LSTM的混合方法能有效融合拓扑特征和时序特征，显著提升网络入侵检测性能，在多个攻击类别上均优于传统基线方法。

Abstract: Network intrusion detection systems (NIDS) are crucial in cybersecurity infrastructure, needing advanced techniques to detect hostile activity in network traffic. This research introduces a hybrid approach that combines Topological Data Analysis (TDA) with Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks to improve anomaly detection in network security. Our multi-layered design combines TDA's persistent homology with LSTM networks to capture topological characteristics of network traffic patterns and simulate temporal sequences. We assessed our methodology using the CIC-IDS2017 dataset, which includes over 2.8 million labelled flows, 77 network variables, and 14 attack categories that reflect modern threat landscapes such as DDoS, brute force, web attacks, penetration, and botnet activities. Integrating Betti curves and persistence diagrams with deep learning architectures enhances feature extraction performance. Our hybrid TDA+LSTM model has an AUC of 1.000 and F1-score of 1.000, with 5-fold cross-validation producing a mean AUC of 1.000 $\pm$ 0.000 and mean F1 of 0.999 $\pm$ 0.001. An ablation research demonstrates the complimentary contributions of topological (F1=0.990) and temporal characteristics (F1=1.000). Comparative research shows that the suggested strategy beats TDA+Random Forest (F1=0.994) and Isolation Forest (F1=0.835) baselines in several attack categories.

</details>


### [20] [A Lifecycle and Application-Stack Survey of Large Language Model Vulnerabilities: Attacks, Risks, Defenses, and Open Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31639)
*Seyed Bagher Hashemi Natanzi,Bo Tang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过生命周期和应用堆栈视角，系统化梳理了大语言模型系统中的漏洞文献，将攻击组织为八个阶段，并分析了各阶段的攻击者能力、安全目标、代表性攻击、实际风险、评估实践和防御措施。最后提出了安全LLM系统的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型已从单纯的文本生成器演变为嵌入检索管道、企业助手、编码环境、机器人系统、安全运维工作流和自主智能体的复杂系统，能够读取私有数据、调用工具、写入文件、执行代码并在组织边界间行动。这种转变改变了安全问题：风险不仅来自模型权重，而是来自数据、提示词、模型输出、工具、记忆和用户权限交互的完整生命周期和应用堆栈。现有研究缺乏从生命周期视角系统化理解这些漏洞的工作。

Method: 采用生命周期和应用堆栈视角，将攻击组织为八个阶段：（1）数据收集、（2）预训练、（3）后训练对齐、（4）模型打包与供应链、（5）检索与记忆、（6）提示与推理、（7）工具/智能体执行、（8）部署与维护。对每个阶段分析攻击者能力、受影响的安全目标、代表性攻击、实际风险、评估实践和防御措施。同时将LLM特定漏洞映射到机密性、完整性、可用性、安全性、隐私、公平性、问责制和自主权控制等安全目标。

Result: 不同于仅列出孤立攻击名称的分类法，本文的系统化方法强调了信任边界在何处失效、不受信任的数据如何变成可执行指令、委托授权如何放大模型错误、以及为什么点状防御很少能组合生效。揭示了LLM系统的安全脆弱性根源在于信任边界的跨越和权限的委托代理问题。

Conclusion: 提出了安全LLM系统的研究议程，包括：组合安全性、可溯源感知的检索、工具调用遏制、长周期智能体评估、隐私保护适配、现实红队测试和部署级事件响应。

Abstract: Large language models are no longer only text generators. They are increasingly embedded in retrieval pipelines, enterprise assistants, coding environments, robotic systems, security-operation workflows, and autonomous agents that can read private data, call tools, write files, execute code, and act across organizational boundaries. This shift changes the security problem: risks do not arise from the model weights alone, but from the full lifecycle and application stack through which data, prompts, model outputs, tools, memories, and user authority interact. This paper systematizes the literature on vulnerabilities in large language model systems through a lifecycle and application-stack lens. We organize attacks across eight stages: data collection, pretraining, post-training alignment, model packaging and supply chain, retrieval and memory, prompting and inference, tool/agent execution, and deployment/maintenance. For each stage, we analyze attacker capabilities, affected security objectives, representative attacks, practical risks, evaluation practices, and defenses. We further map LLM-specific vulnerabilities to confidentiality, integrity, availability, safety, privacy, fairness, accountability, and agency-control objectives. Unlike taxonomies that list isolated attack names, the proposed systematization emphasizes where trust boundaries fail, how untrusted data becomes executable instruction, how delegated authority amplifies model errors, and why point defenses rarely compose. We close with a research agenda for secure LLM systems, including compositional security, provenance-aware retrieval, tool-call containment, long-horizon agent evaluation, privacy-preserving adaptation, realistic red teaming, and deployment-grade incident response.

</details>


### [21] [Exploring Side-Channel Protections in Hardware Implementations of PQC ML-KEM Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31681)
*Davis Ranney,Yashaswini I Makaram,A. Adam Ding,Yunsi Fei*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了ML-KEM标准中Fujisaki-Okamoto验证步骤在FPGA和微控制器实现上的侧信道漏洞，发现即使高阶掩码设计在FPGA上仍存在一阶泄漏，可导致完整密钥恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着ML-KEM被采纳为后量子密码标准，其抗物理侧信道攻击的能力变得至关重要，特别是其中的FO验证步骤对功耗和电磁分析尤为脆弱。

Method: 对比评估了三种验证实现（未保护、基于哈希的一阶掩码、高阶掩码）在微控制器和FPGA平台上的侧信道安全性，重点关注FPGA并行化处理带来的泄漏效应。

Result: FPGA实现尽管速度快、并行度高，但表现出更强的侧信道泄漏；高阶掩码设计因硬件级效应和数据相关处理仍泄漏底层信息；FPGA上的并行处理引入了足够的一阶泄漏以实现完整密钥恢复。

Conclusion: 在性能受限和并行化硬件环境中，确保后量子密码算法的安全性仍然面临持续挑战，现有掩码方案在FPGA上不足以完全防止侧信道攻击。

Abstract: As ML-KEM is adopted as a post-quantum cryptographic standard, resilience against physical side-channel attacks has become essential. Among the constituent steps, the decapsulation Fujisaki-Okamoto (FO) verification is particularly vulnerable to side-channel power and electromagnetic (EM) analysis. In this work, we focus on common FPGA-based implementations and examine their side-channel vulnerabilities, and compare them with those of microcontroller implementations. Three verification implementations, unprotected, hash-based (first-order), and higher-order masked, are evaluated for side-channel security on both a microcontroller and an FPGA. While FPGAs offer higher speed and parallelism, they often exhibit stronger side-channel leakage, especially in high bandwidth configurations. The higher-order masked designs still leak information about the underlying data due to hardware-level effects and data-dependent processing. Our experiments show that their parallelized processing on FPGAs introduces sufficient first-order leakage for full secret-key recovery. These results underscore the persistent challenge of securing PQC algorithms in performance-constrained and parallelized hardware environments.

</details>


### [22] [Robocalls: A Worldwide or US-only Problem? Analyzing Spam and Fraud in International Phone Calls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31790)
*Kemal Altwlkany,Andro Merćep,Tomislav Đuričić,Ante Kapetanović,Emanuel Lacic*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对国际骚扰电话问题，构建了首个公开的多模态国际骚扰电话数据集（870万条通话记录、839份转录文本、677段录音，覆盖65个国家），通过9个月的数据收集与分析，揭示了美国与其他国家在骚扰电话模式、协同攻击、回调号码、语言差异等方面的异同，发现尽管骚扰电话是全球性问题，但美国面临的威胁程度显著高于其他国家。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 骚扰电话（robocalls）造成巨大经济损失（仅2025年美国报告损失11亿美元），且由于跨司法管辖、多语言、数据隐私、缺乏公开数据集等法律与实践限制，现有研究难以有效应对这一全球性威胁。

Method: 通过9个月跨洲际数据收集，构建包含870万条通话详单记录、839份骚扰电话转录文本（来自28个已识别诈骗活动集群）和677段录音的多模态数据集；从呼叫模式、协同攻击、共同诈骗活动、回调号码、语言差异等多维度对65个国家的骚扰电话进行国际比较分析。

Result: 发现骚扰电话是国际性问题，但美国面临的威胁严重程度显著高于其他国家；识别出28个骚扰电话诈骗活动集群；揭示了跨国家协同攻击模式、不同方言区域的语音差异等新发现。

Conclusion: 基于分析结果提出了减少骚扰电话有效性的对策建议，并为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Unsolicited automated phone calls (robocalls) are a serious threat: in the US alone, these calls resulted in reported losses of 1.1$ billion during 2025. Phishing and spoofing consistently rank among the most reported crimes within the FBI's Internet Crime Complaint Center, with phone call scams having the highest reported median loss. Combating robocalls is difficult due to many legal and practical constraints: robocalls often encompass multiple legal jurisdictions of different countries/states, the large volume of robocalls, their multilingual nature, the lack of publicly available data, privacy concerns with obtaining data, etc. We present a study of international robocalls, aggregating robocall reports from countries across all inhabited continents and contribute by providing new findings on international robocalls from 65 different countries. We also present the first publicly available multimodal and international robocall dataset: 8.7 million call detail records, 839 robocall transcripts from 28 identified robocall campaign clusters, and 677 robocall recordings. We describe our methodology for collecting robocall data over a 9-month period and provide a detailed analysis comparing robocalls in the US with those in other countries. Our analysis covers several aspects, including uncovering calling patterns, identifying co-targeting attacks, discovering common robocall campaigns, extracting callback numbers, analyzing linguistic differences among robocalls in the same language but different regions, and other insights. Our results indicate that although robocalls are an international problem, the severity of the threat is significantly higher in the US than in other countries. We provide steps for future research and suggest remedies to reduce the effectiveness of robocalls based on our analysis.

</details>

### [23] [A Multi-Dimensional, Per-Pass Empirical Study of the LLVM Optimization Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31238)
*Federico Bruzzone,Walter Cazzola*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对LLVM -O3优化流水线进行了系统性实证研究，通过84750次测量覆盖113个前缀阶段，发现该流水线在PolyBench/C核上呈现非单调性（6.6-9.7%的转换导致性能回退）、后端负载集中（中位数内核需84.8%流水线才能获得80%加速）、最终-O3配置在29/30内核上被Pareto支配，并揭示了IR指令计数不可靠、运行时优化等价于节能（30-60%）、相位干扰的理想化加性上界为46.35%等关键发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化单个优化pass的边际影响对于相位排序、pass选择、优化设计及pass/硬件交互分析至关重要，但在LLVM中，优化pass间的交互、测量噪声和大规模流水线使得这种量化非常困难。

Method: 将LLVM -O3优化流水线分解为113个累积前缀阶段，在30个PolyBench/C内核上，通过84750次测量（涵盖执行时间、编译时间、二进制大小、硬件计数器和RAPL能量），并在严格噪声抑制条件下进行系统性实证研究。

Result: 流水线非单调（6.6-9.7%转换导致回退），后端负载集中（中位数非回退内核需84.8%流水线达80%加速）；少数Pareto主导pass驱动大部分收益，最终-O3在29/30内核上被Pareto支配；IR指令数不可靠预测运行时；运行时优化实质是节能（30-60%）；相位干扰理想化加性上界为46.35%。

Conclusion: 这些发现为更明智的pass剪枝、成本模型校准和自动调优提供了依据。

Abstract: Quantifying the marginal impact of individual optimization passes underpins phase ordering, pass selection, optimization design, and analysis of pass/hardware interactions. In LLVM -- the standard backend for C/C++, Rust, and ML stacks via MLIR -- interactions among optimization passes, measurement noise, and pipeline scale make this difficult. We present a systematic, empirical study of the LLVM -O3 optimization pipeline. We decompose the pipeline into cumulative per-pass prefixes. We then measure execution time, compile time, binary size, hardware counters, and RAPL energy across 84,750 measurements covering 113 cumulative prefixes of the -O3 pipeline evaluated on 30 PolyBench/C kernels under rigorous noise mitigation. On these compute-bound affine kernels, the pipeline is non-monotone (6.6-9.7% of transitions regress) and strongly back-loaded (the median non-regressing kernel needs 84.8% of the pipeline for 80% of its speedup). Most gains are driven by a small Pareto-dominant core of passes, while the final -O3 configuration is Pareto-dominated on (size, speedup) for 29 of 30 kernels. We further show that IR instruction count is an unreliable predictor of runtime, that runtime-targeted passes are de facto energy-targeted (30-60% savings), and that the search-free idealized-additive upper bound on losses due to phase interference is 46.35%. These findings enable more informed pass pruning, cost-model calibration, and autotuning.

</details>


### [24] [Citation Discipline in Spec-Driven Development: A Cross-Model Empirical Study of Output Determinism and Automated Hallucination Detection in LLM-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30689)
*Subham Panda*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过两项对照实证研究，比较了三种基于规范的开发（SDD）框架（traceSDD、Spec Kit、OpenSpec）在LLM代码生成中的表现，发现引用标注机制会以确定性为代价换取可验证性，且这种权衡在不同模型架构中一致存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SDD框架在需求与生成代码之间的可追溯性机制存在根本差异，而不同追溯策略对代码生成的确定性和幻觉检测能力的影响尚缺乏系统性的实证研究。

Method: 在Claude Sonnet 4.6（N=20，4个条件，240个实现）和GLM-5-turbo（N=50，4个条件，600个实现）两个前沿LLM上进行了两项受控实证研究，测量输出确定性和自动化幻觉检测率（TDR）两个主要指标。

Result: 未引用条件比引用条件产生显著更高的确定性（Claude: d=-0.76, p=0.003; GLM: d=-0.72, p<0.001）；仅引用条件支持自动化幻觉检测（Claude: 86.4%, GLM: 88.0%, 替代方案均为0%, FPR=0%）；traceSDD在确定性上显著优于Spec Kit，但与OpenSpec无显著差异。

Conclusion: 引用标注在代码生成中会以确定性为代价换取可验证性，且这种权衡在不同模型架构间具有泛化性。

Abstract: Spec-Driven Development (SDD) frameworks guide Large Language Model (LLM)-powered code generation through formal specifications, yet they differ fundamentally in how they enforce traceability between requirements and generated code. This paper presents two controlled empirical studies comparing three SDD frameworks: $traceSDD$, which enforces mandatory per-line requirement citations using hierarchical REQ-XXX.Y.Z identifiers; $Spec Kit$, which uses artifact-level traceability through user stories and acceptance criteria; and $OpenSpec$, which relies on post-hoc external trace maps. We measure two primary outcomes across two frontier LLMs -- Claude Sonnet 4.6 (N=20, 4 conditions, 240 implementations) and GLM-5-turbo (N=50, 4 conditions, 600 implementations): $output$ $determinism$ (lexical similarity across independent LLM sessions) and $automated$ $hallucination$ $detection$ $rate$ (TDR). Our pre-registered analysis reveals a consistent, cross-model replicated trade-off: the uncited condition produces significantly higher determinism than the cited condition (Claude: $d=-0.76$, $p=0.003$; GLM: $d=-0.72$, $p<0.001$), while only the cited condition enables automated hallucination detection (TDR: Claude 86.4%, GLM 88.0%, vs 0% for all alternatives, FPR=0% across both studies). traceSDD (cited) significantly outperforms $Spec Kit$ on determinism (Claude: $d=0.47$, $p=0.049$; GLM: $d=0.42$, $p=0.003$) but not OpenSpec (Claude: $d=0.18$, $p=0.44$; GLM: $d=0.14$, $p=0.32$). These findings establish that citation annotations trade determinism for verifiability, and that this trade-off generalizes across model architectures.

</details>


### [25] [Towards Knowledge Alignment in Code LLMs: Contrastive Unlearning for Evolving APIs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30810)
*Huy Q. Tran,Dang H. Vu,Tuyen N. Dinh,Anh H. D. Nguyen,Anh N. H. Vu,Anh M. T. Bui,Phuong T. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CURE方法，通过对比性反学习同时抑制过时API并鼓励正确替代API，使LLM能更可靠地适应软件库演化，在基准测试中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在代码生成中常因知识截止和软件库快速演化而生成已弃用的API用法，导致代码不可靠。现有微调方法缺乏选择性，而现有模型级方法（如机器反学习、模型编辑）虽能抑制过时API，但未能显式引导模型使用正确的替代API，导致生成不完整或不匹配。

Method: 提出CURE（对比性反学习方法），核心思想是将反学习从单纯抑制过时知识转变为同时抑制过时API并鼓励正确的替代API。通过对比学习框架，联合优化模型以降低对弃用API的预测概率，同时提高对有效替代API的预测概率。

Result: 在已弃用API基准数据集上的实验表明，CURE不仅能减少弃用API的使用，还能提高正确API替代率，同时保持通用代码生成性能，在两个SOTA基线方法上实现了显著超越。

Conclusion: 研究强调了在使LLM适应演化中的软件生态系统时，将抑制（suppression）与替代（replacement）相结合的重要性，为未来模型知识更新提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently achieved strong performance in code generation. However, due to knowledge cut-off and the rapid evolution of software libraries, they often generate deprecated API usages that lead to unreliable and incompatible code. Existing fine-tuning methods lack selectivity when only a small portion of model knowledge requires modification. Recent model-level approaches, such as machine unlearning and model editing, offer a promising direction for modifying parametric knowledge. However, their use for deprecated API mitigation remains largely unexplored. Moreover, existing methods primarily suppress outdated APIs, but do not explicitly steer models toward correct replacements, often leading to mismatched or incomplete generations. To address this limitation, we developed CURE, a contrastive unlearning approach that shifts unlearning from purely suppressing outdated knowledge to explicitly promoting correct API replacements. Concretely, CURE jointly discourages deprecated APIs while encouraging their valid alternatives, enabling more reliable adaptation to evolving software libraries. The experiments on recent deprecated API benchmark dataset show that CURE not only reduces deprecated API usage but also improves correct API replacement, while preserving general code generation performance. CURE substantially outperforms two SOTA baselines with respect to different quality metrics. These findings highlight the importance of combining suppression with replacement when adapting LLMs to evolving software ecosystems.

</details>


### [26] [Loc2Repair: A Framework for Evaluating the Impact of File-Level Issue Localization in Repo-Level LLM Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30963)
*Mohammad Nour Al Awad,Sergey Ivanov*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Loc2Repair是一个模块化评估框架，用于解耦和分析仓库级自动修复中的文件定位与补丁合成环节。实验表明，显式的文件级定位能持续提升修复成功率（从44.7%提升至48.9%-52.4%）并降低平均耗时，但金标准定位下的失败案例也揭示了定位之外仍存在改进空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库级自动修复常被当作单一端到端能力报告，这掩盖了文件定位不准确、补丁合成错误、迭代调试失败等不同故障模式。作者提出Loc2Repair框架，旨在隔离文件级问题定位作为上游变量，进行受控分析。

Method: Loc2Repair将定位与修复解耦，在共享运行时、工件模式和评估框架下，允许研究者组合不同定位模型和修复骨干。在SWE-bench Verified上使用三种修复骨干，比较无显式定位的基线修复、两种预测定位引导的修复和金标准修改文件集引导的修复。

Result: 显式定位在所有骨干上持续提升解决率：基线44.7%，预测定位引导为48.9%和49.1%，金标准定位为52.4%。平均耗时方面，两种预测定位分别减少100.94秒和52.25秒，金标准减少154.45秒，但token效应在不同模型间存在异质性。

Conclusion: 文件级定位是持续有效的修复杠杆，能提升修复效果并降低平均延迟；但金标准引导下的失败案例表明定位之外仍有改进空间。

Abstract: Repository-grounded automated repair is often reported as a single end-to-end capability, which hides distinct failure modes such as poor file targeting, incorrect patch synthesis, and failed iterative debugging. We present Loc2Repair, a modular evaluation framework for controlled analysis of repository-grounded repair pipelines, and use it to isolate file-level issue localization as an upstream variable. Loc2Repair decouples localization and repair under a shared runtime, artifact schema, and evaluation harness, allowing researchers to combine different localization models and repair backbones under matched conditions. Using three repair backbones on SWE-bench Verified, we compare baseline repair without explicit localization, repair guided by predicted localization from two localizers, and repair guided by gold modified-file sets. Explicit localization consistently improves resolved rate across all backbones: pooled performance increases from 44.7% for baseline repair to 48.9% and 49.1% with predicted localization, and to 52.4% with gold localization. Localization also reduces mean elapsed time overall: in pooled paired analysis, mean elapsed time decreases by 100.94 s and 52.25 s for the two predicted-localization settings, and by 154.45 s with gold guidance, although token effects remain heterogeneous across models. Overall, Loc2Repair shows file-level localization is a consistent repair lever, improving effectiveness and mean latency in pooled analysis, while gold-guided failures expose headroom beyond localization.

</details>


### [27] [Partially ordering software licenses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31032)
*Hamidah Oderinwale,David Atkinson,Rachel Hong,Art Abal,Ben Laufer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文利用大语言模型对开源许可证进行规模化比较，通过两两对比构建基于宽松度的偏序关系，并基于现有分类法分析许可证选择背后的共享条款，揭示更严格许可证的可解释属性及其对开源生态的法律影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前许可证的作者归属和选择方式缺乏结构化，导致难以大规模比较许可证之间的宽松程度和条款可比性。随着Hugging Face、GitHub等平台上许可证数量不断增加，缺乏系统性的方法来研究它们之间的关系。

Method: 利用大语言模型（LLMs）提出两种规模化比较许可证的方法：1）通过两两比较构建基于宽松度的偏序关系；2）基于现有软件许可证分类法，将许可证选择理解为共享条款的组合。

Result: 恢复出了与更严格许可证对应的可解释属性，揭示了许可证限制性与开源生态的法律影响之间的关联。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够系统化地追踪许可证的严格程度，并理解许可证选择背后的条款组合逻辑，为开源生态中的法律合规提供结构化分析工具。

Abstract: Licenses are legal instruments that inventors may use to protect the technologies they build and regulate how they are used -- however, the nature of their authorship and selection means that how they are interpreted, chosen, and enforced is largely unstructured. In practice, this makes it difficult to compare licenses at scale -- when is one license considered more permissive than the other, and when are their terms incomparable to each other? Currently, there is a growing list of licenses that are introduced and used, but there is no systematic way to study their relationships. This matters for platforms such as Hugging Face, GitHub, and the Python Package Index, where developers publish or build upon technologies that each have their own licenses. Using large language models (LLMs), we introduce methods for comparing licenses at scale: first, in a pairwise fashion to construct a partial ordering based on permissiveness, and second, by drawing on existing taxonomies of software licenses. The former allows us to trace restrictiveness, and the latter allows us to understand license selection as a combination of shared provisions. Our analysis recovers certain interpretable attributes that correspond to stricter licenses, with legal implications for the open-source ecosystem.

</details>


### [28] [An Empirical Study of Security Calibration in Large Language Models for Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31159)
*Mohammed Latif Siddiq,Md. Nafiu Rahman,Joanna C. S. Santos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次大规模研究LLM生成代码的安全性校准问题，评估了GPT-4o-mini、Gemini-2.0-Flash和Qwen3-Coder-Next三个模型。研究发现：过度自信普遍存在；安全校准优于功能校准；基于校准的自动修复效果有限且易引入功能回归；架构门控在控制基准上有效但实际仓库级场景下校准反而恶化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM正快速改变软件开发，但在安全关键场景中使用时，模型是否知道其生成的代码不安全？这一校准属性（模型置信度与输出正确性是否对齐）尚未被充分研究，因此需要大规模实证分析。

Method: 对GPT-4o-mini、Gemini-2.0-Flash和Qwen3-Coder-Next三个模型在多种温度设置下进行评估，使用两类互补基准：自包含安全任务和多语言仓库级上下文。通过对比功能校准与安全校准，并研究校准引导的自动修复及缓解策略（如架构门控）。

Result: 过度自信在所有评估模型中普遍存在；功能校准始终差于安全校准；校准引导的自动修复仅带来有限改进且频繁引入功能回归；架构门控在控制基准上改善校准，但在真实仓库级设置中校准恶化，增加高置信度脆弱输出的风险。

Conclusion: LLM生成代码的安全校准存在系统性缺陷，过度自信问题突出。安全校准虽优于功能校准但仍有不足。现有的缓解策略（如校准引导修复、架构门控）效果有限，尤其在真实仓库级场景下效果退化，亟需更可靠的校准方法。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are rapidly transforming software development, yet their use in security-critical contexts raises a key question: do models know when their generated code is insecure? This property, known as calibration, measures whether a model's confidence aligns with the true correctness of its outputs. We present the first large-scale empirical study of security calibration in LLM-generated code. We evaluate GPT-4o-mini, Gemini-2.0-Flash, and Qwen3-Coder-Next across multiple temperature settings on two complementary benchmarks: self-contained security tasks and multi-language repository-level contexts. Our results suggest that overconfidence is prevalent across the evaluated LLMs. Functional calibration is consistently worse than security calibration, suggesting that models estimate security outcomes more reliably than functional correctness, potentially because functional correctness depends on complex execution behavior. We also examine whether calibration-guided automated repair can help remediate vulnerabilities in LLM-generated code, finding only limited improvements while frequently introducing functional regressions. Moreover, we study different mitigation strategies for reducing False Trust, where models assign high confidence to vulnerable code. The results show that although architectural gating improves calibration on controlled benchmarks, calibration deteriorates in realistic repository-level settings, increasing the risk of high-confidence vulnerable outputs.

</details>


### [29] [FeatX: Editing Software by Editing Features for Repository-Level Code Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31206)
*Xutian Li,Yifeng Zhu,Xianlin Zhao,Yanzhen Zou,Lu Zhang,Bing Xie*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FeatX是一个面向功能的软件编辑工具，通过提取层次化的epic-feature结构并调用三阶段进化代理，将功能编辑转化为代码补丁，在用户研究和38个真实提交的重放实验中显著降低了认知负荷并提升了可用性，功能级修改定位F1相对提升了42.6%，总成本仅0.07美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在软件演化中的交互范式以代码为中心，需要手动管理上下文和迭代提示，缺乏面向功能层面的编辑支持，导致认知负荷高和可用性低。

Method: FeatX提取仓库的层次化epic-feature结构及功能到代码的显式映射，再通过三阶段进化代理将功能编辑转化为代码补丁，并通过四个协调面板呈现工作流。

Result: 在控制用户研究和38个真实功能编辑提交的重放实验中，FeatX相比vanilla ChatGPT显著降低了认知负荷并提升了可用性；功能级修改定位F1相比强LLM基线相对提升42.6%，总成本仅0.07美元。

Conclusion: FeatX通过面向功能的编辑范式有效降低了软件演化任务中的认知负荷，提升了LLM辅助编辑的可用性和定位精度，且成本极低。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for software evolution, yet most interaction paradigms remain code-centric and require manual context management and prompt iteration. We present FeatX, a feature-oriented tool for editing software by editing features. Given an existing repository, FeatX extracts a hierarchical epic-feature structure with explicit feature-to-code mappings, then invokes a three-stage Evolution Agent to translate feature edits into code patches. The workflow is exposed through four coordinated panels. Across a controlled user study and replay experiments on 38 real-world feature-editing commits, FeatX significantly reduces cognitive load and improves usability compared with vanilla ChatGPT. It also achieves a 42.6\% relative improvement in function-level modification localization F1 over strong LLM baselines, at substantially lower cost (\$0.07 in total). The tool and collected dataset are available at https://github.com/a496263365/FeatX/tree/demo, with a demonstration video at https://youtu.be/OZqKZ4Ii-yM.

</details>


### [30] [Mutating the "Immutable": A Large-Scale Study of Git Tag Alterations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31354)
*Solal Rapaport,Laurent Pautet,Samuel Tardieu,Stefano Zacchiroli,Théo Zimmermann*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究首次大规模实证分析Git标签变更现象，在3.284亿个软件仓库中发现1020万次标签变更，影响18.9万个唯一仓库。与Nixpkgs的交叉分析显示32个包引用了被变更的标签，其中7个出现可验证的构建错误，证明标签变更会破坏可复现的包构建。建议构建系统和包管理器使用加密提交哈希固定依赖，开发平台提供标签变更审计日志，社区应建立标签变更的系统监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Git标签被普遍视为不可变引用，用于标记发布版本并保障构建可复现性和软件供应链完整性，但Git允许通过删除或强制推送来更改标签。这种标签变更的普遍性威胁到可复现构建和依赖完整性，而此前缺乏大规模实证研究来量化这一问题的严重程度。

Method: 对Software Heritage中3.284亿个软件仓库进行大规模分析，识别出1020万次标签变更，覆盖18.9万个唯一仓库；进一步与Nixpkgs包管理器进行交叉分析，验证标签变更对实际构建系统的影响。

Result: 发现1020万次标签变更，影响18.9万个唯一仓库；与Nixpkgs交叉分析发现32个包引用了被变更的标签，其中7个出现可验证的构建错误。

Conclusion: 标签不可变的假设在现实中不成立，标签变更会破坏可复现构建。建议采用加密提交哈希固定依赖、建立标签变更审计日志、并推动社区将标签变更监控纳入供应链安全实践。

Abstract: Git tags are commonly viewed as immutable references in software development, marking releases and specific repository states that underpin build reproducibility and software supply-chain integrity. Despite their intended immutability, Git allows tags to be altered through deletion or modification via force-pushed updates. The prevalence of such alterations threatens reproducible builds and dependency integrity. We conduct the first large-scale empirical study of tag alterations in public code repositories, analyzing 328.4 M software repositories from Software Heritage and identifying 10.2 M tag alterations affecting 189 k unique repositories. A cross-analysis with Nixpkgs reveals that 32 packages reference tags altered in our dataset, with 7 exhibiting confirmed build errors, providing concrete evidence that tag alterations break reproducible package builds. Our findings challenge the widespread assumption that tags are immutable anchors for released software. We therefore recommend that build systems and package managers pin dependencies to cryptographic commit hashes, that development forges expose tagmutation audit logs, and that the community adopt systematic monitoring of tag alterations as a standard supply-chain security practice.

</details>


### [31] [A Self-Negotiation Framework for Ethical Decision-Making during Task Interruptions in Service Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31357)
*Nele Reichert,Mashal Afzal Memon,Marco Autili,Nico Hochgeschwender*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自协商框架，通过为每位用户建立伦理偏好画像，使服务机器人在多人同时请求服务时，无需外部协调即可自主进行符合伦理的冲突仲裁，并在仿真中验证了其在1.5秒内完成多边协商、结果与用户偏好一致且运行时接近线性增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖静态规则或集中式仲裁，无法支持机器人自主进行基于伦理的冲突解决，导致多用户同时请求服务时缺乏合理的优先级判定机制。

Method: 提出自协商框架：为每位用户构建包含上下文伦理偏好与条件的伦理画像，通过内部协商过程解决冲突，并基于ROS模块化实现。

Result: 系统一致产生与用户伦理偏好对齐的结果，支持多边协商，响应时间在1.5秒以内，运行时随用户输入增加呈近线性增长。

Conclusion: 该自协商框架能有效支持服务机器人在多用户冲突场景下进行自主、伦理对齐的决策，具有良好的实时性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Service robots operating in public environments frequently encounter interruptions when multiple users request service simultaneously. Resolving such conflicts requires ethical decision-making, as prioritizing one user request can disadvantage another. Current approaches rely on static rules or centralized arbitration and do not support autonomous, ethics-based conflict resolution. This paper addresses the question of how a single robot can arbitrate between multiple users during task interruptions and make ethically aligned decisions without relying on external coordination. We introduce a self-negotiation framework that represents each user by an ethical profile that captures their contextual ethical preferences and conditions, and resolves conflicts through an internal negotiation process. The framework is implemented in a modular ROS-based implementation and evaluated in simulation with a realistic interruption scenario. The results show that the system consistently produces user ethical preference-aligned outcomes, supports multilateral negotiation among users, and responds within 1.5 seconds, with near-linear runtime growth under increasing user input.

</details>


### [32] [MOA: A Profiling-Guided LLM Framework for Memory-Optimization Automation at Codebase Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31368)
*Jiaxi Liang,Yuanxiang Shi,Zezhou Yang,Chenxiong Qian*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MOA是一个基于LLM的自动化框架，通过三个智能体分析、检测和修复大规模C/C++代码库中的内存低效问题，在超1亿行代码的OpenHarmony系统中识别13种反模式、检测超1万处低效、生成769个补丁（专家接受率92.5%），平均堆内存降低42.2%、二进制体积减少10.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有内存优化工作流缺乏端到端自动化，开发者需手动综合复杂工具输出转化为可执行且语义保留的修复方案，难以在大规模代码库中扩展。

Method: MOA由三个智能体组成：Analysis从性能剖析数据中挖掘反模式；Checker Generator通过模板引导的细化综合静态分析器；Patcher通过状态机驱动的工作流生成优化补丁。

Result: 在OpenHarmony（超1亿行C/C++代码）上，从3个服务中识别13种反模式（9种未知），在7个服务中检测超1万处低效，生成769个补丁，专家接受率92.5%，平均堆内存降低42.2%，二进制体积减少10.6%。

Conclusion: MOA可作为生产规模性能工程的有效工具，实现了内存低效的自动化检测与修复。

Abstract: Modern large-scale software systems often suffer from pervasive memory inefficiencies (e.g., bloat, churn), leading to excessive resource costs and performance degradation. Existing optimization workflows lack end-to-end automation, forcing developers to manually synthesize complex tool outputs into actionable and semantics-preserving fixes, precluding scalability in large codebases. To address this, this paper presents MOA, an LLM-driven framework that automatically detects and repairs recurring memory inefficiencies across production-scale codebases. Specifically, MOA operates through three agents: an Analyzer that mines anti-patterns from profiling data, a Checker Generator that synthesizes static analyzers through template-guided refinement, and a Patcher that generates optimization patches via state-machine-driven workflows. Our evaluation on OpenHarmony, an open-source operating system with over 100 million lines of C/C++ code, shows that MOA identifies 13 anti-patterns (9 previously unknown) from 3 profiled services, detects over 10,000 inefficiencies across a broader set of 7 services, and generates 769 patches with 92.5% expert acceptance rate, achieving 42.2% heap reduction and 10.6% binary size reduction on average. We envision MOA as a valuable tool for performance engineering at production scale.

</details>


### [33] [Failure-Based Testing for Deep Reinforcement Learning Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31372)
*Weibin Lin,Jiangtao Meng,Zheng Zheng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Prior Random Testing (PRT)，一种基于任务诱发失败洞察的黑盒测试方法，通过优先测试高难度任务区域来高效检测深度强化学习智能体的失败情况，相比随机测试降低成本50%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于奖励信号的DRL测试方法对性能接近最优的智能体无效，因其在标准条件下奖励信号始终较高，难以发现关键失败。

Method: 提出PRT方法，利用任务固有困难度线索，优先测试失败易发区域，同时保持测试用例多样性，实现黑盒级别的定向优先级排序。

Result: 在四个广泛使用的基准测试中，PRT在首次失败发现成本和测试用例多样性方面均排名前列；相比随机测试，多样性更优且测试成本降低超过50%。

Conclusion: PRT通过任务诱发失败洞察引导测试优先级，有效提升了DRL智能体的失败检测效率，优于现有模糊测试、搜索式和生成式方法。

Abstract: Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) agents have been widely adopted across diverse domains to address challenging decision-making problems, such as autonomous driving and robotic control. Given that many of these applications are safety- and security-critical, rigorous testing of DRL agents is indispensable. Existing testing methods are typically guided by reward signals to detect failures. However, for well-trained agents, whose performance approaches optimal levels in standard operating conditions, reward signals remain generally high, making current methods ineffective at uncovering critical failures.
  To address these challenges, we propose a novel failure-based method that leverages task-induced failure insights to enhance failure detection capability while reducing the number of tests required. Since DRL agents are inherently designed with human-defined tasks, they provide valuable cues about task difficulty. Intuitively, a DRL agent is more likely to fail when confronted with a more difficult task; therefore, PRT prioritizes these tasks. Building on this foundation, we propose Prior Random Testing, a black-box failure-based testing method that enables targeted prioritization while preserving the diversity of generated test cases. Guided by task-induced failure insights, PRT prioritizes failure-prone regions of the input domain, thereby facilitating efficient failure detection.
  PRT is evaluated on four widely used benchmarks and compared with different state-of-the-art methods including fuzzing, search-based and generative-based methods. PRT ranks among the top performers in terms of both the cost of finding the first failure and the diversity of test cases. Notably, compared to random testing, PRT achieves better diversity and reduces the testing cost by over 50%.

</details>


### [34] [Maximizing Parallel Execution of Series-Parallel Task Graphs for Safety-Critical Embedded Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31481)
*Jinghao Sun,Zhenchu Hu,Ye Ma,Bo Tang,Qingxu Deng,Xiuzhen Cheng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于拉格朗日迭代启发式算法（LIH）来解决串并联任务图的最大并行执行问题（MPE），在分阶段批处理模型下通过加权团划分最小化批处理执行时间总和。实验表明LIH在91.25%的实例中匹配精确最优解，平均gap仅0.073%，运行时间远优于精确基线方法，并在PLC梯形逻辑案例中验证了端到端有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键型嵌入式控制程序必须在有界时间内完成每个控制周期。当程序的依赖结构中包含可并发执行的子任务时，传统处理器上的顺序执行会成为性能瓶颈，因此需要研究如何最大化并行执行以提升实时性。

Method: 将MPE问题形式化为加权团划分问题以最小化批执行时间总和（每个批次成本由其最慢任务决定）；提出LIH算法，构建基于定价过滤的受限候选批次池（包含单例列和随机贪心团生成），应用拉格朗日定价指导列选择，并通过修复过程恢复合法团划分。

Result: LIH在91.25%的可比实例中匹配精确最优解，平均gap为0.073%，平均运行时间18.19ms；在最大精确参考节点设置下，精确基线平均需数百秒，而LIH保持在50ms以下。

Conclusion: LIH在保证接近最优解质量的同时显著降低了计算时间，适用于安全关键嵌入式系统的实时优化；通过PLC梯形逻辑案例验证了从PLCOpen程序到MPE图优化再到FPGA实现的端到端可行性。

Abstract: Safety-critical embedded control programs must complete each control cycle within a bounded period. Sequential execution on conventional processors can become a bottleneck when the dependency structure of the program contains subtasks that could be executed concurrently. This paper studies the Maximum Parallel Execution (MPE) problem for series-parallel task graphs under a staged batching model: compatible tasks inside one batch execute in parallel, while the selected batches are launched sequentially in a topological order that preserves precedence. We formulate MPE as a weighted clique-partitioning problem that minimizes the sum of batch execution times, with each batch cost determined by its slowest task. To solve this problem efficiently, we propose a Lagrangian-based Iterative Heuristic (LIH). LIH constructs a pricing-filtered restricted pool of feasible candidate batches from singleton columns and random greedy clique generation. It then applies Lagrangian pricing to guide column selection and uses a repair procedure to recover a legal clique partition. Experiments against a weighted mixed-graph-coloring branch-and-bound baseline and a randomized greedy baseline show that LIH matches the exact optimum in 91.25% of comparable instances, with an average gap of 0.073% and an average runtime of 18.19 ms. In the largest exact-reference node setting, the exact baseline requires hundreds of seconds on average, whereas LIH remains below 50 ms. We further present an end-to-end PLC ladder-logic case study in which PLCOpen-style programs are converted to MPE graphs, optimized by LIH, translated into FPGA-oriented HDL, and simulated against the original PLC scan execution.

</details>
