*Nataliia Klievtsova,Juergen Mangler,Stefanie Rinderle-Ma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文探讨了知识管理与流程管理中"流程"这一交叉点，通过分析流程资源（数据、对象、制品、人）之间的关系，提出了知识密集型流程的统一定义。研究分析了知识管理流程中的常见模式及其在流程模型中的表示，将流程模型分为6个类别以反映知识密集度，并收集了通用知识流程模式。这些模式源自真实世界的知识收集过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识管理和流程管理两个领域对"流程"的理解存在割裂：知识管理关注知识密集型流程与人类参与，而流程管理注重流程的形式化建模与执行。本文旨在弥合这一鸿沟，探索流程资源之间的关系，以形成对知识密集型流程更普适的定义。

Method: 通过分析知识管理流程中频繁出现的不同模式，研究这些模式在流程模型中的表示方式以及所代表的知识类型。模式来源于真实世界的知识收集过程。

Result: 提出了流程模型的6类分类法，用于直观反映知识密集度；收集了通用知识流程模式集合。

Conclusion: 对知识密集型流程采取整体视角（建模、执行、监控、评估），将加速并提升数字化转型项目的质量。

Abstract: The artefact at the intersection of knowledge and process management is the process, which describes how enterprises are generating value. In knowledge management literature the relation of knowledge and processes is discussed, often leading to the definition of knowledge intensive processes, which entail a high level of human involvement. The process management community on the other hand focuses on the formalization of processes as models that can be executed, monitored, and improved. In this paper we explore the relationship of process resources such as data, objects, artefacts and humans, in order to come to more universal definition of knowledge intensive processes. For this purpose, we analyze different patterns that frequently occur in knowledge management processes, how they are represented in process models, and which types of knowledge are represented by them. The results comprise a categorization of process models into 6 categories that allow to easily see the knowledge intensity as well as a collection of common knowledge process patterns. The patterns were derived from a real-world knowledge gathering process, which includes a wide array of patterns and concepts detailed in this paper. We think that a holistic view on knowledge intensive processes, how to model, execute, monitor, and asses their impact, will speed up and improve the quality of digital transformation projects.

</details>


### [36] [Falsification, Not Exposure: An Internally Preregistered Placebo-Controlled Decomposition of Self-Repair Feedback in Frozen Small Code Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31511)
*Mehmet Iscan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于波普尔证伪主义的方法论，通过安慰剂对照实验分解反馈包，证明代码加上可执行反例的反馈比单纯重试更有效，而指令或自我批评等无内容反馈无显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部署场景中，小型冻结代码模型通常被当作重试机制，在看到自身错误输出后修复程序。但从波普尔证伪主义视角看，生成的程序是猜想，测试执行违反是可执行的反例，因此反馈的价值不应归因于重新暴露于失败代码，而应归因于猜想是否受到外部可执行批评的检验。现有研究缺乏对反馈内容真正起效成分的严格分解和因果检验。

Method: 构建安慰剂对照实验仪器：(1) 反馈包分解为代码、事实、指令等成分；(2) 盲重采样基线（匹配输出生成预算）；(3) 内容无关、形状匹配的安慰剂对照；(4) 匹配预算的不一致对检验；(5) 新鲜生成确认；(6) 可执行审计。在HumanEval+/MBPP+的290个死任务单元上，使用0.5B-1.5B冻结模型，主实验7000次生成，预注册跟进1400次生成。

Result: 盲重采样比裸代码重试多解锁+18个任务（25/7，Holm p=0.0021）。代码加事实比裸代码多解锁+18（21/3，p=0.00042），比通用要点安慰剂多解锁+15（p=0.0041）。仅指令效果不可区分（+3，p=0.36）。代码加事实与盲重采样持平（各26个解锁，不等价）。六个外部控制跟进与内容无关的形状安慰剂持平。

Conclusion: 在这种设置下，证伪主义的帮助不在于词汇或自我批评，而在于与外部可执行反例的比较。本文的贡献不是新修复算法，而是一套可证伪的研究方法论（反馈包分解、安慰剂镜像、匹配预算不一致对检验、新鲜生成确认、可执行审计），使模型的程序猜想和研究者的"反馈内容有效"声明都变得可证伪。

Abstract: In deployment settings where retraining is infeasible, small frozen code models are routinely asked to repair a failed program after seeing their own failing output, usually treated as a retry mechanism. From a Popperian view, a generated program is a conjecture and a test-execution violation is an oracle-relative, executable counterexample, so feedback's value should be attributed not to re-exposure to failing code but to whether the conjecture is opened to external, executable criticism. As the third stage of a falsification-centered measurement program, this study builds a placebo-controlled instrument that decomposes the feedback packet against a blind-resampling baseline at matched output-generation budget and against content-free, shape-matched placebos. The contribution is not a new repair algorithm but a reflexive methodology (packet decomposition, placebo mirroring, matched-budget discordant-pair tests, fresh-generation confirmation, executable audits) that makes both the model's program conjecture and the researcher's "feedback content works" claim falsifiable. Across six HumanEval+/MBPP+ cells with three 0.5B-1.5B frozen models, 290 dead task-cell units (no best-of-8 candidate passing the public tier) were evaluated; the main run produced 7,000 fresh generations and a preregistered follow-up 1,400 more. Blind resampling exceeded bare-code retry by +18 net unlocks (25/7, Holm p=0.0021). Code-plus-facts recovered +18 over bare code (21/3, p=0.00042) and +15 over a generic-bullet placebo (p=0.0041). An instruction-only effect was not distinguishable (+3, p=0.36). Code-plus-facts and blind resampling tied at 26 unlocks each (not equivalence). Six external-controller follow-ups tied a content-free shape placebo. In this regime, falsification helped not as vocabulary or self-critique, but as comparison with external, executable counterexamples.

</details>


### [37] [FormIDEAble: Safe and Socially-aware Autonomous Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31572)
*Livia Lestingi,Amel Bennaceur,Marcello M. Bersani,Carlos Gavidia-Calderon,Anastasia Kordoni,Mark Levine,Bashar Nuseibeh,Matteo Rossi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出FormIDEAble，一种形式化方法，通过在Priced Timed Markov Decision Process中建模人机协作并求解成本有界可达性问题，为社交关键系统生成具有安全保证的社交感知协作策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么考虑社交动态但缺乏形式化保证，要么提供形式化保证但忽略了人类行为，因此需要一种既能保证安全又能感知社交行为的决策方法。

Method: 将人机协作建模为Priced Timed Markov Decision Process，将决策问题形式化为成本有界可达性问题，从而合成具有安全保证的社交感知协作策略。

Result: 在紧急疏散场景中的实验验证了方法的有效性，并揭示了优化目标与安全保证之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: FormIDEAble为社交关键系统提供了形式化保证的、社交感知决策的形式化基础。

Abstract: Autonomous agents operating in socio-critical settings must coordinate with humans under uncertainty while respecting explicit safety constraints. Existing approaches either account for social dynamics without formal guarantees or provide formal assurance while abstracting away human behaviour. We introduce FormIDEAble, a formally grounded approach for synthesising socially-aware cooperation strategies with safety guarantees. The cooperation between humans and the autonomous agent is modelled as a Priced Timed Markov Decision Process, and decision-making is formulated as a cost-bounded reachability problem. We illustrate the approach using an emergency evacuation scenario. Initial experimental evidence demonstrates the effectiveness of the approach and highlights the trade-offs between optimisation and safety guarantees. FormIDEAble provides a principled foundation for formally assured, socially-aware decision-making in socio-critical systems.

</details>


### [38] [From Failure to Alignment: A Requirements Engineering Framework for Machine Learning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31589)
*Amel Bennaceur,Gopi Krishnan Rajbahadur,Prince Mercy,Bashar Nuseibeh,Faeq Alrimawi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出REAL框架，一个基于需求工程的模型驱动方法，通过整合数据、模型和系统需求，利用失败驱动探索替代需求，并进行迭代可追溯的精化，以开发更符合利益相关者需求的机器学习系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织需要能够检查机器学习系统的可信度并向利益相关者清晰传达，而需求工程通过关注利益相关者可以推动MLS设计，但目前缺乏一个系统化的、由利益相关者需求和MLS开发约束共同驱动的需求建模与推理过程。

Method: 提出REAL框架，基于三个原则：(1) 将数据、模型和系统整体需求编织在一起；(2) 利用失败驱动探索替代需求；(3) 对MLS需求进行迭代和可追溯的精化。

Result: 通过自动驾驶示例展示了REAL框架，结果表明该框架支持开发更符合利益相关者需求的MLS，且提供了可复现的在线资源包。

Conclusion: REAL框架能够有效帮助开发与利益相关者需求对齐的机器学习系统，为MLS的需求工程提供了一个系统化的模型驱动方法。

Abstract: Organisations designing, developing, and deploying machine learning systems (MLS) need to be able to check that these systems are trustworthy, and communicate this clearly to their stakeholders, be they different categories of users, engineers, or wider society. By focusing on stakeholders, Requirements Engineering is well positioned to drive the design and engineering of MLS that align with the needs of their stakeholders. Yet, we still need a systematic process for modelling and reasoning about requirements for MLS that is driven both by stakeholders' needs and constraints for MLS development.
  This paper proposes a framework entitled REAL (Requirements Engineering for mAchines that Learn - and Fail) to help develop MLS that align with stakeholders' needs by adopting a requirements engineering approach. This model-based framework is based on three principles. First, weaving together requirements for data, models, and the system as a whole. Second, using failure to drive the exploration of alternative requirements. Third, iterative and traceable refinement of MLS requirements.
  We demonstrate the proposed framework using an example from autonomous driving and show that REAL supports the development of MLS that better align with stakeholders' requirements. A replication package is available online.

</details>

*Jukka Ruohonen,Justin Stark,Scott Wilkie,Mikkel Baun Kjærgaard*

TL;DR: 本文提出将数字主权（DS）概念化为软件架构的质量属性，特别是在欧盟云计算政策框架下。分析表明DS具备经典质量属性的可测量性、验证性、权衡性及基于场景的分析方法等特性，从而为这一模糊概念提供了清晰的分析工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字主权日益重要但概念模糊，缺乏可操作的分析框架。作者旨在通过软件架构视角将其转化为可衡量、可验证的质量属性，以增强其分析严谨性和实际应用价值。

Method: 采用概念分析法，将DS与软件架构质量属性的经典特征（可测量性、可验证性、权衡性、场景方法论）进行对比映射，结合欧盟云计算政策框架进行案例分析。

Result: DS能够被有效地概念化为软件架构的质量属性，满足经典质量属性的关键特性，包括可测量性、有效性验证、属性间的权衡关系以及场景分析方法论。

Conclusion: 将数字主权概念化为质量属性可以强化其分析精度，为欧盟及其他地区的云计算架构设计提供了可操作化的评估框架。

Abstract: Digital sovereignty (DS) is an increasingly important concept and political agenda throughout the world, including in the European Union (EU). However, the concept is also regrettably vague. With this critical point in mind, the paper presents an analysis of digital sovereignty as a quality attribute for software architectures in the context of cloud computing and the EU's policy frameworks for it. The analysis reveals that DS can be sharpened analytically by conceptualizing it as a quality attribute. The analysis further demonstrates how DS satisfies many of the classical properties of quality attributes for software architectures, including their measurability and validation, the trade-offs they involve, and the scenario-based methodology commonly used for analyzing them.

</details>


### [40] [ScratchWorld: Evaluating If World Models Compute Executable Consequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31689)
*Yufeng Lin,Jialu Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ScratchWorld基准，以Scratch项目为可执行世界，通过精确状态度量（value-aware changed-field F1）评估模型的世界建模能力。实验表明，七个语言/推理模型在困难设置下最高仅13.8% F1，而简单复制输入状态可达到98.0%的虚假全状态准确率但0.0% changed-field F1，揭示了现有评估中持久状态复制导致的准确性幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型评估在稀疏变化世界中，因过度奖励复制持久状态而高估模型性能，缺乏精确衡量模型是否真正理解状态变化因果关系的度量标准。

Method: 构建ScratchWorld基准，将Scratch项目作为可执行世界，使用固定Scratch VM生成回放验证的转移、隐藏变量、因果轨迹和反事实结果。提出value-aware changed-field F1指标，仅对变化字段及其执行值给予分数。设置四种输入模态（原始程序、结构化状态、自然语言、渲染），以及多项诊断任务（下一状态预测、长程追踪、因果事件归因、反事实预测、隐状态漂移、干预敏感性等）。

Result: 七个语言/推理模型在状态部分观测压力测试中最高仅13.8% value-aware changed-field F1。复制输入状态可达到98.0%的全状态字段准确率但0.0% changed-field F1，真实项目上膨胀最严重。模型常对动作或干预做出反应，但未遵循决定变化值的可执行规则。

Conclusion: 现有世界模型评估存在因复制持久状态而产生准确性幻觉的问题，ScratchWorld通过精确的changed-field度量揭示了模型在真正理解状态变化因果方面的严重不足，为更可靠的世界模型评估提供了新范式。

Abstract: World-model evaluations often score a predicted future by overlap with a target state or observation. In sparse-change worlds, this can turn copied persistent state into apparent accuracy. We introduce ScratchWorld, an offline diagnostic benchmark that treats Scratch projects as executable worlds and uses a pinned Scratch VM to produce replay-verified transitions, hidden variables, causal traces, and counterfactual outcomes. ScratchWorld evaluates next-state prediction, long-horizon tracking, causal event attribution, and counterfactual prediction; each replay-verified target can be presented under raw-program, structured-state, natural-language, or rendered input modalities, and our experiments use the structured-state condition. Its primary state metric is value-aware changed-field $F_1$, which gives credit only for the changed field and its executed value. In a 659-example release, seven prompted language/reasoning models reach at most 13.8% value-aware changed-field $F_1$ in a state-only partial-observation stress test. A same-instance copy diagnostic makes the overlap confound concrete: copying the input state reaches 98.0% implied full-state field accuracy and 0.0% changed-field $F_1$, with the largest inflation on real projects. Auxiliary diagnostics separate hidden-state rollout drift, intervention sensitivity, causal attribution, and perturbation robustness. Across these settings, models often react to actions or interventions without following the executable rule that determines the changed value.

</details>


### [41] [AdaTrans: Automated C to Rust Transformation via Error-Adaptive Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31706)
*Xiaofan Liu,Zecan Li,Zhuang Zhao,Ziqi Shuai,Yanming Yang,Qi Xin,Jifeng Xuan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AdaTrans是一个将C代码自动转换为Rust的框架，通过策略驱动的检索增强生成、错误分层转换策略和多阶段验证管道，在104个算法问题上实现了95.51%的编译通过率和81.09%的问题解决率，不安全代码率仅1.19%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: C代码到Rust的自动转换因Rust严格的借用和所有权语义而面临挑战，现有LLM方法常产生违反规则或依赖不安全构造的代码，亟需一种系统化的解决方案。

Method: AdaTrans框架包含三个核心机制：策略驱动的检索增强生成（RAG）机制将编译器错误映射到特定修复；错误分层转换策略（ESTS）根据错误类型自适应调整行为；多阶段验证管道确保代码可编译性和功能等价性。

Result: 在104个算法问题数据集上，AdaTrans的平均编译通过率为95.51%，平均问题解决率为81.09%，不安全文件率仅1.19%，显著优于现有工具。

Conclusion: AdaTrans通过结合RAG、错误分层策略和多阶段验证，有效提升了C到Rust代码转换的可靠性和安全性，为自动化迁移提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The automated transformation of C code to Rust is challenging due to Rust's strict ownership and borrowing semantics. While Large Language Models (LLMs) show promise, they often produce code that violates these rules or relies on unsafe constructs. We propose AdaTrans, a framework that addresses these issues through three core mechanisms: a Strategy-Driven Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) mechanism to map compiler errors to specific repairs, an Error-Stratified Transformation Strategy (ESTS) that adapts its behavior based on error types, and a multi-stage validation pipeline to ensure both compilability and functional equivalence. Evaluating on a dataset of 104 algorithmic problems, AdaTrans achieves a mean compilation pass rate of 95.51% and a mean solve rate of 81.09%, significantly outperforming existing tools while maintaining an unsafe file rate of only 1.19%.

</details>

Abstract: While code obfuscation impairs human code comprehension, it remains unclear if large language models share these failure modes. Evaluating several LLMs with five obfuscation tiers using the Block Model, we localize comprehension failures at the atom, block, relational, and macro levels. We find that reasoning-tuned models demonstrate significant alignment with human difficulty patterns across experience levels, whereas instruction and coder-tuned models show near-zero correlation. Chain-of-Thought trace length tracks task difficulty across tasks. Results indicate that performance under control-flow flattening degrades in proportion to state-space complexity, while adversarial identifier renaming disrupts comprehension through the interaction of semantic displacement and identifier-level interference. These findings suggest that reasoning-tuned LLMs approximate human sensitivity to code complexity more effectively than instruction-tuned variants.

</details>

Result: 构建了JETO-Bench基准，包含660个识别出的ETIP和91个手动验证的可执行ETIP，覆盖174个开源Java仓库，扫描了11年开源开发历史及近180万次提交。OpenHands在91个可执行ETIP上修复成功率为14.3%（13/91）。

Abstract: Automated fixing of performance issues is gaining increasing attention. However, existing benchmarks of execution time improvement patches are fixed datasets that target Python, C++, or .NET and cannot be extended to new patches according to user-defined configurations. In this paper, we present JETO-Mine, the first configurable and reusable tool for automatically creating reproducible benchmarks of execution time improvement patches (ETIPs) in real-world Java projects. JETO-Mine employs a three-phase pipeline: a static analysis phase that crawls GitHub repositories and identifies ETIPs using user-defined filters and an LLM-based issue classifier, a dynamic analysis phase that wraps the identified ETIPs in Docker images for fully reproducible execution and performs statistical testing to find objective evidence of execution time improvement, and an evaluation harness that enables quantitative assessment of both generated patches and generated tests. Unlike existing benchmarks, JETO-Mine is designed as a reusable tool that allows researchers continuously collect new benchmarks with their own desired filters and statistical rigor levels. We use JETO-Mine to build JETO-Bench, a benchmark of 660 identified ETIPs and 91 manually verified executable ETIPs collected from 174 open-source Java repositories. To build JETO-Bench, JETO-Mine scans 11 years of open-source development history and nearly 1.8 million commits. We run OpenHands, a leading open-source coding agent, on the 91 manually verified executable ETIPs in JETO-Bench and find that it correctly fixes 14.3% (13/91) of the issues, aligning with results reported by similar studies on other programming languages. Our results also reveal that open-source Java projects largely lack tests that demonstrate execution time improvements, presenting an opportunity for future research in test generation.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究分布式软件生态中接口变体（interface variant）的演化动态，通过对npm、Maven Central、PyPI、crates.io四个包管理器的历史挖掘与2100次解析器探针实验，测量了生态系统特异的选型系数s，并区分了接口变体传播的三种证据角色：固定（fixation）是机械推论、检查器推导（checker-derived）方向携带采纳信号但非正交检验、无检查器的时间检验未能超越仅基于年龄的基线。结果表明解析器证据可转化为群体输入，但当前注册数据尚无法闭合解析器到采纳的反馈回路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有兼容性研究通常将接口变更视为局部的写入者-读取者决策，忽视了分布式软件栈中变体通过RPC、遥测、中间件或服务契约在提供者、消费者及传递依赖之间传播的结构化人口过程。本文旨在探究这种观测何时构成具有承载能力的软件工程估计量，而非仅仅是演化标签的重命名。

Method: 1) 挖掘npm、Maven Central、PyPI、crates.io四个生态系统的包图历史；2) 执行2100次包管理器解析器探针实验；3) 从干净的冲突概率中估计生态系统特异的选型系数s；4) 利用s对观测包图上的成对比较吸收过程进行前向评估；5) 区分三种证据角色：固定（fixation）、检查器推导方向（checker-derived direction）、无检查器时间检验（checker-free temporal test），并通过方向排列零假设和Brier分数/AUC等指标进行验证。

Result: 1) 固定项：一旦s被测量，偏离1/N的中性期望是机械性的；2) 检查器推导方向携带采纳信号：方向排列零假设下检查器方向间隙MAE为0.07，而零假设中位数为0.43（p=0.002）；3) 无检查器时间检验：早期解析器通道特征未能超越仅基于年龄的基线（Brier 0.28 vs 0.24, AUC 0.51 vs 0.54）。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了一个可复现的估计器审计框架，用于分布式包图中接口变体的动态分析，揭示了解析器证据在何种条件下可转化为群体选择输入，同时也表明当前注册数据尚无法闭合从解析器到采纳的完整反馈回路。

Abstract: Compatibility research usually treats an interface change as a local writer-reader decision. Distributed software stacks make that decision population structured: an RPC, telemetry, middleware, or service-contract variant is introduced by one provider release and then spreads, stalls, or is mediated across consumers, transitive dependencies, and resolver rules. This paper asks when that observation is a load-bearing software-engineering estimator rather than evolutionary relabeling. We mine interface histories, audit npm, Maven Central, PyPI, and crates.io package graphs, execute 2100 package-manager resolver probes, estimate an ecosystem-specific selection coefficient $s$ from clean conflict probabilities, and use that measured $s$ to forward evaluate a pairwise-comparison absorbing process on the observed package graph. We separate three evidential roles. Fixation is a forward evaluation, not independent evidence: once $s$ is measured, deviation from $1/N$ follows mechanically from the non-neutral process. Checker-derived direction carries adoption signal: a direction-permutation null gives checker-direction gap MAE 0.07 versus null median 0.43 ($p=0.002$). But because that direction is derived from the same boundary state whose admitting frequency is predicted, it is a diagnostic rather than an orthogonal selection test. The stricter checker-free temporal test asks whether early resolver-channel features predict later blocked-to-admitted flips; in this snapshot they do not beat age-only (Brier 0.28 versus 0.24, AUC 0.51 versus 0.54). The result is a reproducible estimator audit for interface-variant dynamics in distributed package graphs, showing where resolver evidence becomes population input and where the current registry data still fail to close the resolver-to-adoption loop.

</details>

*Alessandro Botta,Shiven Garisa,Jaya Vardhini Akurathi,Ahsanul Ameen Sabit,Trey Woodlief,Soneya Binta Hossain*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CoCoMUT，一个Java工具，用于自动从项目中提取方法级上下文信息（包括类、文档、调用图等），并生成结构化的JSON数据集。该工具在20个真实Java项目上评估，生成了56,512条方法上下文记录，源-字节码调用边匹配率达97.8%，人工审计正确率99.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程助手通常需要超越孤立方法体的上下文信息（如类信息、文档、调用者、被调用者、类型层次结构等），而手动收集这些上下文耗时、不一致且难以在大规模Java项目中复现。

Method: CoCoMUT通过以下流程实现：发现项目结构并解析构建和类路径信息 → 构建SootUp静态调用图 → 协调字节码级调用边与Spoon源提取 → 为每个目标方法生成包含源代码、类信息、文档、调用图及元数据的结构化记录，最终生成版本化的JSON数据集。

Result: 在20个真实Java仓库（Maven和Gradle各半）上评估：处理了全部20个仓库，生成56,512条方法上下文记录和386,048条序列化调用边；字节码目标属于项目源码的调用边中，97.8%成功匹配到源方法；200个随机采样方法的人工审计中，99.0%的记录通过所有正确性检查。

Conclusion: CoCoMUT提供了一个可复用、任务无关的流水线，统一了构建发现、源码提取、调用图构建、源码-字节码协调和版本化数据集生成，可为文档生成、代码解释、测试、审查、修复、搜索和程序理解等工作流提供可靠输入。

Abstract: Software-engineering assistants often need method-level context beyond an isolated body, including enclosing-class information, documentation, callers, callees, type hierarchy, and structural characteristics. Manually collecting this context is time-consuming, inconsistent, and difficult to reproduce across large Java projects.
  We present CoCoMUT, a Java tool for Code-Context Mining and Automated Dataset Generation. CoCoMUT extracts context for a focal method or generates datasets at class, package, or system scope. It discovers project structure, resolves build and classpath information, constructs a SootUp static call graph, and reconciles bytecode-level call edges with Spoon-based source extraction. Each method record combines source, class, documentation, call-graph, and metadata context, providing reproducible inputs for training and running learned software-engineering techniques.
  The key contribution is a reusable, task-independent pipeline that unifies build discovery, source extraction, call-graph construction, source-bytecode reconciliation, and versioned JSON dataset generation. The resulting records can be consumed individually as context for a focal method or collectively as datasets for documentation, explanation, testing, review, repair, search, and program-comprehension workflows. We evaluate CoCoMUT on 20 real-world Java repositories evenly split between Maven and Gradle. CoCoMUT processed all 20 repositories, emitting 56,512 method-context records and 386,048 serialized call edges. Among call edges whose bytecode targets belonged to project source, CoCoMUT reconciled 97.8% to source method identities. In a manual audit of 200 randomly sampled methods across 10 systems, 99.0% of generated context records passed all applicable correctness checks.

</details>

*Xuanyu Liu,Hui Hu,Jiao Zhao,Ziliang Wang,Zhengbing He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度强化学习的卡车-无人机协同配送方法，解决包含智能储物柜、取件、送件及禁飞区约束的最后一公里物流问题，在多数实验场景下优于传统元启发式和神经启发式方法，且计算时间极短。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际储物柜式卡车-无人机配送系统面临取件、送件、电池约束、负载依赖飞行及禁飞区绕行等多重复杂挑战，现有方法缺乏对此类综合性问题的有效求解框架。

Method: 将路由构建过程建模为马尔可夫决策过程，设计两阶段深度强化学习神经启发式方法：第一阶段用注意力编码器+双向门控循环单元解码器求解卡车路径（带容量约束车辆路径问题）；第二阶段结合策略迁移与混合调度分配启发式，构建卡车与无人机协同路径。

Result: 在不同规模实例上的实验表明，所提方法在多数情况下优于元启发式和神经启发式基线方法，且计算时间极短，具备可扩展性。

Conclusion: 所提出的深度强化学习框架能有效处理储物柜式卡车-无人机配送中的多约束协同优化问题，为实际运营约束下的最后一公里物流提供了高效、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Truck-drone delivery is an emerging last-mile logistics mode combining the long-haul capacity of trucks with the flexible service capability of drones. In locker-based operations, smart lockers serve not only as temporary parcel storage facilities but also as automated drone docking and service nodes. These automated nodes support drone takeoff, landing, parcel handover, and battery replacement, thereby significantly extending the service range and operational flexibility of drone-assisted delivery networks. However, practical locker-based delivery systems face complex real-world challenges, requiring the integrated coordination of not only parcel delivery, return pickup, battery-constrained and load-dependent drone flights, but also necessary detours around restricted airspace. To address this practical and multifaceted challenge, this paper introduces a locker-based truck-drone routing problem with integrated considerations of pickups, deliveries, and no-fly zones (LTDRP-PDNF), with the objective of minimizing the total operational cost of a fleet of drone-equipped trucks. We formulate the route construction process as a Markov Decision Process and develop a two-stage deep reinforcement learning-based neural heuristic. The first stage utilizes an attention-based encoder and a Bidirectional Gated Recurrent Unit decoder to solve the truck-only routing problem, formulated as a capacitated vehicle routing problem. The second stage combines a policy-transfer strategy with a hybrid dispatch assignment heuristic to construct fully coordinated truck and drone routes for LTDRP-PDNF. Experiments on instances of different scales demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms metaheuristic and neural heuristic baselines in most cases while maintaining exceptionally short computation times, offering an effective, scalable solution framework under practical operational constraints.

</details>

*Amos Alwala,Gabriel da Silva Lima,Wallace Moreira Bessa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于高斯RBF神经网络的自适应反馈线性化控制框架，用于四旋翼飞行器在动态不确定环境中的轨迹跟踪。该方法利用RBF网络的通用逼近能力在线补偿未建模动力学，基于Lyapunov稳定性理论推导控制律并保证闭环稳定性。在Crazyflie 2.1平台上进行的仿真和实物实验表明，相比基线反馈线性化控制器，该方法在位置范数和偏航角RMSE上分别降低了7.13%和49.27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动态不确定环境中，四旋翼飞行器的完整系统动力学部分已知或高度非线性，传统的控制方法难以应对未建模动力学和外部扰动，需要一种能够在线学习并补偿不确定性的控制框架。

Method: 基于高斯RBF神经网络的机器学习反馈线性化控制框架，通过RBF网络在线自适应更新权重来逼近和补偿未建模动力学，结合Lyapunov稳定性理论设计控制律以保证闭环稳定性和轨迹跟踪的渐近收敛。

Result: 在Crazyflie 2.1平台上的Gazebo仿真和真实飞行实验中，面对未建模空气阻力、执行器动力学和外部扰动，所提控制器在位置范数RMSE上降低7.13%以上，偏航角RMSE降低49.27%以上，且收敛速度更快。

Conclusion: 基于RBF神经网络的自适应反馈线性化控制对于存在未建模动力学和外部扰动的四旋翼轨迹跟踪任务是有效的，能够在不依赖先验训练的情况下显著提升控制精度和收敛性能。

Abstract: The control of agile quadrotors in dynamic and uncertain environments remains an open area of investigation to this day, particularly when the complete system dynamics are partially known or highly nonlinear. This work introduces a novel machine learning-based feedback-linearization control framework that employs a Gaussian Radial Basis Function (RBF) neural network (NN) to model and compensate for unmodeled dynamics in real time. The proposed controller leverages the universal approximation capability of RBF networks to model nonlinearities and uncertainties. An online adaptation of the RBF NN updates the network's weights without prior training. The control law is derived using the Lyapunov stability theory, herein guaranteeing closed-loop stability and providing theoretical guarantee of asymptotic convergence of a trajectory tracking task. Gazebo simulation and real flight experiments are conducted using the Bitcraze's Crazyflie 2.1 quadrotor subject to unmodeled air drag, actuator dynamics, and external disturbance. Despite incomplete knowledge of prior dynamics and presence of external disturbance such as air drag and drift in state estimation, the proposed controller improves trajectory tracking with rapid convergence and reduction of position-norm and yaw orientation RMSE by more than $7.13\%$ and $49.27\%$ respectively compared to baseline feedback linearization controller.

</details>


### [48] [Position: Vision-Language-Action Models Cannot Be Verified to Perform Physical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30686)
*Taozhao Chen,Ian Manchester,Huaming Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文论证当前VLA系统的性能提升主要源于语义匹配而非真正的物理泛化，因为现有评估指标（任务成功率）无法区分语义映射与物理动作决策这两种能力来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA系统在机器人操控基准上表现持续提升，但研究者普遍将性能增益归因于基于互联网数据预训练的视觉-语言模型的语义表征能够迁移到物理执行泛化。这种假设缺乏独立验证，且现有评估协议无法对其进行检验。

Method: 通过将VLA策略分解为语义映射和物理动作决策两个组件，证明任务成功率指标无法区分这两类能力来源；识别出"可辨识性缺口"和"叙事漂移"两个核心问题；提出通过引入受控变化的评估设计来分别测量语义泛化和物理泛化。

Result: 当前基准性能的提升与多种竞争性解释一致（包括语义匹配、分布重叠和真正的物理泛化），因此无法直接证明VLM的表征传递了物理执行能力。

Conclusion: 需要设计能够因果归因的评估方案，将VLM作为语义接口而非物理能力的隐式来源来检验其对机器人操控的贡献。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) systems, built on pretrained vision-language models (VLMs), have shown rapidly improving performance on robot manipulation benchmarks. These gains are commonly interpreted as evidence that semantic representations learned from internet-scale data transfer to physical execution generalization. This position paper argues that the assumption underlying this interpretation -- that semantic generalization is sufficient to support physical action decisions -- has not been independently verified and cannot be tested under current evaluation protocols. We support this claim by decomposing VLA policies into semantic mapping and physical action decision, and showing that task success rate -- the dominant evaluation metric -- cannot distinguish between these two sources of capability. As a result, improvements in benchmark performance are consistent with multiple competing explanations, including semantic matching, distributional overlap, and genuine physical generalization. We further argue that this identifiability gap has been reinforced through narrative drift, whereby successive systems inherit and strengthen prior interpretations of performance gains without isolating the underlying causal mechanism. To address this limitation, we propose a research direction based on evaluation designs that introduce controlled variation to separately measure semantic and physical generalization. Such designs make it possible to causally attribute performance without requiring access to model internals, and to empirically assess the role of VLM backbones as semantic interfaces rather than implicit sources of physical competence. Our goal is not to refute the role of VLMs in robotics, but to clarify the conditions under which claims of physical generalization can be meaningfully evaluated.

</details>


### [49] [DSIP: A Dynamic Coordination Planner for Signal-Free Intersections using Diffusion-Model-Based Multi-Agent Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30694)
*Qian Hu,Haoyang Peng,Songan Zhang,Ming Yang,Hongtei Eric Tseng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DSIP框架，利用扩散模型实现无信号交叉口的多车连续轨迹优化，在SUMO仿真中相比传统信号灯和强化学习方法显著降低了延误并提高了平均速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统信号控制导致频繁启停，造成延误和效率低下，尤其在交通需求高时更为严重。CAV的兴起使得轨迹级协同成为超越相位管理的潜在策略，本文旨在探索扩散模型驱动的无信号交叉口协同规划的可行性及其理论上限性能。

Method: 提出DSIP——基于扩散模型的无信号交叉口规划器，将交叉口管理从离散时间相位调度转变为连续的多车轨迹优化，并通过理想化通信与执行条件下的仿真评估其理论性能上限。

Result: 在SUMO平台多种四向交叉口配置下，DSIP相比固定配时信号控制和强化学习方法，在中高密度交通场景下显著降低了平均延误并保持了更高的平均速度。

Conclusion: 基于扩散的轨迹规划为未来自主交叉口管理提供了可扩展且稳健的基础，通过软件定义的协同可释放交叉口潜在通行能力，提供了一种无需基础设施扩建即可提升城市交通流效率的低成本路径。

Abstract: Traffic signal control at urban intersections inherently introduces stop-and-go behavior, resulting in increased delays and reduced traffic efficiency, especially under high traffic demand. With the emergence of connected and automated vehicles (CAVs), trajectory-level coordination has emerged as a high-potential strategy to augment or transcend conventional phase-based management. This paper proposes DSIP (Diffusion-model-based Signal-free Intersection Planner), a multi-agent motion planning framework driven by a generative diffusion process. DSIP shifts the intersection management paradigm from discrete temporal phasing to continuous multi-vehicle trajectory optimization. This work evaluates the theoretical upper-bound performance of this coordination strategy under idealized communication and execution conditions to isolate the core benefits of the diffusion-driven approach. Using the SUMO platform, we evaluate DSIP across diverse four-leg intersection configurations. Experimental results demonstrate that DSIP significantly reduces average delay and maintains higher average speed compared to both fixed-time signal control and state-of-the-art reinforcement-learning-based controllers, particularly in medium- to high-density traffic. These findings suggest that diffusion-based trajectory planning provides a scalable and robust foundation for future autonomous intersection management. By unlocking latent intersection capacity through software-defined coordination, this approach offers a cost-effective pathway to improve urban traffic flow efficiency without requiring physical infrastructure expansion.

</details>


### [50] [ViTL: Temporal Logic-Guided Zero-Shot Natural Language Navigation via Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30696)
*Kaier Liang,Hengde Dai,Cristian-Ioan Vasile*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ViTL框架利用大语言模型将自然语言命令编译为线性时序逻辑(LTL)公式，再转换为确定性有限自动机(DFA)以协调多通道价值地图，并结合方向性评分机制，实现零样本长时域自然语言导航任务的完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本目标导航方法局限于单目标任务，无法处理包含时序和逻辑约束的多目标长时域自然语言指令（如"先清洁椅子或沙发，然后打开电视"）。

Method: 任务层面：LLM将命令编译为LTL公式，再转为DFA协调多通道价值地图并触发动态重规划。导航层面：在观测图像上标记前沿方向，从VLM提取每个方向的评分（方向性评分）。

Result: 在HM3D数据集上，ViTL实现了零样本长时域自然语言导航任务的时序约束完成；方向性评分相比基线提高了单目标导航的准确性和效率。

Conclusion: ViTL首次实现了零样本条件下含时序约束的多目标自然语言导航，验证了结合LLM的LTL编译与VLM方向性评分的有效性。

Abstract: Enabling robots to follow natural language commands to complete zero-shot long-horizon tasks remains challenging. It requires extracting implicit temporal and logical constraints from natural language commands and executing multiple sub-tasks accordingly. Recent zero-shot object navigation methods use vision-language models (VLMs) to guide frontier-based exploration in unknown environments, but they are limited to single-target tasks. Real-world commands such as "Clean either the chair or the couch, then turn on the tv." require navigating to multiple targets in a temporally constrained order, which no existing zero-shot system can handle. We present ViTL, a framework that addresses this gap at two levels. At the task level, we use a large language model (LLM) to compile natural language commands into Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) formulas, which are then converted into Deterministic Finite Automata~(DFA) that coordinate multi-channel value maps and trigger dynamic replanning when new objects are detected. At the navigation level, we introduce directional score: rather than producing a direction-agnostic value across the entire field of view, we label frontier directions on the observation image and extract per-direction scores from the VLM. Experiments on Habitat-Matterport 3D (HM3D) show that the full framework enables zero-shot long-horizon completion of natural language navigation tasks with temporal constraints, and that directional score improves single-target navigation accuracy and efficiency over the baseline.

</details>


### [51] [Vision-Language Procedural Reasoning for Context-Aware Reward Modeling of Robotic Endovascular Guidewire Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30698)
*Wentong Tian,Jiyuan Zhao,Tianliang Yao,Yuxiang Fan,Zhengyu Shi,Dong Liu,Peng Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种视觉-语言程序推理（VL-PR）框架，通过多模态大语言模型（MLLM）进行程序推理，动态调整奖励函数权重，实现自主导丝导航，在物理机器人平台上展现出优于静态奖励方法的任务可靠性和导航效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主导丝导航方法依赖静态奖励函数，缺乏对解剖学上下文和任务进度的显式程序推理，难以应对复杂血管解剖中的目标冲突和阶段转换。

Method: 提出VL-PR框架，利用多模态大语言模型（MLLM）作为程序推理模块，解释实时视觉观测以推断高层导航上下文，动态调整不同导航阶段奖励分量的重要性，实现上下文感知的奖励自适应。

Result: 在物理机器人平台上跨多种血管场景的实验表明，该方法相比静态奖励方法具有更高的任务可靠性和更优的导航效率。

Conclusion: VL-PR框架通过程序推理驱动的奖励自适应，解决了多目标竞争和阶段转换问题，为复杂多任务机器人血管内手术提供了一种可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Robotic-assisted endovascular interventions demand accurate, stable, and context-aware guidewire navigation in complex and patient-specific vascular anatomies. Despite recent advances in robotic precision and learning-based control, existing autonomous navigation methods remain limited by their reliance on static reward functions and the lack of explicit procedural reasoning regarding anatomical context and task progression. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a vision-language procedural reasoning (VL-PR) framework for autonomous guidewire navigation. The framework integrates a multimodal large language model (MLLM) as a procedural reasoning module that interprets real-time visual observations to infer high-level navigation contexts. Instead of generating low-level control commands, the inferred procedural insights enable context-aware reward adaptation by dynamically adjusting the importance of reward components across different navigation phases. This approach allows a single policy to resolve competing objectives and handle complex transitions while preserving a consistent global task goal. Experiments on a physical robotic platform across diverse vascular scenarios demonstrate enhanced task reliability and streamlined navigational efficiency, highlighting the advantages over static-reward methods and offering a scalable solution for complex and multi-task robotic endovascular procedures.

</details>


### [52] [From Grasps to Dexterity: Large-Scale Grasp Pretraining for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30749)
*Ying Yuan,Xinyu Liu,Sriram Krishna,David Held*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究大规模灵巧抓取数据集能否支持铰接工具使用中的功能性灵巧操作，提出分层模仿学习框架，利用355k轨迹的抓取预训练数据集预训练底层控制器，再微调于下游任务。在仿真基准DexCraft和真实实验中，该方法优于端到端扩散策略基线，真实环境全任务成功率比DP3高33.3个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模灵巧抓取数据集编码了丰富的手-物交互先验知识，但以往研究局限于抓取生成和抓取放置操作。本文旨在探索这类数据能否支撑铰接工具使用中的功能性灵巧操作，即机器人需抓取工具、保持接触并操作其活动部件。

Method: 采用分层模仿学习框架，包含高层手部子目标预测器和低层目标条件控制器。从大规模灵巧抓取注释构建355k轨迹的预训练数据集，用于预训练低层控制器，再在下游任务演示上进行微调。同时构建DexCraft仿真基准，包含6个需要协调手指运动的铰接工具使用任务。

Result: 在仿真和真实实验中，该方法优于端到端扩散策略基线（如DP3）和从零训练的分层策略。真实环境中全任务成功率比DP3高33.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 抓取数据集不仅可以用于抓取合成，还可以作为接触丰富灵巧操作的可扩展预训练数据。

Abstract: Large-scale dexterous grasp datasets encode rich priors over hand-object interaction, but their use has largely been confined to grasp generation and pick-and-place manipulation. We study whether such data can instead support functional dexterity in articulated tool use, where a robot must acquire a tool, maintain contact, and operate its functional moving parts. We adapt a hierarchical imitation learning framework that combines high-level hand sub-goal prediction with a low-level goal-conditioned controller. We construct a 355k-trajectory grasp-pretraining dataset from large-scale dexterous grasp annotations and use it to pretrain the low-level controller. The controller is then fine-tuned on downstream task demonstrations. To evaluate this setting, we introduce DexCraft, a simulation benchmark with six articulated tool-use tasks requiring coordinated finger motion. Across simulation and real-world experiments, our approach outperforms end-to-end diffusion policy baselines and hierarchical policies trained from scratch. In the real world, it improves full-task success by 33.3 percentage points over DP3. These results show that grasp datasets can serve not only as resources for grasp synthesis, but also as scalable pretraining data for contact-rich dexterous manipulation. Videos are shown on https://yingyuan0414.github.io/grasp2dexterity/ .

</details>


### [53] [Wind and State Estimation on SE(3): Comparative Evaluation of EKF and UKF with Continuous and Discrete Quadrotor Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30804)
*Hiranya Udagedara,Adam Bigsby,Mahdis Bisheban*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文评估了在SE(3)上使用离散和连续四旋翼动力学方程进行风速估计的性能，发现结合离散SE(3)动力学与UKF的方法即使在低成本传感器下也能实现更高的估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型的风速估计方法因仅依赖机载传感器而被广泛使用，但四旋翼无人机的高度非线性特性使该任务具有挑战性。现有研究大多采用连续或离散化形式，缺乏对SE(3)上离散与连续动力学方程的系统评估。

Method: 使用基于离散拉格朗日量构建的李群变分积分器作为离散模型（无需近似或离散化），分别评估SE(3)上四旋翼动力学的离散形式和连续形式，结合扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）和无迹卡尔曼滤波（UKF）进行风速估计，并通过MATLAB数值仿真（悬停和轨迹跟踪）及室外自由飞行实验验证性能。

Result: 离散SE(3)动力学与UKF的组合在悬停和轨迹跟踪飞行中均实现了更高的风速估计精度，同时保持良好的轨迹跟踪性能，且对低成本传感器具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 离散四旋翼模型结合UKF不仅适用于风速估计，还可推广到其他高精度任务，即使在依赖低成本机载传感器的条件下也能取得优异性能。

Abstract: Use of quadrotor UAVs for wind velocity estimation is gaining popularity in recent studies, leveraging their maneuverability, compact size and low cost. Among available approaches, model-based wind velocity estimation is most commonly used, since it relies only on onboard sensors. However, as the quadrotor is a highly nonlinear system, thus making this task challenging. This study evaluate the use of both discrete and continuous dynamic equations of the quadrotor UAV for wind velocity estimation on SE(3), rather than commonly adapted continuous or discretized form. Lie Group Variational Integrator, developed on discrete Lagrangian is used as the discrete model without any approximation or discritization. The study assess both the discrete and continuous form of the quadrotor dynamics on SE(3) using Extended Kalman filter (EKF), and Unscented Kalman filter (UKF). The quadrotor UAV performance is evaluated in both MATLAB-based numerical simulations and free outdoor flight. The numerical simulations are conducted during both hovering and trajectory-tracking flights. Results demonstrate that, by using discrete SE(3) dynamics coupled with UKF, the quadrotor achieves higher estimation accuracy while maintaining trajectory tracking, even with low-cost sensors. These findings highlight the potential of discrete quadrotor models with UKF not only for wind velocity estimation but also for other high-accuracy tasks, even when relying on low-cost onboard sensors.

</details>


### [54] [Off the Rails: Hijacking the Scoring Head in Generative End-to-End Driving Planners with Safety-Violating Adversarial Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30807)
*Halima Bouzidi,Mboutidem Ekemini Mkpong,Haoyu Liu,Mohammad Abdullah Al Faruque*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Derail框架，通过攻击生成式端到端自动驾驶规划器的评分头（scoring head），将轨迹选择从安全候选翻转为不安全候选，评分下降39-80%，碰撞率高达50%，并揭示评分头推理模式是值得防御的通用攻击面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式自动驾驶规划器（扩散去噪、词汇检索等）虽架构多样，但共享共性推理模式：固定候选轨迹由基于BEV特征的评分头打分，最高分者被选中。评分头是感知与运动指令间的唯一屏障，且候选间的决策边界很小，这一攻击面尚未被充分研究。

Method: 提出Derail对抗攻击框架，专门针对生成式规划器的评分头进行攻击，通过对抗扰动翻转轨迹选择决策。在多种生成式规划器上进行评估，并与通用的损失最大化攻击和特征散度攻击进行对比。

Result: Derail在多种生成式规划器上均使评分下降39-80%，碰撞率最高达50%，持续优于对比的通用攻击方法。安全违反目标（safety-violating objectives）是决定攻击有效性的关键因素。

Conclusion: 生成式AD规划器的评分头推断模式本身是一个值得明确防御考虑的重复出现的攻击面；安全违反目标主导了攻击有效性。

Abstract: Generative models have recently seen rapid adoption in End-to-End (E2E) autonomous driving (AD), with diffusion-based denoising and vocabulary-based retrieval becoming the dominant trajectory-decoding paradigms. Despite their architectural diversity, current generative AD planners share a common inference pattern: a fixed set of candidate trajectories (anchors, vocabulary entries, or proposal queries) is scored by one or more learned heads conditioned on the Bird's-Eye-View (BEV) features, and the highest-scored candidate is returned as the final trajectory. Under this design, the scoring head is the only barrier between perception and the motion command, and its decision margins between competing candidates are often small. We introduce \textsc{Derail}, an adversarial framework that exploits this scoring-head attack surface. Evaluated on various generative planners, \textsc{Derail} flips the trajectory selection from a safe to an unsafe candidate, with score drops of $39$--$80\%$ and collision rates of up to $50\%$, consistently outperforming generic loss-maximization and feature-divergence attacks. Our analysis suggests that safety-violating objectives govern attack effectiveness against generative AD planners, and that the scoring-head inference pattern itself is a recurring attack surface worth explicit defensive consideration.

</details>


### [55] [TAPE: Tether-Aware Path Planning for Autonomous Exploration of Unknown 3D Cavities Using a Tangle-Compatible Tethered Aerial Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30817)
*Louis Petit,Alexis Lussier Desbiens*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出首个针对三维未知空腔自主探索的方法TAPE，采用双层分层架构，通过全局前沿规划求解TSP最小化距离，局部规划通过可调决策函数平衡路径成本与缆绳长度。仿真与实地测试表明，相比无局部规划器的TSP方案，该方法距离仅增加4.1%，但缆绳长度始终未超限（100% vs 53%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有三维未知空腔探索方法未考虑缆绳长度最小化问题，而缆绳缠绕主要受局部路径影响，因此需要一种能同时最小化行驶距离和缆绳展开长度的自主探索方法。

Method: 提出TAPE方法，采用双层分层架构：全局层基于前沿规划求解TSP以最小化行驶距离；局部层通过可调决策函数在路径代价和缆绳长度之间进行权衡，从而减少缆绳展开长度。

Result: 仿真中，相比无局部规划器的TSP方案，TAPE方法行驶距离仅增加4.1%，但缆绳长度始终低于最大允许值（100%达标），而基线方法仅53%达标。

Conclusion: TAPE方法能在三维未知空腔探索中有效平衡行驶距离与缆绳长度，在牺牲极小行驶距离的前提下显著提升缆绳管理的可靠性，适用于实际勘探场景。

Abstract: This letter presents the first method for autonomous exploration of unknown cavities in three dimensions (3D) that focuses on minimizing the distance traveled and the length of tether unwound. Considering that the tether entanglements are little influenced by the global path, our approach employs a 2-level hierarchical architecture. The global frontier-based planning solves a Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP) to minimize the distance. The local planning attempts to minimize the path cost and the tether length using an adjustable decision function whose parameters play on the trade-off between these two values. The proposed method, TAPE, is evaluated through detailed simulation studies as well as field tests. On average, our method generates a 4.1% increase in distance traveled compared to the TSP solution without our local planner, with which the length of the tether remains below the maximum allowed value in 53% of the simulated cases against 100% with our method.

</details>


### [56] [Robustness-Based Synthesis for Time Window Temporal Logic Specifications via Mixed-Integer Linear Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30820)
*Philip Smith,Ahmad Ahmad,Kevin Leahy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对离散时间线性系统在TWTL任务规范下的控制综合问题，提出了基于混合整数线性规划（MILP）的开环和闭环（MPC）两种求解框架，并通过鲁棒度最大化保证规范满足性，其中闭环MPC利用任务自适应时域降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TWTL是一种能够紧凑表达含显式时序约束的序列任务的规范语言，但已有的综合方法在处理TWTL规范时面临挑战。本文旨在为离散时间线性系统提供一种能够保证规范满足性且具有计算效率的综合方法。

Method: 基于TWTL的定量语义（鲁棒度），将TWTL公式的鲁棒满足编码为混合整数线性约束；构建MILP最大化鲁棒度；提出开环（优化完整控制序列）和闭环（基于MPC的滚动时域优化）两种综合框架；闭环MPC利用TWTL的确定性有限自动机（DFA）识别当前活跃子任务，实现任务自适应时域，仅预测当前任务的剩余窗口而非整个公式时域。

Result: 证明了任意具有正目标值的可行解都能保证规范的布尔满足性。闭环MPC的任务自适应时域策略显著降低了每次重新求解的计算成本，使其远低于初始开环求解。

Conclusion: 本文提出的MILP综合方法能够有效处理TWTL规范下的线性系统控制问题，任务自适应时域MPC在保证规范满足性的同时大幅提升了计算效率，为TWTL在实时控制场景中的应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Time Window Temporal Logic (TWTL) is a rich specification language for cyber-physical systems that can compactly express sequential tasks with explicit timing constraints. In this paper, we consider the problem of synthesizing control inputs for discrete-time linear systems subject to TWTL task specifications. Building on the quantitative semantics (robustness) recently introduced for TWTL in [1], we encode the robust satisfaction of a TWTL formula as a set of Mixed-Integer Linear constraints and pose synthesis as a Mixed Integer Linear Program (MILP) that maximizes the robustness degree. We prove that any feasible solution with positive objective value guarantees Boolean satisfaction of the specification. We address two synthesis settings: an \emph{open-loop} formulation that optimizes the full control sequence from the initial state, and a \emph{closed-loop} receding-horizon Model Predictive Controller (MPC) formulation that re-solves the MILP at each step using the current measured state. A key feature of our MPC formulation is a \emph{task-adaptive horizon} that exploits the TWTL Deterministic Finite Automaton (DFA) to determine the active sub-task at each step, limiting the prediction horizon to the remaining window of the current task rather than the full formula horizon, this makes each re-solve significantly cheaper than the initial open-loop solve.

</details>


### [57] [Sampling-Based Coordination-Informed Multi-Objective Multi-Robot Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30893)
*Antonio Marino,Esteban Restrepo,Soon-jo Chung,Paolo Robuffo Giordano,Claudio Pacchierotti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了CIMORL框架，通过分布式权重预测、特权专家训练和理论保证，在分布式约束下实现多机器人系统的帕累托最优协调，在合作与对抗场景中取得21.2%超体积提升，并通过Crazyflie无人机实验验证了鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体强化学习方法依赖固定或中心化协调机制，限制了适应性且违反分布式约束，无法同时优化多机器人系统中相互竞争的目标并保持协调行为。

Method: 提出CIMORL框架，包含分布式权重预测机制、特权专家训练策略（利用全局信息）以及帕累托最优解的理论保证；提出CIMORL-TS（树搜索）和CIMORL-MPPI两种基于采样的变体，在训练阶段利用特权全局信息，实现完全去中心化的部署。

Result: 在合作与对抗场景中，相较基线方法实现21.2%超体积提升和更优的策略稳定性；在Crazyflie无人机上完成资源分配和多攻击者多防御者部分可观测场景的实物验证。

Conclusion: CIMORL框架在分布式约束下有效实现了多机器人系统的多目标协调优化，理论与实验共同验证了其在帕累托最优性、策略稳定性和实际部署鲁棒性方面的优势。

Abstract: Multi-robot systems must simultaneously optimize competing objectives while maintaining coordinated behavior. Existing multi-agent reinforcement learning approaches often rely on fixed or centralized coordination, which limits adaptability and violates distributed constraints. This work introduces the Coordination-Informed Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning (CIMORL) framework, integrating a distributed weight prediction mechanism, a privileged expert training strategy, and theoretical guarantees for Pareto-optimal solutions. We present the base CIMORL method alongside two sampling-based variants, CIMORL-TS (Tree Search) and CIMORL-MPPI (MPPI), which leverage privileged global information during training to enable fully decentralized deployment. Experimental validation in cooperative and adversarial scenarios demonstrates a $21.2\%$ hypervolume improvement and superior policy stability compared to state-of-the-art baselines. Real-world experiments with Crazyflie drones further validate the framework's robustness in resource allocation and multi-attacker multi-defend scenarios under partial observability.

</details>


### [58] [The Quadruped Soft Tail: Compliant Grasping and Swabbing for Contamination Surveys in Harsh Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30900)
*Harald Minde Hansen,Nandita Gallacher,Kristin Y. Pettersen,Jan Tommy Gravdahl,Mario di Castro*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种安装在四足机器人上的轻量、柔软、柔顺的肌腱驱动尾巴系统，配备中空柔性脊柱和肌腱驱动软体夹爪，用于在放射性区域（如CERN）进行铍污染调查。该系统结合了运动灵活性与软体操作柔顺性，通过运动学模型和任务空间控制器实现远程遥操作，完成擦拭采样、夹取和释放样本的全流程，避免了人员暴露风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射性区域（如CERN）的铍污染调查环境中充满电缆和电子设备，传统刚性机械臂不适合在杂乱、狭窄空间中操作，而现有机器人系统难以同时兼顾灵活运动与安全采样，因此需要一种兼具柔顺性和灵巧操作能力的机器人方案。

Method: 设计了一种肌腱驱动的软体尾巴，包含中空柔性脊柱和肌腱驱动软体夹爪；开发了封闭形式运动学模型和奇异性鲁棒的任务空间控制器，用于直观遥操作；通过实验验证夹爪驱动对形态影响极小，共模肌腱驱动可有效调节刚度和预紧力。

Result: 夹爪驱动对尾巴形状的影响可忽略不计；共模肌腱驱动能有效实现刚度调节和预载控制；所提运动学模型为实时任务空间控制提供了合适基础。

Conclusion: 该系统将四足机器人的敏捷运动与软体机器人的柔顺操作相结合，能够在杂乱、受限或危险环境中完成完整的污染调查流程，无需人员暴露。该软体尾巴概念不仅适用于CERN的铍污染调查，还可推广至其他腿式机器人在类似环境中的应用。

Abstract: Beryllium contamination surveys in radioactive areas are challenging for robots in environments cluttered with cables and electronics. To address this problem, we have developed a novel quadruped system augmentation: A lightweight, soft, and compliant tendon-actuated robotic tail mounted on a quadruped robot. The tail features a hollow, flexible backbone and a tendon-actuated soft gripper that enables the robot to pick up sampling tissues, swab contaminated surfaces, and release the tissues at designated collection locations for subsequent beryllium analysis. To enable intuitive teleoperation, a closed-form kinematic model and a singularity-robust task-space controller are developed. Experimental results demonstrate that gripper actuation has a negligible effect on robot shape, while common-mode tendon actuation provides an effective mechanism for stiffness modulation and preload control. Furthermore, experimental validation indicates that the proposed kinematic model provides a suitable basis for real-time task-space control. The proposed system combines the agility of legged locomotion with the compliance of soft robotic manipulation, enabling the complete contamination-survey procedure to be performed without human exposure. While motivated by beryllium contamination surveys at CERN, the proposed quadruped soft-tail concept is broadly applicable to legged robots operating in cluttered, confined, or hazardous environments where conventional rigid-link manipulators are undesirable.

</details>


### [59] [Motion Planning in Compressed Representation Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30940)
*Lukas Lao Beyer,Sertac Karaman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种将深度学习与基于模型的规划统一起来的生成式框架，通过学习高压缩率的离散层次化潜空间自编码器，直接在潜空间中进行搜索实现运动规划，在nuPlan和Waymo数据集上取得了强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习方法在机器人运动规划中展现出强大能力，但基于搜索或优化的模型规划算法因灵活性、效率和领域知识整合能力仍是重要工具。现有两种范式缺乏统一框架，作者希望将二者优势结合。

Method: 首先学习一个具有高压缩率和层次化离散值令牌潜空间的自编码器；然后利用该自编码器的降维和层次化粗到细结构，直接在潜空间中对令牌进行搜索来实现运动规划；该搜索可以优化测试时指定的任意目标函数。

Result: 在nuPlan和Waymo Open Motion Dataset上评估，展示了潜空间搜索可用于多种引导行为生成任务，在闭环运动规划和多智能体引导场景合成中取得了强性能，且无需任何任务特定训练。

Conclusion: 该方法成功统一了深度学习和模型规划两种范式，在保持灵活性和效率的同时，通过高压缩自编码器的生成能力产生真实解，为运动规划提供了一种无需任务特定训练即可优化任意目标函数的新途径。

Abstract: Deep learning methods have vastly expanded the capabilities of motion planning in robotics applications, as learning priors from large-scale data has been shown to be essential in capturing the highly complex behavior required for solving tasks such as manipulation or navigation for autonomous vehicles. At the same time, model-based planning algorithms based on search or optimization remain an essential tool due to their flexibility, efficiency, and the ability to incorporate domain knowledge via expert-designed algorithms and objective functions. We propose a new generative framework to unify these two paradigms. First, we learn an autoencoder with a high compression ratio and a latent space of hierarchically ordered, discrete-valued tokens. Leveraging both the dimensionality reduction and the hierarchical coarse-to-fine structure learned by this autoencoder, we then perform motion planning by directly searching in the latent space of tokens. This search can optimize arbitrary objective functions specified at test time, providing a large degree of flexibility while maintaining efficiency and producing realistic solutions by relying on the generative capabilities of the highly compressed autoencoder. We evaluate our method on nuPlan and the Waymo Open Motion Dataset, showing how latent space search can be used for a variety of guided behavior generation tasks, achieving strong performance for closed-loop motion planning and multi-agent guided scenario synthesis without requiring any task-specific training.

</details>


### [60] [Multisensory Continual Learning: Adapting Pretrained Visuomotor Policies to Force](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30988)
*Jaden Clark,Changhao Wang,Yihuai Gao,Seongheon Hong,Hojung Choi,Mark Cutkosky,Yifan Hou,Shuran Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MuSe方法，通过多阶段融合、多感官未来预测和预训练数据经验回放，将有限的多感官数据融入预训练的纯视觉策略中，实现机器人多感官持续学习。实验表明，该方法在接触式操作任务上表现优异，同时保持甚至提升了原始任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作中力觉、触觉、听觉等感官反馈对接触式任务至关重要，但这些模态具有硬件和任务特异性，大规模多感官数据集稀缺，导致无法为每种传感器组合预训练策略。因此需要研究如何让预训练的视觉策略持续学习新模态。

Method: 提出MuSe框架，包含三个关键组件：(1) 多阶段融合（multi-stage fusion）将多感官信息融入视觉策略；(2) 多感官未来预测（multisensory future prediction）作为辅助任务；(3) 预训练数据经验回放（experience replay）防止灾难性遗忘。具体以纯视觉策略为基础，引入力-扭矩传感进行实例化。

Result: 在真实世界操作任务上，MuSe在接触式微调任务中表现优异，同时保持甚至提升了原始预训练任务的性能，表明适度规模的多感官数据集可以提升机器人超出微调分布的通用能力。

Conclusion: 多感官持续学习是可行的，有限的多感官数据通过适当方法融入预训练视觉策略，不仅能适应新任务，还能泛化提升原始任务表现，为机器人多模态学习提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Robot manipulation often relies on sensory feedback beyond vision, particularly in contact-rich settings where force, tactile, or audio signals reveal interaction states that are not directly observable from images. However, these modalities are often hardware- and task-specific, and large-scale multisensory robot datasets remain scarce. As a result, it is impractical to pretrain policies with every sensor they may encounter. We study multisensory continual learning: adapting a pretrained robot policy to new tasks with newly introduced modalities while preserving performance under the original sensor suite. We propose MuSe, which incorporates limited multisensory data into pretrained vision-only policies through multi-stage fusion, multisensory future prediction, and experience replay over pretraining data. We instantiate MuSe by augmenting a pretrained vision-only policy with force-torque sensing and evaluate it on real-world manipulation tasks. Our experiments show that MuSe performs strongly on contact-rich finetuning tasks while preserving, and in some cases improving, performance on the original pretraining tasks. These results suggest that a modest multisensory dataset can improve general robot capabilities beyond the finetuning distribution. Project website: https://jadenvc.github.io/multisensory-continual-learning/

</details>


### [61] [Ground Plane-Aided Extrinsic Calibration of Inertial and RGB-D Sensors for Uncrewed Aerial Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31019)
*Ilyar Asl Sabbaghian Hokmabadi,Mahdis Bisheban*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对无人机IMU和RGB-D相机的无靶标外参标定方法，利用深度学习地面分割提取地平面法向量，结合加速度计重力向量进行鲁棒估计，性能优于MATLAB工具箱，与Kalibr相当且无需棋盘格靶标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IMU-相机外参标定方法通常依赖专用设备、平面靶标以及初始参数估计，操作复杂且受限，需要一种无需靶标、简便易行的标定方法。

Method: 利用深度学习进行地面分割，从RGB-D深度通道提取地面点云并估算地平面法向量；将地平面法向量与加速度计传感的重力向量对齐，通过鲁棒估计方法求解外参。

Result: 所提方法优于MATLAB工具箱，与Kalibr性能相近，且无需棋盘格等专用靶标。

Conclusion: 无靶标、基于深度学习地面分割的鲁棒外参标定方法可有效替代传统依赖靶标的标定方案，适用于无人机IMU与RGB-D相机系统。

Abstract: Accurate extrinsic calibration of inertial sensors, such as Inertial Measurement Units (IMUs) and cameras is crucial for trajectory estimation of Uncrewed Aerial Vehicles (UAVs). While numerous calibration methods have been proposed, these techniques often rely on specialized equipment, planar targets, and an initial estimate of the calibration parameters.
  In this research, we propose a targetless calibration method designed for UAVs equipped with IMUs and RGB-Depth (RGB-D) cameras. Our approach leverages deep-learning-based floor-segmentation to extract ground points from the depth channel of RGB-D images. Subsequently, the normal vector to these points is estimated. The known orientation of the normal to the floor segment and the gravity vector sensed in the accelerometer's frame are utilized in a robust estimation approach to estimate the extrinsic calibration parameters. We illustrate that the developed method outperforms MATLAB's Toolboxes and exhibits similar performance to Kalibr without the use of specialized checkerboard targets.

</details>


### [62] [Labimus: A Simulation and Benchmark for Humanoid Dexterous Manipulation in Chemical Laboratory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31037)
*Yuhan Wu,Zhao Jin,Tao Li,Yuheng Zhang,Zhengping Che,Jian Tang,Zhichao Wang,Shuo Wang,Jun Jiang,Xiaobo Li,Yanyong Zhang,Yan Xia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Labimus是首个面向有机化学实验室的人形灵巧操作基准，通过真实到仿真的建模重建了30多个功能资产，定义了六种原子操作和七步固体称量流程，并引入精度感知评估协议。基准测试发现现有策略存在精度差距——完成任务但无法满足实验定量容差要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验室自动化在机器人平台和AI驱动科学推理方面取得了显著进展，但许多实验室操作（如固-固转移）本质上具有动态性，需要实时适应不同材料和实验条件。这些精度关键的操作难以标准化，促使使用具有灵巧手的人形机器人。然而，现有基准缺乏对人形机器人在精度关键的实验室环境中的操作评估。

Method: Labimus通过真实到仿真（real-to-sim）建模，从真实有机化学工作站重建了30多个功能保真资产；集成了铰接式实验室仪器、基于粒子的粉末物理和闭环仪器读数，实现了完整的操作到测量管线；定义了六种原子操作和基于真实标准操作程序的七步固体称量工作流；引入精度感知评估协议，联合度量任务完成度、实验精度和长时间执行效果。

Result: 对三种代表性策略在程序化布局和环境扰动下的基准测试表明，存在精度差距：能够成功完成实验室任务的策略仍无法满足实验协议要求的定量容差。

Conclusion: Labimus基准揭示了任务完成与实验有效性之间的根本脱节，为开发用于科学实验室的可靠人形机器人提供了新的测试平台。

Abstract: Laboratory automation has made remarkable progress through robotic platforms and AI-driven scientific reasoning. However, many laboratory operations (e.g., solid--solid transfer) remain inherently dynamic and require real-time adaptation to different materials and experimental conditions. Such precision-critical manipulations are difficult to standardize, motivating the use of humanoid robots with dexterous hands. Despite this opportunity, no existing benchmark evaluates humanoid manipulation in precision-critical laboratory environments. We present Labimus, to our knowledge, the first benchmark for humanoid dexterous manipulation in organic chemistry laboratories. Labimus reconstructs over 30 functionally faithful assets from real organic chemistry workstations through real-to-sim modeling, collectively covering the core operations of routine organic chemistry experiments. The benchmark integrates articulated laboratory instruments, particle-based powder physics, and closed-loop instrument readouts, enabling a complete manipulation-to-measurement pipeline. It further defines six atomic operations and a seven-step solid-weighing workflow derived from real laboratory standard operating procedures. We introduce a precision-aware evaluation protocol designed to jointly measure task completion, experimental precision, and long-horizon execution. We benchmark three representative policies under procedural layouts and environmental perturbations. Results reveal a precision gap: policies that successfully complete laboratory tasks can still fail to satisfy the quantitative tolerances required by experimental protocols. Our benchmark exposes a fundamental disconnect between task completion and experimental validity, providing a new testbed for developing reliable humanoid robots for scientific laboratories.

</details>


### [63] [Efficient Sim-to-Real Transfer of World-Action Models from Synthetic Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31101)
*Zixing Wang,Kausik Sivakumar,Jinghuan Shang,Yafei Hu,Zhaoming Xie,Ran Gong,Xiaohan Zhang,Karl Schmeckpeper*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次实现了世界动作模型从仿真到真实机器人操作的零样本迁移。基于Cosmos Policy视频扩散模型，结合AnyTask运动规划流水线生成的合成数据和广泛域随机化，在Franka机器人上完成物体抓取、抽屉打开和拾取放置任务，平均成功率35%，无需任何真实演示数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缩小仿真到真实的差距是部署学习型操作策略的核心挑战。仿真学习可以替代昂贵的真实机器人演示，但世界动作模型此前从未被证明能从仿真迁移到真实机器人操作。

Method: 基于Cosmos Policy（视频扩散模型适配视觉运动控制），构建具有广泛域随机化的仿真环境，使用AnyTask运动规划流水线生成每个任务约800个合成演示，零样本部署于Franka机器人。

Result: 在物体抓取、抽屉打开和拾取放置任务上零样本部署，平均成功率达到35%。

Conclusion: 这是首次成功实现世界动作模型从仿真到真实机器人操作的迁移，证明了仅靠合成数据预训练的世界动作模型可以在真实世界中零样本部署。

Abstract: Bridging the sim-to-real gap is a core challenge in deploying learned manipulation policies. Sim-to-real learning is attractive because it can replace expensive real robot demonstrations with scalable synthetic data, yet world-action models have not previously been shown to transfer from simulation to real robotic manipulation. We study whether a world-action model can be trained from synthetic priors and deployed zero-shot in the real world. To this end, we build upon Cosmos Policy, a video diffusion model adapted for visuomotor control. We construct simulation environments with extensive domain randomization and generate demonstrations using the AnyTask motion planning pipeline. We evaluate our approach across object lifting, drawer opening, and pick-and-place tasks using ${\sim}800$ synthetic demonstrations per task and no real demonstrations. When deployed zero-shot on a Franka Robot, our policy attains a 35\% average success rate. To our knowledge, this represents the first successful sim-to-real transfer of a world-action model for robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [64] [What Probing Reveals about Autonomous Driving: Linking Internal Prediction Errors to Ego Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31106)
*Hyeonchang Jeon,Kyungbeom Kim,Eugene Vinitsky,Kyung-Joong Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出线性探针和定向扰动方法，系统评估驾驶策略（行为克隆和强化学习）中的预测和规划能力，发现即使闭环表现良好，策略在近碰撞事件中仍缺乏及时的环境车辆预测，且纠正错误预测可改善自车规划安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶策略在名义场景下表现安全鲁棒，但闭环评分无法揭示策略是否真正具备车辆运动预测和轨迹规划能力，还是仅依赖脆弱的启发式方法，因此需要系统方法探测策略的真正能力边界。

Method: 使用线性探针（linear probing）和定向扰动（targeted perturbations），在行为克隆和强化学习模型中，按规模尺度追踪预测和规划内部信号的涌现、平台或失败。通过因果干预验证纠正错误预测对自车规划的影响。

Result: 尽管闭环性能良好，策略在近碰撞事件中未能形成及时的环境车辆预测，限制了自车规划可用的预测信号；因果干预表明纠正错误预测可引导自车规划向更安全轨迹改进。

Conclusion: 闭环性能不能可靠反映策略的真实预测和规划能力，线性探针和扰动是有效的诊断工具，预测能力的缺失是安全驾驶的关键瓶颈，纠正预测错误有助于提升规划安全性。

Abstract: Large-scale datasets and fast simulators have enabled improvements in driving policies that appear safe and robust, yet strong performance in nominal scenarios can still mask flawed reasoning and unsafe heuristics. Summary scores from closed-loop simulators do not give significant insight into the policy, making it difficult to determine whether they truly predict the motion of surrounding vehicles, how the ego vehicle generates future plans, or whether they merely rely on brittle heuristics that happen to succeed in nominal scenarios. To better understand the limits and weaknesses of driving policies, we focus on probing for forms of prediction, i.e., where surrounding vehicles will move next, and planning, i.e., understanding how to generate safe trajectories. We focus on these two capabilities because they reflect behaviors expected of effective driving policies, and use their presence or absence to assess policy quality across data-driven behavior cloning and simulation-driven reinforcement learning policies. To evaluate the presence of these capabilities, we investigate them as a function of scale, asking whether the closed-loop gains from larger datasets and longer simulation training reflect stronger prediction and planning or merely better behavioral heuristics. We use linear probing and targeted perturbations in both imitation learning and reinforcement learning models to track when these internal signals emerge, plateau, or fail. Despite good closed-loop performance, policies often fail to form timely surrounding-vehicle predictions during near-collision events, revealing a limitation in the predictive signals available for ego planning. Finally, causal intervention shows that correcting mistaken predictions improves ego planning toward safer trajectories.

</details>


### [65] [ELASTIC: Efficiently Learning to Adaptively Scale Test-Time Compute for Generative Control Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31132)
*Andrew Zou Li,Gokul Swamy,Yonatan Bisk,Andrea Bajcsy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ELASTIC是一种通过学习状态依赖的测试时计算调度策略，为生成式控制策略（如扩散策略和视觉-语言-动作模型）在序列细化步数和并行采样数之间进行自适应分配，在相同计算预算下显著提升任务成功率并降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式控制策略（GCPs）在机器人控制中支持测试时计算缩放，但序列缩放（细化动作）和并行缩放（多候选搜索）的最优分配是状态、任务和策略相关的，难以先验确定。例如，抓取早期需要并行探索，接近接触时则需要序列细化。现有方法缺乏自适应的计算调度机制。

Method: 将计算分配问题形式化为一个元马尔可夫决策过程（meta-MDP），其中元策略与冻结的预训练机器人策略交互，在每个去噪迭代中选择序列步数和并行采样数，以最大化任务成功同时最小化计算开销。元策略通过强化学习训练，无需访问GCP的训练数据。

Result: 在模拟操作基准测试中，ELASTIC在相同计算预算下帕累托支配固定和单轴缩放基线。在真实机器人操作任务中，使用π₀.₅视觉-语言-动作模型时，ELASTIC匹配了最佳10采样的成功率，同时将壁钟延迟降低了34%。

Conclusion: ELASTIC证明了通过学习状态相关的自适应计算调度，能够有效平衡生成式控制策略中序列细化与并行探索之间的计算分配，在不降低任务成功率的前提下显著提升计算效率，为机器人控制中的测试时缩放提供了新的优化维度。

Abstract: Generative control policies (GCPs), such as diffusion policies and flow-based vision-language-action models, enable test-time scaling in robot control. Test-time compute can be allocated along two axes: sequential scaling, which increases denoising steps to refine actions, and parallel scaling, which samples multiple candidate actions to search across modes of the policy distribution. However, the optimal allocation of sequential and parallel compute is hard to know a priori as it is state-, task-, and policy-dependent. For example, early stages of a grasp may benefit from broader parallel exploration, while near-contact phases may require more sequential refinement for precision. We present ELASTIC, an algorithm that learns state-dependent test-time compute schedules for GCPs. We formulate compute allocation as a meta-Markov Decision Process in which a meta-policy interacts with a frozen pretrained robot policy and selects sequential steps and parallel samples at each denoising iteration to maximize task success while minimizing compute. Using reinforcement learning, this meta-policy also learns adaptive compute schedules without access to the GCP's training data. Across simulated manipulation benchmarks with diffusion policies, ELASTIC Pareto-dominates fixed and single-axis scaling baselines at matched compute budgets. On real-world robot manipulation with the $π_{0.5}$ vision-language-action model, ELASTIC matches best-of-$10$ success while reducing wall-clock latency by 34%.

</details>


### [66] [A Modular Vision-Language-Action Robotics Framework for Indoor Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31144)
*Anindya Jana,Snehasis Banerjee,Arup Sadhu,Ranjan Dasgupta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于CMU视觉-语言-动作（VLA）挑战的集成系统，采用模块化架构，通过并行处理感知管道（基于OwlViT构建语义体素地图）和语言管道（使用VLM分类用户指令），将自然语言指令转化为自主代理的可执行动作，并在500秒探索限制下实现了语言到机器人动作的桥梁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在弥合人类自然语言与机器人自主动作之间的鸿沟，使自主代理能够基于自然语言指令执行复杂任务。

Method: 采用模块化架构，包含两条并行处理流：(1) 感知管道：利用OwlViT嵌入从实时摄像头数据构建语义体素地图，受500秒探索时间限制；(2) 语言管道：使用视觉-语言模型对用户指令进行分类。将分类后的查询与地图的几何和语义上下文结合，生成详细提示输入VLM，最终输出可执行动作。

Result: 系统能够在时间约束下（500秒探索限制）利用部分地图完成查询分类、语义定位与动作生成，展示了从语言到机器人动作的可行解决方案。

Conclusion: 该集成系统有效桥接了人类语言与机器人动作之间的差距，为VLA挑战提供了一种可行的模块化解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents an integrated system for the CMU Vision-Language-Action (VLA) Challenge, designed to enable an autonomous agent to perform complex tasks based on natural language instructions. Our framework employs a modular architecture that orchestrates environment mapping, question processing, and navigation. The system operates in two parallel streams: a perception pipeline that constructs a semantic voxel map from real-time camera feeds using OwlViT embeddings, and a language pipeline that classifies user commands with a Vision-Language Model. The mapping is time-constrained; the system proceeds with a partial map if a 500-second exploration limit is reached. The classified query is then grounded in the geometric and semantic context of the map to generate a detailed prompt for the VLM. This yields an actionable output, demonstrating a capable solution for bridging the gap between human language and robotic action.

</details>


### [67] [LLM-Powered Interactive Robotic Action Synthesis from Multimodal Speech, Gestures, and Music](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31158)
*Snehasis Banerjee,Ranjan Dasgupta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个利用大语言模型(LLM)融合语音、手势和音乐节拍等多模态人类输入来合成复杂机器人动作的框架，并在四足机器人上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人机交互依赖僵硬、预编程的命令，限制了机器人表达能力和适应性，亟需更直观、自然的多模态交互方式。

Method: 系统架构包含语音转录模型、手势识别模块和节拍检测信号处理流水线；通过提示模板将多模态输入上下文化后送入LLM，LLM结合预定义动作空间推理生成连贯动作序列，并通过ROS在四足机器人上执行。

Result: 框架能够解析语音中的语义命令、手势中的指向信息以及音乐中的节奏线索，融合生成连贯的机器人动作序列。

Conclusion: 该工作向更具流畅性、创造性和上下文感知能力的人机交互迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: The quest for intuitive and natural human-robot interaction (HRI) remains a significant challenge in robotics. Traditional methods often rely on rigid, pre-programmed commands that limit the robot's expressiveness and adaptability. This paper introduces a novel framework that leverages the reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) to synthesize complex robotic actions from a rich tapestry of multimodal human inputs: natural speech, hand gestures, and music/sound beats. Our system architecture integrates a speech transcription model, a gesture recognition module, and a signal processing pipeline for beat detection. These processed inputs are contextualized using prompt templates and fed into a LLM. The LLM, informed by a predefined robot action space, reasons over the combined inputs to generate a coherent sequence of actions. This sequence is dispatched to an action queue for execution on a quadruped robot over ROS. The framework has ability to interpret and fuse semantic commands from speech, deictic information from gestures, and rhythmic cues from music. This work represents a step towards creating robots that can interact with humans in a more fluid, creative, and context-aware manner.

</details>


### [68] [MIRTH: Mutual-Information Reasoning with Temporal Hubs for Vision-Language-Action Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31167)
*Hao Sun,Yu Song,Shiyu Teng,Ziwei Niu,Yen-Wei Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MIRTH是一个统一框架，通过在预训练VLA骨干上引入双尺度时序记忆枢纽、基于互信息的潜在推理令牌和并行动作解码，解决了单帧VLA模型的时间短视、推理间隙和推理低效问题，在LIBERO和真实LeRobot上达到SOTA并展现错误恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单帧VLA模型存在三个固有局限：时间短视（丢弃历史动态信息）、高层指令与底层动作指令之间的推理间隙（reasoning gap）、以及自回归标量解码导致的推理效率低下。

Method: 提出MIRTH框架，在预训练VLA骨干上增加三个关键创新：(1)双尺度时序记忆枢纽，将长期场景演变和短期运动趋势压缩为紧凑嵌入；(2)基于互信息目标优化的潜在推理令牌，构建语义计划空间以对齐多模态上下文与动作轨迹；(3)并行动作解码方案，用向量级预测替代自回归生成以最大化控制吞吐量。

Result: 在LIBERO仿真基准和真实LeRobot平台上达到SOTA性能，并展现出涌现的错误恢复能力。

Conclusion: MIRTH通过时序记忆、潜在推理和并行解码三个创新点，有效克服了单帧VLA模型的核心局限，实现了更高效、更鲁棒的机器人控制。

Abstract: VLA models have emerged as a powerful paradigm for transferring semantic knowledge from web-scale data to physical robotic control. However, current single-frame architectures suffer from intrinsic limitations: temporal myopia that discards historical dynamics, reasoning gaps between high-level instructions and low-level motor commands, and inference inefficiency due to autoregressive scalar decoding. In this work, we propose MIRTH, a unified framework designed to address these challenges. MIRTH augments a pretrained VLA backbone with three key innovations: (1) dual-scale temporal memory hubs that compress long-term scene evolution and short-term motion trends into compact embeddings; (2) latent reasoning tokens optimized via a mutual-information objective carving out a semantic plan space to align multimodal context with action trajectories; and (3) a parallel action decoding scheme that replaces autoregressive generation with vector-wise prediction to maximize control throughput. Extensive evaluations on the LIBERO simulation benchmark and a real-world LeRobot platform demonstrate that MIRTH achieves state-of-the-art performance and exhibiting emergent error recovery capabilities. The codes and collected datasets are released at http://github.com/kiva12138/mirth.

</details>


### [69] [Diffusion-based 4D Trajectory Prediction and Distributed Control for UAV Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31197)
*Tianshun Li,Hongliang Lu,Haoang Li,Xinhu Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种粗到精的4D轨迹预测与不确定性感知分布式非线性模型预测控制（DNMPC）统一框架，通过解耦维度预测和扩散残差动力学修正，在复杂低空环境中实现UAV编队的高精度实时追踪，追踪误差降低10-15%，平均误差低于0.07米，推理速度达34 FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UAV集群在复杂低空环境（如城市空域、工业场所）中需要精确的4D轨迹预测与闭环跟踪以确保安全高效运行，但面临无人机群动力学的内在非线性和编队控制的严格实时约束两大挑战。

Method: 1) 维度解耦轨迹预测模块：按轴预测降低计算复杂度；2) 扩散残差动力学修正模块：捕获时间相关动态不确定性；3) 将修正预测集成到DNMPC闭环中保证编队稳定性；4) 构建了同步多场景4D UAV集群数据集（7,900+帧）。

Result: 轨迹追踪误差降低10-15%，在复杂城市和工业环境下平均追踪误差低于0.07米，推理速度达34 FPS（延迟低于30毫秒），适用于敏捷飞行。

Conclusion: 所提出的统一框架在复杂低空环境中实现了UAV集群的高精度实时4D轨迹追踪，在精度和实时性上均优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: Accurate 4D trajectory prediction and closed-loop tracking are essential for Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) swarms to achieve safe and efficient operations in complex low-altitude environments such as urban airspaces, industrial sites, and indoor facilities. However, this task remains challenging due to intrinsic nonlinearity of UAV swarm dynamics and strict real-time constraints of swarm formation control. To address these challenges, we propose a unified framework that couples coarse-to-fine trajectory forecasting with uncertainty-aware Distributed Nonlinear Model Predictive Control (DNMPC). Our approach features two key innovations: 1) a dimension-decoupled trajectory prediction module that reduces computational complexity by forecasting axis-wise motion, and 2) a diffusion-based residual dynamics refinement module that captures temporally correlated dynamic uncertainties. These refined predictions are then integrated into a DNMPC loop to ensure formation stability. We also introduce a synchronized multi-scenario 4D UAV swarm dataset spanning six representative airspace scenarios. The dataset contains over \textbf{7,900} frames of synchronized three-UAV trajectories with frame-level annotations of speed intention and target sector. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, reducing trajectory tracking error by up to \textbf{10-15\%} and achieving sub-\textbf{0.07\,m} average tracking error in complex urban and industrial environments, while maintaining real-time inference speeds of 34 FPS (sub-30 ms latency) suitable for agile flight.

</details>


### [70] [Information-Aided DVL Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31216)
*Zeev Yampolsky,Itzik Klein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对传统DVL标定依赖GNSS信号的局限，提出信息辅助标定（IAC）方法，既提升GNSS可用环境下的标定精度，又实现了无GNSS环境下的DVL自标定，在实际AUV数据上分别获得最高20%和35%的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DVL速度测量对AUV导航精度至关重要，但传统Kalman滤波标定方法依赖GNSS信号，在GNSS不可用环境中无法进行标定，导致导航性能下降。

Method: 提出信息辅助标定（IAC）方法，通过引入额外信息辅助估计尺度因子和未对准误差，支持GNSS可用环境下的高精度标定和GNSS-free环境下的自标定两种模式。

Result: 在真实AUV数据集上，GNSS可用环境下平均改进最高20%，GNSS-free自标定下速度矢量估计改进最高35%。

Conclusion: IAC方法提升了导航精度、降低了导航漂移，增强了任务可靠性，有效解决了GNSS不可用环境下的DVL标定难题。

Abstract: The Doppler velocity log (DVL) velocity measurements are critical to the accuracy of autonomous underwater vehicle (AUV) navigation solutions and, consequently, to mission success. To ensure accurate measurements, the DVL is commonly calibrated before mission start while the AUV sails on the water surface, receiving global navigation satellite system (GNSS) signals that provide accurate reference measurements. Conventionally, Kalman filter-based approaches are employed during calibration to estimate the scale factor and misalignment errors. However, in certain environments, GNSS signals may be unavailable, rendering conventional calibration impossible and forcing the use of uncalibrated DVL measurements, which degrades navigation performance. To address this limitation, this work proposes information-aided calibration (IAC) with two main contributions: first, improving the accuracy of conventional Kalman filter-based calibration in GNSS-enabled environments, and second, enabling GNSS-free DVL self-calibration. Using real-world AUV datasets, the proposed IAC models achieve up to a 20% average improvement in GNSS-enabled environments and up to a 35% improvement in velocity vector estimation during GNSS-free DVL self-calibration. Overall, the proposed approach improves navigation accuracy, reduces navigation drift, and consequently enhances mission reliability.

</details>


### [71] [TactX: Learning Shared Tactile Representations Across Diverse Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31236)
*Junsung Park,Sachin Bhadang,Carmelo Sferrazza,Sha Yi,Xiaolong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TactX提出了一种跨模态（电阻式、磁式、视觉式）的可迁移触觉表示学习框架，通过对齐不同传感器的潜在空间，实现策略在不同触觉传感器间的零样本迁移，平均成功率从27.5%提升至45.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉传感器表示与策略高度耦合，缺乏跨平台、跨传感器的泛化能力，限制了触觉操作技能的迁移性。

Method: 通过配对接触数据训练各传感器对应的特定编码器，将异构触觉观测映射到共享潜在空间，所有传感器对的编码器联合训练以构建一致的潜在空间。

Result: 在四项接触丰富操作任务中，基于一种传感器训练的策略可零样本迁移至物理结构不同的其他传感器，平均成功率从27.5%（纯视觉策略）提升至45.9%。

Conclusion: TactX实现了传感器无关的触觉操作，证明了跨模态触觉表示的可迁移性，为构建通用触觉操作策略提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Tactile sensors provide critical information for contact-rich manipulation, yet tactile representations and policies remain tightly coupled to each specific sensor, limiting transferability across robots and hardware platforms. We propose TactX, a framework for learning a transferable tactile representation across sensors spanning three fundamentally different transduction modalities: resistive, magnetic, and vision-based. TactX maps heterogeneous tactile observations into a shared latent space through modality-specific encoders trained on paired contact data. Such paired interactions provide a natural alignment signal across modalities, and the encoders are jointly trained across all sensor pairs, inducing a consistent latent space for all sensor types. Our experiments show that TactX aligns tactile representations across sensors while preserving object-level contact information, as evidenced by sensor-identity prediction and object classification in the learned latent space. We evaluate TactX on four contact-rich manipulation tasks: pick-and-place, plug insertion, board wiping, and object reorientation, and show that policies trained with one sensor transfer zero-shot to physically distinct sensors through the shared latent. This improves the average success rate from 27.5% for vision-only policy to 45.9%, providing a step toward sensor-agnostic tactile manipulation.

</details>


### [72] [Plan Right, Then Plan Tight: Symbolic RL for Efficient Embodied Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31260)
*Xiangli Shi,Xiaomeng Zhu,Ye Tian,Yuchun Guo,Ziyang Sun,Lujie Yin,Yuxuan Zhou,Yufei Huang*

Main category: cs.RO
