TL;DR: 本文提出通过BDDL规范作为统一接口，结合视频解析、LLM验证器和轻量符号引擎为具身任务规划提供密集验证信号，并引入GroupAdapt难度感知长度调度策略，在BEHAVIOR-1000上将8B规划器的Strict-Pass分数提升至97.3，响应长度压缩79%至207 tokens。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身任务规划方法存在验证困难问题：基于提示或强化学习的规划器能生成流畅动作文本，但缺乏低成本确定性验证来检查动作序列在目标世界中的有效性；高保真仿真虽然准确但速度太慢，无法作为内循环训练信号。因此需要一种可验证的监督和奖励信号，且不依赖字符串匹配或完整仿真。

Method: 1) 提出BDDL规范作为数据构建、计划验证和奖励设计的统一接口；2) 构建视频到BDDL解析器、LLM验证器和轻量符号引擎组成的验证流水线，以毫秒级延迟提供密集反馈；3) 提出GroupAdapt难度感知长度调度策略，利用批次内组通过率作为零成本信号，使困难提示获得更宽的长度容差，并随通过率提升自动收紧。

Result: 在BEHAVIOR-1000上，8B规划器Strict-Pass分数达97.3，较Qwen3-8B基线相对提升25.9%，超越最强大型模型基线3.5%，同时响应长度压缩79%至207 tokens。

Conclusion: BDDL规范作为统一接口能够有效解决具身任务规划中的验证监督问题，结合GroupAdapt调度策略可同时提升规划有效性和效率。

Abstract: Embodied task planning asks an agent to turn a natural-language instruction into an executable sequence of actions in a physical scene, and is a building block for household, assistive, and service robots. Recent prompting-based and reinforcement-learning planners generate fluent action text but lack a cheap deterministic check that the produced plan is valid in the target world, while high-fidelity simulation is too slow to serve as an inner-loop training signal. The general problem is therefore how to obtain verifiable supervision and rewards for embodied planners without relying on string-level matching or full simulation. Here we show that a single BDDL specification, automatically constructed from open-world video evidence or curated tasks, can serve as a shared interface for data construction, plan verification, and reward design. A video-to-BDDL parser, an LLM verifier, and a lightweight symbolic engine together supply dense feedback at millisecond latency. We further introduce GroupAdapt, a difficulty-aware length schedule that uses the in-batch group pass rate as a zero-cost signal so that hard prompts get wider length tolerance and automatically tighten as their pass rate improves. Under the guidance of the proposed verifier and GroupAdapt schedule, the 8B planner attains a Strict-Pass score of 97.3 on BEHAVIOR-1000, yielding a 25.9 percent relative improvement over the Qwen3-8B baseline. This result exceeds the strongest large-model baseline by 3.5 percent, while simultaneously compressing the response length by 79 percent to 207 tokens, demonstrating both effectiveness and efficiency.

</details>


### [73] [3D HAMSTER: Bridging Planning and Control in Hierarchical Vision Language Action Models through 3D Trajectory Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31329)
*Dongyoon Hwang,Byungkun Lee,Dongjin Kim,Hyojin Jang,Hoiyeong Jin,Jueun Mun,Minho Park,Hojoon Lee,Hyunseung Kim,Jaegul Choo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出3D HAMSTER分层框架，通过让VLM直接预测3D轨迹（而非2D引导）来提升机器人操作泛化能力，在3D轨迹预测、仿真和真实操作中均优于2D引导基线，尤其在视觉外观变化和未见过的语言/空间/视觉条件下优势明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分层VLA模型使用VLM预测2D末端执行器轨迹作为下游策略的显式引导，但低层策略在3D点云空间操作，2D引导缺乏深度信息，导致每个轨迹点被迫赋予其下方场景表面的深度值，产生几何畸变的轨迹。

Method: 在VLM中增加专用深度编码器和密集深度重建目标，使规划器直接输出度量可靠的3D轨迹序列；将这些3D轨迹直接集成到基于点云的低层策略中。

Result: 在3D轨迹预测、仿真和真实机器人操作中，3D HAMSTER一致优于专有VLM和2D引导基线，在出现视觉外观变化、未见过的语言/空间/视觉条件下提升最为显著。

Conclusion: 通过让高层规划器直接输出3D轨迹而非2D引导，可以消除几何畸变，显著提升分层VLA模型在复杂泛化场景下的操作性能。

Abstract: Hierarchical Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models decouple high-level planning from low-level control to improve generalization in robot manipulation. Recent work in this paradigm uses 2D end-effector trajectories predicted by a Vision-Language Model (VLM) as explicit guidance for a downstream policy. However, state-of-the-art low-level policies operate in 3D metric space on point clouds, and feeding them 2D guidance that lacks depth forces each waypoint to be assigned the depth of whatever scene surface lies beneath it, producing geometrically distorted trajectories. We propose 3D HAMSTER, a hierarchical framework that closes this gap by having the planner directly output metrically reliable 3D trajectories. We augment a VLM with a dedicated depth encoder and a dense depth reconstruction objective to predict 3D waypoint sequences, which are directly integrated into a pointcloudbased low-level policy. Across 3D trajectory prediction, simulation, and real-world manipulation, 3D HAMSTER consistently outperforms proprietary VLMs and 2D-guided baselines, with the largest gains under appearance-altering shifts and unseen language, spatial, and visual conditions. The project page is available at https://davian-robotics.github.io/3D_HAMSTER/.

</details>


### [74] [Verification-Gated Agentic Mission-State Governance for Intelligent Industrial Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31339)
*Guoqin Tang,Qingxuan Jia,Yichen Tan,Zeyuan Huang,Ning Ji,Gang Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种验证门控的智能体任务状态治理框架，通过维护同步的任务森林和黑板状态对象，并引入执行耦合拓扑进行确定性验证与原子提交，确保多机器人工业系统在长周期执行中保持任务依赖、资源所有权、安全约束和修复边界。在室内工厂和远程构建基准测试中，该框架减少了无效提交、锁冲突、重复分配等问题，提升了可验证和安全审计的任务状态进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主提议生成无法保证多机器人工业系统在长周期执行中维持任务依赖、资源所有权、安全保持和修复边界，急需一种可验证的治理机制。

Method: 构建双重同步状态对象（任务森林+黑板），从快照派生执行耦合拓扑，所有候选操作需经确定性验证和原子提交后才能更新任务状态。

Result: 在室内工厂多机器人场景、30种子远程构建压力测试、结构消融和可扩展性探测中，验证了更优的可验证和安全审计任务进展，无效提交、锁冲突、重复分配、遗弃节点和破坏性修复均显著减少。

Conclusion: 智能体AI应作为提议生成层，受可审查的任务状态验证治理，而非不受约束的执行权威。

Abstract: Agentic artificial intelligence is increasingly used to decompose industrial tasks, propose robot actions, and adapt execution plans in dynamic cyber-physical environments. However, autonomous proposal generation alone does not guarantee that multi-robot industrial systems preserve task dependencies, resource ownership, safety holds, or repair boundaries during long-horizon execution. This paper introduces a verification-gated agentic mission-state governance framework for intelligent industrial multi-robot systems. The framework maintains two synchronized state objects: an evolving task forest for persistent hierarchy, delayed grounding, and repairable substructures; and a governed blackboard for online execution state, robot traces, resource locks, world beliefs, proposals, verification records, and scene-temporary constraints. From each forest--blackboard snapshot, a derived execution coupling topology exposes cross-branch dependencies for proposal verification, parallel-commit eligibility, and bounded repair. Candidate assignments, repairs, deferrals, and constraint updates may be generated by heuristic, optimization, or agentic reasoning modules, but they can update the committed mission state only after deterministic verification and atomic commit. We evaluate the framework in an indoor factory multi-robot scenario, 30-seed remote-construction stress benchmarks, structural ablations, and scalability probes. The results show improved verified and safety-audited mission-state progress with fewer invalid commitments, lock conflicts, duplicate assignments, abandoned nodes, and disruptive repairs under modeled mission predicates. The study positions agentic AI as a proposal-generating layer governed by inspectable mission-state verification rather than as an unchecked execution authority.

</details>


### [75] [Stage-Transition Dense Reward Modeling for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31377)
*Yang Yang,Bingjie Chen,Zihan Wang,Yizhe Li,Guoping Pan,Yi Cheng,Houde Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: STDR是一种将专家视频转化为逻辑驱动的稠密奖励信号的视觉奖励学习框架，通过阶段转换反馈和阶段内进度反馈两种信号来训练强化学习智能体，并集成OOD检测与抓取调节模块以增强鲁棒性。在14个操作任务上，STDR一致提升了样本效率和成功率，在多个挑战性任务上匹敌或超越了手工设计的稠密奖励，真实机器人实验也验证了其稳定且校准良好的奖励分配能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域机器人操作强化学习受限于稀疏和延迟奖励，而手工设计稠密奖励信号成本高昂且对环境和物体配置变化脆弱。

Method: 提出了阶段转换稠密奖励（STDR）框架，利用语义理解从专家演示中推断任务阶段结构，在线训练时提供两种互补信号：(i) 阶段转换反馈（目标导向奖励）和(ii) 阶段内进度反馈（细粒度引导），并集成OOD检测机制和抓取调节模块来防止奖励破解。

Result: 在MetaWorld、ManiSkill和Franka Kitchen的14个操作任务上，STDR一致提升了样本效率和成功率，多个挑战性任务上匹敌或超越手工设计的稠密奖励。真实机器人评估显示STDR能分配稳定、与进度对齐的奖励，对视觉噪声具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: STDR作为一种自动化的稠密奖励学习方法，能够有效替代手工设计的奖励函数，在多种任务上展现出一致的性能提升和良好的跨设置泛化能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for long-horizon robotic manipulation is often limited by sparse and delayed rewards, while manually designing dense shaping signals is costly and brittle to changes in environments and object configurations. This work proposes Stage-Transition Dense Reward (STDR), a visual reward-learning framework that converts unstructured expert videos into logically grounded dense rewards for training RL agents from scratch. STDR leverages semantic understanding to infer a task's stage structure from demonstrations, and delivers two complementary learning signals during online training: (i) stage-transition feedback that provides goal-directed reward, and (ii) within-stage progress feedback that supplies fine-grained guidance toward completing each stage. Furthermore, an out-of-distribution (OOD) detection mechanism and a grasping regulation module are integrated to enhance robustness and prevent reward hacking. Experiments on 14 manipulation tasks across MetaWorld, ManiSkill, and Franka Kitchen show that STDR consistently improves sample efficiency and success rates over multiple baselines, and matches or surpasses handcrafted dense rewards on several challenging tasks. Real-robot evaluations further indicate that STDR assigns stable, progress-aligned rewards on successful executions while producing appropriately low rewards for failures, suggesting robustness to visual noise and better-calibrated reward assignment across settings.

</details>


### [76] [Revisiting Parameter Redundancy in Vision-Language-Action Models: Insights from VLM-to-VLA Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31382)
*Fengnian Zhang,Tao Huang,Siyu Xu,Zhong Jin,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文质疑VLA模型剪枝后必须通过微调恢复性能的常规范式，发现VLM到VLA适应过程中参数变化呈现结构化分布，据此设计无恢复的多模块联合剪枝方案，在LIBERO基准上减少12%-30%参数并保持约90%原始性能，而现有方法在无恢复条件下完全崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战VLA模型剪枝后性能下降不可避免且必须依赖微调恢复的现有认知，质疑被剪枝参数是否真正冗余，揭示这一范式可能掩盖了对关键参数的 indiscriminate 剪枝问题。

Method: 1) 量化VLM到VLA适应过程中参数散度的空间分布，揭示跨模块的结构化模式；2) 引入受控剪枝作为诊断探针，通过比较无微调条件下移除不同参数子集对性能的直接影响，建立适应诱发的散度信号与功能贡献之间的因果联系；3) 基于发现的模块异质性，设计多模块联合剪枝方案。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上，对OpenVLA和π0.5模型减少12%-30%参数，无任何剪枝后恢复即可保持约90%原始性能；现有参数剪枝标准在相同无恢复约束下导致性能完全崩溃。

Conclusion: 揭示了VLA适应中的参数演化机制，为资源受限环境中部署高效、鲁棒的机器人策略提供了新路径。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have made significant strides in embodied intelligence by integrating the powerful representations of pre-trained Vision-Language Models (VLMs). However, the massive parameter scale of VLAs imposes a heavy computational burden, and these models exhibit extreme sensitivity to parameter pruning. Current paradigms often treat the resulting performance degradation as inevitable, relying on fine-tuning or low-rank corrections to recover efficacy. We challenge this convention by questioning whether the removed parameters are truly redundant if VLA pruning necessitates performance recovery to be effective, or if this paradigm masks the indiscriminate pruning of critical parameters. We revisit parameter redundancy through the lens of VLM-to-VLA adaptation, first quantifying the spatial distribution of parameter divergence during adaptation to reveal structured patterns across different modules. Subsequently, we introduce controlled pruning as a diagnostic probe: by comparing the direct impact of removing different parameter subsets on VLA performance without any fine-tuning, we establish a causal link between adaptation-induced divergence signals and functional contributions. Based on the discovered modular heterogeneities, we design a multi-module joint pruning scheme. Evaluations on the LIBERO benchmark demonstrate that our approach reduces the parameters of OpenVLA and $π_{0.5}$ by 12\%--30\% while maintaining approximately 90\% of the original performance without any post-pruning recovery. In contrast, existing parameter pruning criteria result in total performance collapse when evaluated under the same recovery-free constraints. Our study reveals the parameter evolution mechanism in VLA adaptation and provides a new path for deploying efficient, robust robotic policies in resource-constrained environments.

</details>


### [77] [UniTac: A Unified Multimodal Model for Cross-Sensor Tactile Understanding and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31451)
*Jiahang Tu,Fengyu Yang,Chenyang Ma,Xihang Yu,Ziyao Zeng,Shaokai Wu,Hanbin Zhao,Zhi Tao,Chao Zhang,Hui Qian,Alex Wong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UniTac是首个统一感知与生成的多模态触觉模型，通过双层级表征编码传感器和物体属性，在理解任务（物体属性描述、传感器识别）和生成任务（重建与对齐的两阶段训练，以及基于传感器先验的采样策略）上均达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型（UMMs）在视觉、语言等领域展现了强大的理解与生成能力，但尚未扩展到触觉领域。触觉信号的特殊性在于其语义由物体层级（语义信息）和传感器层级（物理配置）共同决定，因此需要一种能同时处理这两方面信息的统一模型。

Method: 1) 将触觉过程建模为从非接触到接触的过渡，通过双层级表征编码传感器和物体属性；2) 理解任务：物体属性描述和传感器识别；3) 生成任务：两阶段训练范式（重建+对齐），以及基于传感器先验的采样策略模拟真实触觉接触。

Result: 在大规模多传感器数据集上，UniTac在触觉理解任务上达到SOTA性能，并能跨传感器生成逼真的触觉信号。

Conclusion: 将统一多模态范式成功拓展到触觉领域，证明双层级表征和两阶段生成策略对触觉理解与生成的有效性，为触觉AI的跨传感器泛化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Unified multimodal models (UMMs) have shown great promise in integrating understanding and generation across diverse modalities. However, existing research rarely extends this paradigm to the tactile domain, where both object-level semantics and sensor-level configurations jointly determine the meaning of touch. To address this gap, we propose UniTac, the first UMM designed for tactile understanding and generation. UniTac models the tactile process as a transition from non-contact to contact, capturing the physical interaction between sensors and objects through a dual-level representation that encodes both sensor and object attributes. For tactile understanding, UniTac introduces two tasks, object property description and sensor identification, to enhance reasoning over physical and cross-sensor information. For tactile generation, we design a two-stage training paradigm consisting of reconstruction and alignment, together with a sensor-prior-based sampling strategy that simulates realistic tactile contact. Trained on large-scale multi-sensor datasets, UniTac achieves state-of-the-art performance in tactile understanding and generates realistic tactile signals across sensors.

</details>


### [78] [A Large-Language-Model Supported Personalized Driving Framework for Lane Change in Highway Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31483)
*Dong Bi,Yongqi Zhao,Paul Kovacevic,Tomislav Mihalj,Ji Zhou,Jiayuan Gong,Arno Eichberger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的个性化驾驶框架，用于高速公路车道变换场景。该框架将自然语言驾驶命令映射到Apollo自动驾驶栈中的可执行规划参数，通过三种驾驶风格（激进、正常、保守）实现个性化行为生成。利用聚类和风格强度排序构建参数集，并采用检索增强生成（RAG）技术提升对隐式用户命令的理解。实验结果表明，该框架能生成可区分的个性化车道变换行为，RAG显著提升了偏好解释能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶系统在将自然语言驾驶偏好（尤其是隐式表达的偏好）转化为可执行的、可区分的驾驶行为方面支持有限，难以满足用户对个性化驾驶的需求。

Method: 提出LLM支持的个性化驾驶框架，将自然语言驾驶命令依据三种驾驶风格映射到Apollo自动驾驶栈的规划参数；通过聚类和风格强度排序构建风格特定的参数集；利用RAG技术构建检索数据集以增强对隐式用户命令的LLM解读能力。

Result: 生成的参数集能够产生可区分的个性化车道变换行为；RAG持续提升了偏好解读性能，尤其是对隐式命令。

Conclusion: 将LLM自然语言交互与Apollo集成，在支持个性化车道变换行为生成方面具有潜力。

Abstract: Personalized driving can improve the user acceptance of automated driving systems. However, existing methods still provide limited support for translating natural-language driving preferences, especially when such preferences are expressed implicitly, into executable and distinguishable driving behaviors. This paper proposes a large language model (LLM)-supported personalized driving framework for highway lane-change scenarios. The framework maps natural-language driving commands to executable planning parameters in the open-source Apollo automated driving stack according to three driving styles: aggressive, normal, and conservative. To establish this mapping, candidate planning parameters are evaluated based on the resulting lane-change behaviors, and style-specific parameter sets are constructed through clustering and style-intensity ranking. For command interpretation, a retrieval dataset is constructed to support retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), enabling LLM-based interpretation of implicit user commands. Experimental results show that the derived parameter sets generate distinguishable personalized lane-change behaviors, while RAG consistently improves preference interpretation, particularly for implicit commands. These results indicate the potential of integrating LLM-based natural-language interaction with Apollo to support personalized lane-change behavior generation. The source code and the relevant datasets are available at: https://github.com/ftgTUGraz/LLM-Personalized-Driving.

</details>


### [79] [Energy-Optimal Spatial Iterative Learning within a Virtual Tube](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31487)
*Chen Min,Shuli Lv,Pengda Mao,Huixin Cao,Li Hong,Quan Quan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种无模型的在线迭代学习框架，用于最小化无人机能量消耗，无需精确系统模型，计算复杂度O(n)，比模型方法快50-60倍，并通过仿真和真实飞行实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机嵌入式能源（如锂电池）续航有限，现有节能轨迹规划方法依赖精确系统模型且计算开销大，亟需一种高效且实用的无模型方法。

Method: 提出模型无关的在线迭代学习（IL）框架，无需无人机动力学或能量消耗的显式模型，通过迭代优化路径点来降低能耗，每次迭代计算复杂度为O(n)。

Result: 在测试案例中，所提方法比基于模型的IPOPT基准方法快约50-60倍，计算复杂度O(n)，仿真和多种无人机平台的真实飞行实验均验证了其有效性和计算效率。

Conclusion: 该无模型在线迭代学习方法能有效降低无人机能耗，计算代价低且实际适用性强，具有较好的跨平台泛化能力。

Abstract: Due to the limited endurance of embedded energy sources such as lithium-polymer (LiPo) batteries, the flight duration and operational range of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) are severely constrained. Although energy-efficient trajectory planning and control have been widely studied, most existing approaches rely on accurate system models and computationally expensive optimization procedures. This paper proposes a model-free online iterative learning (IL) framework to minimize energy consumption. Without requiring explicit models of UAV dynamics or energy consumption, the proposed method improves energy efficiency while maintaining a low computational cost. The per-iteration computational complexity is O(n), where n denotes the number of path points. In the tested cases, the proposed method is approximately 50--60 times faster than the model-based IPOPT benchmark. Simulation results and real-world flight experiments across multiple UAV platforms validate the effectiveness, computational efficiency, and practical applicability of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [80] [ChronoFlow-Policy: Unifying Past-Current-Future Interaction Flow in Visuomotor Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31493)
*Bokai Lin,Yifu Xu,Xinyu Zhan,Hongjie Fang,Jialin Tian,Fu-Cheng Zhang,Yong-Lu Li,Cewu Lu,Lixin Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ChronoFlow，一种基于稀疏3D关键点统一表示过去、当前和未来交互动力学的表征方法，并构建了ChronoFlow-Policy扩散策略，通过联合训练学习该表征与动作序列。在14个模拟和5个真实操作任务中，该方法在长时域和非马尔可夫场景下显著优于现有扩散策略基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉运动策略通常孤立地建模历史上下文或未来动力学，缺乏对交互动力学的统一时间表征，无法像人类一样同时利用过去经验和预期结果进行推理。

Method: 提出ChronoFlow表示，通过稀疏3D关键点（对象和夹爪）统一捕获过去、当前和未来的交互动力学；在此基础上构建ChronoFlow-Policy，一种扩散策略模型，通过联合训练目标同时学习ChronoFlow和动作序列。

Result: 在14个模拟操作任务和5个真实世界操作任务上，ChronoFlow-Policy一致优于强扩散策略基线，并在长时域和非马尔可夫操作场景中展现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ChronoFlow作为一种统一的时间表征能有效提升策略在复杂操作任务中的性能，尤其在需要长期依赖和非马尔可夫决策的场景下具有显著优势。

Abstract: Visual signals play a crucial role in policy learning by enabling models to capture object motion and interaction dynamics. Just as humans reason about actions using both past experience and anticipated outcomes, effective policies should integrate past interactions with future predictions. However, existing visuomotor policies typically model either historical context or future dynamics in isolation, lacking a unified temporal representation of interaction dynamics. In this work, we introduce \textbf{ChronoFlow}, a temporally unified representation that captures \textbf{past, current, and future} interaction dynamics through sparse 3D keypoints of both objects and the gripper. Based on this representation, we propose \textbf{ChronoFlow-Policy}, a diffusion-based visuomotor policy that jointly learns ChronoFlow and action sequences through a co-training objective. Experiments on 14 simulated tasks and 5 real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate that ChronoFlow-Policy consistently outperforms strong diffusion-policy baselines and improves robustness in long-horizon and non-Markovian manipulation scenarios.

</details>


### [81] [Robustness of Robotic Manipulation: Foundations and Frontiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31494)
*Yifei Dong,Zhanyi Sun,Lujie Yang,Manuel Baum,Kei Ikemura,Shuran Song,Florian T. Pokorny,Xianyi Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了机器人操作鲁棒性，给出了形式化定义，从概率论和控制理论角度提出了通用公式，综合了感知、规划、控制、策略学习和硬件层面的鲁棒性机制，并讨论了评估方法和未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同子领域对操作鲁棒性的定义不统一，限制了深入分析和跨领域交流，阻碍了机器人操作向人类水平鲁棒性的进步。

Method: 首先给出鲁棒性的形式化定义，然后从概率论和控制理论角度建立通用公式，再综合各层面（感知、规划、控制、策略学习、硬件）的鲁棒性原理与机制，并通过代表性研究进行说明。

Result: 提出了操作鲁棒性的统一形式化定义和通用公式，系统梳理了多层面的鲁棒性机制，回顾了现有评估方法，并提炼了设计稳健操作系统的原则。

Conclusion: 本文为机器人操作鲁棒性提供了系统性理解，指出了实现人类级鲁棒性的开放问题和未来方向。

Abstract: Humans and animals exhibit remarkable robustness in physical manipulation, yet robots remain far behind. Progress toward human-level manipulation robustness is hindered by the absence of a unified and systematic understanding: different subfields frame robustness in distinct ways, often leaving the concept ambiguous and limiting deeper analysis as well as communication across research areas. This paper presents a systematic study of manipulation robustness. We begin with a formal definition, characterizing robustness as the degree to which a manipulation system can achieve its goal in the presence of uncertainty and variation. Building on this definition, we introduce general formulations of manipulation robustness from probabilistic and control-theoretic perspectives. We then synthesize the guiding principles and concrete mechanisms of manipulation robustness across perception, planning, control, policy learning, and hardware, illustrating each mechanism through representative works, including foundational and recent studies. In addition, we revisit existing metrics and evaluation methods for quantifying manipulation robustness. Finally, we distill broader lessons for designing robust manipulation systems and discuss open problems and future directions toward achieving human-level robustness in robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [82] [Communication-Aware Robot Execution for Cloud Inference under Spatially Heterogeneous Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31497)
*Fengkai Liu,Yuichi Ohsita,Masayuki Murata,Hideyuki Shimonishi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于请求-响应窗口的框架，用于在空间异构连接环境下实现云化机器人的鲁棒任务执行，通过在机器人运动过程中智能选择请求提交点来平衡通信质量与任务进度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云承载的基础模型使机器人能够超越机载计算限制进行语义推理，但在空间异构连接环境下，当前原语执行决定何时需要下一个云结果，而无线环境决定何处可提交请求和接收响应，导致执行变得脆弱。现有方法仅降低延迟或改善单个传输，未解决能同时保证可靠上传和可行响应检索的提交位置选择问题。

Method: 提出请求-响应窗口概念，刻画一次完整云周期所需时间（包含上行传输、云推理、下行检索及推理不确定性）。基于该窗口和可用通信地图，将下一请求点视为当前原语执行过程中的运动决策，选择能提供足够通信质量提交请求且保留当前原语有限支持范围内进度的位置。所选请求点融入局部规划器，引导机器人在提交前到达该点，提交后保持足够连接以检索下一个云结果。

Result: 在基于测量的室内无线场景实验中，所提方法在任务成功率上达到最佳或并列最佳，同时使用更少的请求尝试次数并产生更低的请求失败率。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效解决了空间异构连接下云化机器人的执行脆弱性问题，通过将通信感知的请求点选择集成到运动规划中，在保证可靠通信的同时维持任务进度，实验验证了其优越性。

Abstract: Cloud-hosted foundation models enable robots to use semantic reasoning beyond onboard computational limits. In this setting, the robot executes a currently available primitive generated by the cloud, and continued task progress requires the next cloud result before this primitive is exhausted. This execution becomes fragile under spatially heterogeneous connectivity, because the current primitive determines when the next result is needed, whereas the wireless environment determines where the next request can be submitted and where the response can be retrieved. Strategies that reduce latency or improve individual transmissions can shorten this dependency, but they do not determine a submission location that supports reliable upload and leaves a feasible opportunity for response retrieval. To address this problem, we introduce the request--response window, which characterizes the time required for the next cloud cycle, including uplink transmission, cloud inference, downlink retrieval, and inference uncertainty. Building on this window and an available communication map, the proposed framework treats the next request point as a motion decision during ongoing primitive execution, selecting it to provide sufficient communication quality for cloud request submission while preserving progress within the finite support of the current primitive. The selected request point is incorporated into a local planner, which guides the robot toward the request point before submission and then continues task execution while maintaining sufficient connectivity for retrieving the next cloud result. Experiments in an indoor wireless scenario built from measurements show that the proposed method achieves the best or tied-best task success among the compared methods, while using fewer request attempts and producing lower request failure rates.

</details>


### [83] [Stabilization Learning: A Paradigm Transition Bridging Control Theory and Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31562)
*Quan Quan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了稳定化学习（Stabilization Learning）这一跨学科范式，将其与证书学习和强化学习区分开来，构建了基于六元组和七元组的统一数学框架，通过11类问题的形式化重构展示了其广泛适用性，并指出未来方向在于统一问题框架与高效鲁棒学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有控制与机器学习方法在应对复杂高维非线性系统时，缺乏一个以稳定性为核心目标的统一理论框架。证书学习偏重形式化证明，强化学习追求最优性，二者均未将稳定性作为首要目标，因此需要一个新的范式来桥接控制理论与机器学习，实现兼具理论严谨性与工程实用性的复杂系统控制。

Method: 本文采用理论建构方法，首先界定稳定化学习的定义与核心特征（实时反馈、自适应机制、以稳定性为首要目标），将其与证书学习和强化学习进行区分。然后构建基于六元组的统一数学框架，并扩展为两种七元组模型（带约束学习的障碍空间模型和带目标的跟踪问题模型）。通过对11类问题（包括多智能体协同追踪、视觉伺服机器人位置稳定、棋类游戏、Push-T任务等）的形式化重构来验证框架的适用性。

Result: 明确了稳定化学习的两大类稳定性以及三种典型应用场景（控制、观测、识别）；构建了统一的六元组数学框架及其七元组扩展模型；分析了状态空间、受控系统、度量、策略等关键要素的角色与实现选择；通过11类问题的形式化重构验证了框架在多领域的潜在适用性。

Conclusion: 未来稳定化学习的两个主要研究方向是：构建统一的问题框架以及实现高效鲁棒的学习方法。该范式为复杂系统控制提供了兼具理论严谨性与工程实用性的解决方案。

Abstract: Stabilization learning is an interdisciplinary paradigm that bridges control theory and machine learning. Its core idea is to enable systems to adjust their policies under perturbations or environmental changes through real-time feedback and adaptive mechanisms. It takes stability as its primary goal, distinguishing itself from certificate learning, which focuses on formal proofs, and reinforcement learning, which pursues optimality. It encompasses a range of methods, including Lyapunov-based analysis and design, deep feature extraction, and data-driven feedback synthesis, and is applicable to complex high-dimensional, nonlinear systems. This paper elaborates on the two major categories of stability in stabilization learning, as well as three typical application scenarios: control, observation, and recognition. It constructs a unified mathematical framework based on a six-tuple, and expands into two types of seven-tuple models: constrained learning with barrier spaces and tracking problems with targets. It also analyzes the roles, meanings, and implementation choices of key elements such as state space, controlled system, metrics, and policy. Through the formal reformulation of 11 types of problems, including multi-agent cooperative tracking, visual servo robot position stabilization, chess games, and Push-T tasks, this paper illustrates the potential applicability of the framework across multiple domains. Finally, it points out that future stabilization learning will focus on two major directions: constructing a unified problem framework and achieving efficient and robust learning, providing solutions for complex system control that combine theoretical rigor with engineering practicality.

</details>


### [84] [DynFly: Dynamic-Aware Continuous Trajectory Generation for UAV Vision-Language Navigation in Urban Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31654)
*Wen Jiang,Hanfang Liang,Li Wang,Kangyao Huang,Wang Xu,Wei Fan,Jinyuan Liu,Shaoyu Liu,Hongwei Duan,Bin Xu,Xiangyang Ji,Huaping Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DynFly提出了一种动态感知的连续轨迹生成框架，通过B样条控制点空间表示和Spline-DiT生成器，弥合了高层导航推理与可执行无人机运动之间的鸿沟，在OpenUAV基准上显著提升了导航性能和轨迹质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UAV-VLN方法主要预测离散动作、局部目标或稀疏航点，从导航意图到可执行无人机运动的连续过渡建模薄弱，存在运动-接口鸿沟，限制了生成轨迹的连续性、稳定性和可执行性。

Method: 将专家轨迹表示为B样条控制点空间，使用Spline-DiT生成器通过流匹配学习条件轨迹生成；引入面向无人机动态感知的监督信号，包括位置、有限差分速度/加速度、航向一致性和局部目标对齐约束；该框架可集成到现有UAV-VLN系统中而不改变其视觉-语言推理流程。

Result: 在OpenUAV Test Unseen Full分片上，相比最强基线NDTW提升4.69、SDTW提升2.40、SR提升2.14个百分点、OSR提升4.87个百分点，NE降低4.51米。

Conclusion: DynFly有效弥合了高层导航推理与低层无人机运动控制之间的鸿沟，同时提升了导航性能和轨迹质量，且具有良好的即插即用兼容性。

Abstract: Recent advances in multimodal large models have significantly improved UAV vision-language navigation (UAV-VLN) by enhancing high-level perception and reasoning. However, existing methods mainly focus on predicting discrete actions, local targets, or sparse waypoints, while the continuous transition from navigation intent to executable UAV motion remains weakly modeled. This motion-interface gap limits the continuity, stability, and executability of generated UAV trajectories. To address this gap, we propose DynFly, a dynamic-aware continuous trajectory generation framework that bridges high-level navigation reasoning and executable UAV motion. DynFly bridges high-level navigation intent and continuous UAV motion through a lightweight trajectory generation layer. Specifically, it represents expert trajectories in B-spline control-point space and employs a Spline-DiT generator to learn conditional trajectory generation via flow matching. Furthermore, we introduce UAV-oriented dynamic-aware supervision over position, finite-difference velocity, finite-difference acceleration, heading consistency, and local target alignment, enabling the generated trajectories to better satisfy UAV motion characteristics. And our trajectory generation framework can also be integrated with an existing UAV-VLN framework while preserving its original visual-language reasoning pipeline. Extensive experiments on the OpenUAV UAV-VLN benchmark show that DynFly improves both navigation performance and trajectory quality. On the Test Unseen Full split, DynFly improves the strongest baseline by 4.69 NDTW, 2.40 SDTW, 2.14 SR points and 4.87 OSR points, while reducing NE by 4.51 m.

</details>


### [85] [HABIT: Human-Aware Behavior and Interaction Training Dataset for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31682)
*Jaehwi Song,Suchae Jeong,Byeongguk Jeon,Sungdong Kim,Minjoon Seo,Hyungmok Son,Kimin Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HABIT是一个大规模机器人演示数据集，聚焦于人机共存环境。包含超过1万条演示、160小时、60个任务，分为Collaborator、Coworker、Supervisor三种人机交互角色。实验表明，基于该数据训练的策略能产生人类感知行为（时空同步、避让、手势理解），并快速适应新人机交互任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人演示数据集都是在人类缺席的环境中收集的，导致训练出的策略虽然能独立完成任务，但缺乏人类感知（human-aware）行为，无法在人机共存环境中有效运作。

Method: 收集超过1万条、160小时、60个任务的大规模人机共存演示数据集；将任务划分为三种人机交互角色（Collaborator合作者、Coworker同事、Supervisor监督者）；基于该数据集训练机器人策略并评估其人类感知行为表现。

Result: 训练于HABIT数据的策略展现出人类感知行为：Collaborator任务中的时空同步、Coworker任务中的避让行为、Supervisor任务中的手势理解。而仅使用机器人数据训练的模型无法产生这些行为。此外，基于HABIT训练可实现快速适应新的人机交互任务。

Conclusion: 引入人类存在作为数据集多样性的新维度，能够将机器人策略扩展到人类共享环境中，弥补了现有数据集在人类感知行为方面的缺失。

Abstract: Large-scale demonstration datasets have been central to recent progress in general-purpose robot policies. However, existing datasets are collected in human-absent settings, and policies trained on such data may perform tasks competently in isolation but fail to exhibit human-aware behaviors. To address this gap, we introduce HABIT, a large-scale robot demonstration dataset for human-present environments. We organize tasks into three roles capturing distinct modes of human-robot interaction: Collaborator, where human and robot jointly accomplish a task; Coworker, where they pursue separate tasks in a shared space; and Supervisor, where the human directs the robot. The dataset comprises over 10K episodes and over 160 hours across 60 tasks. Our experiments show that training on human-present data elicits human-aware behaviors that robot-only data fails to produce: spatiotemporal synchronization in Collaborator tasks, yielding in Coworker tasks, and gesture grounding in Supervisor tasks. Moreover, training on HABIT enables rapid adaptation to new human-robot interaction tasks. By introducing human presence as a new axis of dataset diversity, HABIT extends robot policies to environments shared with humans.

</details>


### [86] [FastDSAC: Enhancing Policy Plasticity via Constrained Exploration for Scalable Humanoid Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31691)
*Guanchen Lu,Yajuan Dun,Yi Zhou,Letian Tao,Jingliang Duan,Jie Li,Guofa Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FastDSAC提出了一种截断高斯分布策略约束方法，通过隐式正则化缓解高吞吐量采样场景下价值方法的不稳定性和策略网络塑性损失，在MuJoCo和HumanoidBench上实现了更优的渐进性能与更快收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可扩展强化学习广泛采用高吞吐量采样架构以压缩训练时间，但数据量和更新频率的急剧增加破坏了基于价值方法的稳定性，并削弱了策略网络的塑性，亟需一种能兼顾速度与稳定性的算法。

Method: 1) 引入截断高斯分布近似所学策略，排除超出分布的异常动作以稳定目标值估计；2) 动作约束作为隐式正则化，对抗激进梯度更新导致的塑性损失；3) 采用带自适应方差调节的连续高斯表示替代离散值分布，通过采样置信且信息丰富的转移样本提升价值估计精度。

Result: 在MuJoCo Playground和HumanoidBench上的大量实验表明，FastDSAC不仅稳定了整体训练过程，而且在渐进性能和收敛速度上均优于现有最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: FastDSAC通过截断高斯策略约束和连续值分布表示，有效解决了高更新-数据比场景下的不稳定性和塑性损失问题，为快速并行采样的强化学习提供了一种稳定且高性能的解决方案。

Abstract: Scalable reinforcement learning has popularized high-throughput sampling architectures, which significantly compresses the training time for off-policy methods in robotic locomotion. However, the rapid increase of data volume and update frequency undermines the stability of value-based methods and diminishes the plasticity of policy networks. To address these challenges, this work presents FastDSAC, a fast and high-performance variant of the Distributional Actor-Critic algorithm designed for parallel sampling scenarios. Specifically, we introduce a truncated Gaussian distribution to approximate the learned policy, which effectively excludes out-of-distribution actions that strain target value estimation while keeping necessary stochasticity for exploration. The proposed action constraint functions as an implicit regularization, which counteracts the plasticity loss typically caused by aggressive gradient updates. This preservation of network adaptability enhances sample efficiency, particularly in scenarios with a high update-to-data ratio, and accelerates the early training process. In contrast to prior fast reinforcement learning approaches that rely on discrete value distributions, our method utilizes a continuous Gaussian representation equipped with adaptive variance regulation, which improves value estimation accuracy by sampling confident and informative transitions. Extensive experiments on MuJoCo Playground and HumanoidBench demonstrate that FastDSAC not only stabilizes the overall training process but also achieves superior asymptotic performance and faster convergence compared to state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [87] [RCT: A Robot-Collected Touch-Vision-Language Dataset for Tactile Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31694)
*Jingbo He,Michael Färber,Roberto Calandra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了RCT数据集，包含29,279个触觉帧，覆盖122种工业材料，揭示了现有触觉感知方法在未知材料泛化上的严重不足，并开源了数据集以推动该领域发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在开放世界操作中需要触觉表示能够泛化到未见过的材料上，但现有触觉数据集和评估方法存在接触序列泄漏问题，导致对未知材料泛化能力的评估不准确。

Method: 收集了29,279个触觉帧的机器人触觉-视觉-语言数据集RCT，使用三个DIGIT传感器在122种工业材料上执行完整按压，记录为接触序列。设计了接触序列感知的留出材料评估协议，并对比了不同数据划分策略对模型泛化性能的影响。

Result: 帧随机划分会引入接触序列重叠，使触觉到文本的Recall@1虚高17.7个百分点；留出材料后Recall@1仅25.1±6.1%；TVL/HCT公开划分中所有测试接触序列都出现在训练集中，原始像素最近邻在98.3%情况下能正确匹配序列。

Conclusion: 接触序列感知的留出材料评估是必要的，未知材料泛化是机器人触觉感知的核心挑战，RCT数据集为这一问题的可重复评估提供了基准。

Abstract: For robots manipulating open-world objects, tactile representations must generalize to unseen materials. We introduce RCT (Robotic Contact Tactile), a robot-collected touch-vision-language dataset with 29,279 tactile frames from full robot presses on 122 industrial reference materials in 7 categories, recorded with three DIGIT sensors at multiple contact positions. RCT preserves each press as a contact sequence, enabling held-out evaluation across materials, categories, sensors, contact positions, and contact sequences. Frames from one press are strongly correlated: frame-random splits can place near-duplicate observations of the same physical interaction in both training and test. With the encoder held fixed, removing contact-sequence overlap reduces tactile-to-text Recall@1 by 17.7 percentage points. When materials are additionally held out at training time, performance drops sharply, leaving held-out-material Recall@1 at 25.1 +/- 6.1% averaged over three held-out draws. The public TVL/HCT split shows the same structure: every test contact sequence appears in training, and raw-pixel nearest neighbors recover the correct sequence in 98.3% of cases. Uniformly sampling a press improves contrastive training, and RCT-trained embeddings improve category probes on unseen materials. RCT makes contact-sequence-aware, held-out-material evaluation reproducible and exposes novel-material generalization as a central challenge for robotic tactile perception. The RCT dataset is open-sourced at https://faerber-lab.github.io/RCT/

</details>


### [88] [UniTacVLA: Unified Tactile Understanding and Prediction in Vision Language Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31723)
*Xidong Zhang,Yichi Zhang,Jiaxin Shi,Fucai Zhu,Siyu Zhu,Michael Yu Wang,Xiaojun Wu,Weihao Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出统一触觉学习框架VTLA，通过触觉链式推理和粗到细的未来触觉预测构建状态与动态感知的触觉先验，并设计触觉-动作混合控制器，在接触丰富操作任务中显著提升成功率、精度和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在接触丰富灵巧操作中表现有限，而VTLA方法虽引入触觉感知，但仅将其作为被动辅助输入，难以建模触觉语义和未来物理交互。

Method: 构建统一触觉潜在空间，联合建模当前触觉状态和未来接触变化（触觉链式推理+粗到细未来触觉预测），形成状态感知和动态感知的触觉先验，再通过触觉-动作混合控制器结合实时与预测触觉反馈对低频动作块进行高频修正。

Result: 在调整、插入、擦拭、装配四类接触丰富任务上，无论是在干净环境还是外部扰动下，该方法在成功率、操作精度和接触鲁棒性上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 将触觉信号建模为动态交互线索，结合触觉预测与混合控制，能有效提升灵巧物理交互的性能。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have achieved strong performance in many robotic manipulation tasks, yet remain limited in contact-rich dexterous manipulation. To overcome this limitation, recent vision-tactile-language-action (VTLA) methods incorporate tactile sensing into VLA models to provide direct contact information. However, they typically treat tactile signals as passive auxiliary inputs, making it difficult to model tactile semantics and future physical interactions. To this end, we propose a unified tactile learning framework for contact-rich manipulation that models tactile signals as dynamic interaction cues for both contact understanding and prediction. Specifically, we construct a unified tactile latent space and jointly model current tactile states and future contact changes through tactile chain-of-thought reasoning and coarse-to-fine future tactile prediction, thereby forming a state-aware and dynamics-aware tactile prior. Based on this prior, we introduce a tactile-action mixed controller that combines real-time and predicted tactile feedback to refine low-frequency action chunks with high-frequency corrections. Real-world experiments on four categories of contact-rich tasks, including adjustment, insertion, wiping, and assembly, under both clean and externally perturbed settings, show that our method improves success rate, manipulation accuracy, and contact robustness over existing methods, demonstrating its effectiveness in dexterous physical interaction.

</details>


### [89] [Autonomous UAV Navigation for Individual Wildlife Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31772)
*Claire Sun,Tanya Berger-Wolf,Jenna Kline*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种自主无人机导航系统，该系统通过YOLOv11目标检测和DINOv2姿态分类器实时优化图像捕获，满足下游野生动物个体重识别（re-ID）的图像质量需求，并在斑马、长颈鹿、老虎和大象等物种上验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野生动物个体重识别（re-ID）对种群监测和行为追踪至关重要，但传统数据收集依赖生态学家或公民科学家的人工努力，劳动密集且难以大规模开展。

Method: 结合YOLOv11目标检测与DINOv2姿态分类器，实时指导无人机飞行决策：检测动物、调整机位以暴露侧腹（模式识别关键表面）、逼近至满足最小边界框阈值。

Result: 通过在肯尼亚收集的斑马影像进行案例研究，验证了系统可行性，并证明该方法可泛化至长颈鹿、老虎和大象等其他具有诊断性表面图案的物种。

Conclusion: 建立了任务感知型具身AI用于生态数据收集的框架，使下游re-ID需求驱动实时感知与控制，实现了从被动航空感知到主动优化图像捕获的范式转变。

Abstract: Reliable individual re-identification (re-ID) of wildlife is essential for population monitoring, behavioral tracking, and conservation policy evaluation, yet large-scale data collection remains labor-intensive, relying on manual efforts by ecologists or citizen scientists. We propose an autonomous drone navigation system that actively optimizes image capture for downstream re-ID, moving beyond passive aerial sensing. The system combines YOLOv11 object detection with a DINOv2-based pose classifier to guide real-time flight decisions: detecting animals, orienting to expose the lateral flank (the surface of interest for pattern-based re-ID), and approaching until the subject meets a minimum bounding-box threshold. Unlike prior drone systems that optimize for group-level behavioral video, ours targets the specific image-quality requirements of individual-identification models. We demonstrate feasibility through a case study on zebra using footage collected in Kenya, and show the approach generalizes to other species with diagnostic surface patterns, including giraffes, tigers, and elephants. Our work establishes a framework for task-aware embodied AI for ecological data collection, in which downstream re-ID requirements drive real-time perception and control.

</details>


### [90] [Reinforcement Learning-Based Control for an Inline Skating Humanoid Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31807)
*Ethan Marot,Thomas Bi,Clemens Schwarke,Victor Klemm,Marco Hutter,Raffaello D'Andrea*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过强化学习训练控制策略，使装备被动直排轮滑鞋的人形机器人实现了动态、高效的滑行运动，无需人类数据或模仿学习，成功将策略零样本迁移到真实硬件，相比步行能耗降低50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人结合被动直排轮滑可以实现高速、节能的运动，但被动轮的固有不稳定性和仿真接触伪影是主要挑战，现有研究局限于四足机器人或主动驱动轮。

Method: 使用强化学习训练控制策略，采用球形和椭球形几何轮模型分别用于训练和验证以克服仿真接触问题，设计基于成功率的指令课程和专用滚动奖励，对装备消费级直排轮滑鞋的Booster T1人形机器人进行6自由度精准控制。

Result: 策略相比步行步态降低50%的运输成本（CoT），零样本迁移到物理硬件，实现动态平衡、抵抗主动物理扰动以及高速转弯等敏捷运动。

Conclusion: 无需人类运动数据或先验知识，仅通过奖励设计即可使强化学习策略涌现出高效的轮滑运动策略，并成功部署于真实人形机器人。

Abstract: As humanoid robots become increasingly dynamic, coupling them with reinforcement learning offers a promising approach to solving the complex, underactuated mechanics of passive inline skating. Equipping a humanoid robot with passive inline skating wheels presents an opportunity to combine the versatile agility of humanoids with the high-speed, energy-efficient locomotion strategies utilized by human skaters. In this paper, we train and deploy a reinforcement learning control policy that enables novel locomotion strategies for a humanoid robot modified to equip consumer inline skates instead of conventional feet. Unlike previous work limited to quadrupedal robots or actively driven wheels, our system allows for precise 6-DoF control of the skates to execute dynamic, edge-driven propulsion strategies. Our skating strategies emerge entirely from our reward structure, without reliance on human motion data, imitation learning, or kinematic priors. We overcome the inherent instability of passive wheels and simulation contact artifacts by utilizing different geometric wheel models (spherical and ellipsoidal) during training and validation, along with a custom success-based command curriculum and a specialized rolling reward. Consequently, our policy demonstrates up to a 50% reduction in Cost of Transport (CoT) compared to standard walking gaits. The resulting policy successfully transfers zero-shot to the physical Booster T1 hardware. Real-world deployments demonstrate dynamic balance, the ability to reject active physical perturbations, and agile locomotion strategies capable of turning at speed. A video of our results can be found at https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-_APcOS7uFo.

</details>


### [91] [RoboTacDex: A Dexterous Visual-Tactile-Action Dataset for Humanoid Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31836)
*Xinyi Wang,Donghan Li,Zi'Ang Chen,Chong Yu,Chen Xin,Peng Ye,Yingkai Sun,Tao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboTacDex是一个基于人形机器人Unitree G1的大规模多模态灵巧操作数据集，包含6k条轨迹、19个任务、23种技能和22个物体，提供多视角RGB、深度、触觉反馈和语义标注，旨在推动机器人学习中的灵巧操作研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模、多样化的演示轨迹是提升机器人操作能力的基础，但目前缺乏面向人形机器人双臂灵巧手操作的大规模多模态数据集。

Method: 基于Unitree G1人形机器人，收集了6k条轨迹，覆盖19个任务、23种技能和22个物体；采用改进的多相机同步系统实现毫秒级数据同步，记录多视角RGB、深度、触觉反馈和详细语义标注。

Result: 在数据集上评估了三种代表性模仿学习模型，分析了它们在各类任务上的性能、优势和局限；成功试验结果和适度的泛化能力验证了数据集的有效性和多样性。

Conclusion: RoboTacDex作为大规模多模态灵巧操作数据集，能够有效支持机器人模仿学习研究，数据集将开源。

Abstract: In the field of robot learning, large-scale and diverse demonstration trajectories provide the fundamental basis for enhancing robotic manipulation ability. We introduce RoboTacDex, a large, multi-modal, and diverse dataset of dexterous manipulation behaviors performed with a humanoid robot. Built on the publicly accessible humanoid robot Unitree G1, RoboTacDex consists of 6k trajectories covering 19 tasks, 23 skills, and interactions with 22 objects. RoboTacDex provides comprehensive records including multi-view RGB and depth information, tactile feedback, and detailed semantic annotations. Furthermore, the dataset features a variety of relatively challenging tasks that can only be completed by dual arms and dexterous hands, aiming to mimic human-like operational logic and simulate real-world manipulation complexity. To ensure data collection quality, we develop an improved multi-camera synchronization system to enable millisecond data synchronization and recording of modalities. In our experiments, we evaluate three representative imitation learning models on our dataset,
  analyzing their performance as well as their respective strengths and limitations across different task categories. Successful trial results and a moderate level of generalization capabilities across a suite of tasks indicate the effectiveness and diversity of the collected dataset. Our dataset will be open-sourced soon.

</details>


### [92] [Bridging Local Observation and Global Simulation in Closed-Loop Traffic Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31844)
*Ziyan Wang,Tan Xiang,Peng Chen,Xintao Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CRAFT提出了一种自监督的偏好对齐框架，通过发现训练时的局部上下文与部署时的全局上下文不匹配问题，在测试时对自回归交通模拟器进行行为矫正，在不重新训练的情况下显著降低碰撞和交通违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归交通模拟器在基于自我中心驾驶日志训练时，由于感知限制和遮挡，周围智能体仅被部分观测，导致模型学到不完整的上下文-动作映射。这种局部到全局的上下文不匹配在闭环部署时才会显现，引发异常停车、不安全交互和违反交规等不真实行为。

Method: CRAFT框架包含三个阶段：1）将基础模拟器作为全局可观测沙盒，从日志初始状态生成多样化的假设性闭环展开，暴露上下文引起的失败案例；2）用人类对齐的驾驶先验对这些失败进行归因，转化为偏好监督信号，训练上下文偏好评估器（CPE）；3）在推理时，CPE作为即插即用的对齐模块，在完整场景上下文下对候选动作评分，并重新加权自回归解码过程以偏向全局连贯的行为。

Result: 在不重新训练基础模拟器的情况下，碰撞减少31.2%，交通违规减少33.2%。

Conclusion: CRAFT通过自监督的失败发现和测试时偏好对齐，有效缓解了局部到全局的上下文偏差，显著提升了交通模拟器的行为真实性和安全性。

Abstract: A local-to-global context mismatch arises when autoregressive traffic simulators trained on ego-centric driving logs are deployed in globally observable closed-loop environments. In such logs, the ego vehicle has rich local observations, while surrounding agents are only partially observed due to perception limits and occlusions. As a result, simulators may learn incomplete context--action mappings that remain hidden in log-based training but emerge during closed-loop rollouts, leading to unrealistic behaviors such as abnormal stops, unsafe interactions, and rule violations. We propose CRAFT, a Contextual pReference Alignment Framework for Traffic Simulation, to mitigate this mismatch via self-supervised failure discovery and preference-guided test-time alignment. CRAFT treats the base simulator as a globally observable sandbox, generating diverse what-if rollouts from logged initial states to expose context-induced failures. These failures are grounded with human-aligned driving priors and converted into preference supervision for training a Contextual Preference Evaluator (CPE). At inference time, CPE acts as a plug-in alignment module that scores candidate actions under complete scene context and reweights autoregressive decoding toward globally coherent behaviors. CRAFT mitigates this local-to-global contextual bias, reducing collisions by 31.2\% and traffic violations by 33.2\% without retraining the base simulator.

</details>


### [93] [Z-1: Efficient Reinforcement Learning for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31846)
*Lang Cao,Renhong Chen,Luyi Li,Peng Wang,Mofan Peng,Yitong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Z-1 提出了一种基于 GRPO 的强化学习后训练框架，在 π₀.₅ 基础上通过共享前缀 rollout 构建、树状轨迹分支、完成感知奖励校准和 VLM 与 Action Expert 选择性联合训练等优化，在 24 个 RoboCasa 任务上达到 80.6% 的平均成功率，比 SFT 初始化提升 13.2 个百分点，超越已有 SOTA 模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型大多依赖行为克隆或监督微调（SFT）从固定演示中学习，缺乏从自身失败中改进的机会，限制了策略的进一步提升。

Method: 基于 π₀.₅ 流式 VLA 模型，使用公开 RoboCasa 演示进行 SFT 初始化，然后采用任务级 GRPO 策略进行强化学习后训练。为提升在线优化效率和稳定性，结合了共享前缀 rollout 构建、树状轨迹分支、完成感知奖励校准以及 VLM 与 Action Expert 的选择性联合训练。

Result: 在全部 24 个 RoboCasa 任务上，平均成功率 80.6%，较 SFT 初始化提升 13.2 个百分点，超越已发表 SOTA 模型。

Conclusion: 系统性的 GRPO 后训练能够在无需额外私有演示的情况下显著提升流式 VLA 策略的性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models offer a promising framework for robotic manipulation by connecting language instructions, visual observations, and continuous control. However, most existing policies remain limited by behavior cloning or supervised fine-tuning (SFT) from fixed demonstrations, which provides limited opportunity to improve from the policy's own failures. In this paper, we present Z-1, a reinforcement learning (RL) post-training framework for flow-based VLA models. Built on top of $π_{0.5}$, Z-1 uses only publicly released RoboCasa demonstrations for SFT and then applies a task-wise Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) strategy across $24$ standard RoboCasa tasks. To improve the efficiency and stability of online optimization, Z-1 combines shared-prefix rollout construction, tree-structured trajectory branching, completion-aware reward calibration, and selective joint training of VLM and Action Expert. Across all $24$ RoboCasa tasks, Z-1 achieves an average success rate of $80.6\%$, improving over its SFT initialization by $13.2\%$ points and outperforms the published sota models. These results show that systematic GRPO post-training can substantially improve flow-based VLA policies without additional private demonstrations.

</details>


### [94] [Improving path-tracking performance of an articulated tractor-trailer system using a non-linear kinematic model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31889)
*Marina Murillo,Guido Sanchez,Nestor Deniz,Lucas Genzelis,Leonardo Giovanini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种铰接式拖拉机-拖车系统的非线性数学模型，结合滚动时域技术，通过非线性模型预测控制器(NMPC)控制拖车位置，在直线路径和地头转弯中显著减少了拖车位置偏差，实现了对拖车位置本身的精确控制，而非仅控制拖拉机位置。研究在ROS和Gazebo仿真环境中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精密农业中小田块的播种、喷洒和收割作业需要高精度的路径跟踪能力，而铰接式拖拉机-拖车系统因其双转向机构具有优越的机动性，但现有模型对拖车位置控制精度不足，需要更精确的数学模型来提升路径跟踪性能。

Method: 提出铰接式拖拉机-拖车系统的非线性数学模型，将其嵌入非线性模型预测控制器(NMPC)中，利用滚动时域优化技术进行路径跟踪控制，在ROS框架和Gazebo仿真器中实现真实感仿真验证。

Result: 考虑拖车运动学后，拖车位置偏差在直线路径和地头转弯中均显著降低，实现了对拖车位置本身而非仅拖拉机位置的有效控制。

Conclusion: 所提出的非线性数学模型结合NMPC能够有效提升铰接式拖拉机-拖车系统在精密农业场景下的路径跟踪精度，为农业自动化作业提供了关键技术支撑。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel non-linear mathematical model of an articulated tractor-trailer system that can be used, in combination with receding horizon techniques, to improve the performance of path tracking tasks of articulated systems. Due to its dual steering mechanisms, this type of vehicle can be very useful in precision agriculture, particularly for seeding, spraying and harvesting in small fields. The articulated tractor-trailer system model was embedded within a non-linear model predictive controller and the trailer position was monitored. When the kinematic of the trailer was considered, the deviation of trailer's position was reduced substantially alongside not only straight paths but also in headland turns. Using the proposed mathematical model, we were able to control the trailer's position itself rather than the tractor's position. The Robot Operating System (ROS) framework and Gazebo simulator were used to perform realistic simulations examples.

</details>


### [95] [CoDex: Learning Compositional Dexterous Functional Manipulation without Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31909)
*Bowen Jiang,William Painter Reger,Roberto Martin-Martin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CoDex框架，利用视觉语言模型引导的约束优化和强化学习，使机器人能够在零演示条件下自主发现并执行组合性灵巧功能性物体操作（如按压喷雾瓶、使用胶枪等），并在真实世界多种物体上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合性灵巧功能性物体操作（CD-FOM）任务需要同时理解物体功能、驱动方式和应用区域，并结合灵巧操作能力来控制抓取稳定性、运动轨迹和驱动力，对机器人构成了重大挑战，现有方法缺乏无需人类演示即可自主发现操作策略的能力。

Method: CoDex框架采用零演示方式，首先使用视觉语言模型（VLMs）从任务和场景中推断语义约束，然后利用这些约束引导分析性约束优化生成少量功能性抓取候选，最后通过强化学习高效优化生成完整的"抓取-移动-驱动"策略，实现从仿真到真实世界的迁移。

Result: 在7自由度机器人臂和16自由度多指手上对六种CD-FOM任务（包括喷雾瓶、热胶枪、空气除尘器、手电筒和胡椒研磨器等带有内部机制的物体）进行评估，展示了无需人类演示即可自主发现并执行复杂、物理可行的灵巧行为的能力。

Conclusion: CoDex框架成功实现了零演示条件下的组合性灵巧功能性物体操作，为机器人自主发现复杂操作策略提供了有效途径。

Abstract: In this work, we study Compositional Dexterous Functional Object Manipulation (CD-FOM): tasks such as aiming and actuating a spray bottle on a plant or a glue gun on wood, which require both actuating an object's internal mechanism and controlling its pose to apply the object's function to the environment. These tasks pose significant challenges for robots due to the demanding integration of semantic understanding of the object's function, actuation mode, and application area with intricate physical dexterity to manage grasp stability, movement trajectory, and actuation. We introduce CoDex, a zero-demonstration framework that autonomously discovers CD-FOM manipulation strategies. CoDex uses vision-language models (VLMs) to infer semantic constraints from the task and scene. These constraints guide analytic constrained optimization to generate a short list of functional grasp candidates that can be efficiently refined with reinforcement learning to generate full grasp-move-actuate policies transferable from simulation to the real world. We evaluate CoDex on a 7-DoF robot arm with a 16-DoF multi-fingered hand across six CD-FOM tasks involving previously unseen objects with internal mechanisms, including spray bottles, hot glue guns, air dusters, flashlights, and pepper grinders, and their application to unseen target objects, showcasing its ability to autonomously discover and execute complex, physically viable dexterous behaviors without human demonstrations. More information at https://robin-lab.cs.utexas.edu/CoDex/.

</details>


### [96] [Learning Locomotion on Discrete Terrain via Minimal Proximity Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31912)
*Jiale Fan,Connor Flynn,Tianao Xu,Junzhe He,Andrei Cramariuc,Marco Hutter,Robert Baines*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探索在四足机器人足部嵌入低成本、高频红外接近传感器，提供“预接触”反馈，结合强化学习框架，使机器人能够提前感知地形不连续性（如间隙、踏脚石），在离散地形上显著提升行走鲁棒性，作为全局视觉感知的低功耗、低延迟替代或补充方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有四足机器人地形导航中，全局感知（LiDAR、深度相机）存在延迟、遮挡和高计算成本问题，而本体感知（proprioceptive）仅在接触发生后才能做出反应。因此需要一种低延迟、低计算、抗遮挡的预接触感知方案。

Method: 在四足机器人足部嵌入低成本红外接近传感器（ToF），提供高频“预接触”反馈；将局部稀疏信号集成到强化学习（RL）框架中，在仿真中建模并迁移至真实环境。

Result: 实验表明，局部接近感知显著提升了四足机器人在离散地形（如间隙、踏脚石）上的穿越鲁棒性，且具备低功耗、低延迟优势，可作为复杂全局感知的有效替代或补充。

Conclusion: 稀疏的近场传感可在仿真中可靠建模并高保真迁移到真实世界，为四足机器人在不可预测环境中的鲁棒导航提供了一种实用且高效的感知方案。

Abstract: Learning-based control has revolutionized dynamic locomotion, yet navigating unstructured terrain remains limited by a robot's incomplete awareness of imminent ground contact. While global perception systems such as LiDARs and depth cameras provide environmental context, they are frequently plagued by latencies, occlusions, and the high computational cost of dense geometric reconstruction. On the other hand, proprioceptive feedback is purely reactive, initiating corrections only after impact has occurred. This work explores embedding a minimal suite of low-cost, high-frequency infrared proximity sensors directly into the feet of a quadrupedal robot. These sensors provide "pre-contact" feedback that is robust to self-occlusions and significantly less computationally demanding than conventional vision-based pipelines. By integrating these localized signals into a reinforcement learning framework, we enable the robot to anticipate terrain discontinuities such as gaps and stepping stones that are problematic for traditional perception stacks due to occlusions or state estimation drift. We demonstrate that such sparse, near-field sensing can be reliably modeled in simulation and transferred to the real world with high fidelity. Experimental results show that local proximity sensing substantially improves traversal robustness over discrete terrain and offers a low-power, low-latency alternative or complement to complex global perception suites in unpredictable environments. For more information about results and methods, please see the project website: https://sites.google.com/view/foot-tof/home.

</details>


### [97] [MVP-Nav: Multi-layer Value Map Planner Navigator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31919)
*Wenyuan Xie,Shaokai Wu,Yijin Zhou,Yanbiao Ji,Guodong Zhang,Bayram Bayramli,Qiuchang Li,Xunchu Zhou,Yue Ding,Hongtao Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MVP-Nav提出了一种基于RGB-only感知的物理感知导航框架，通过3D基础模型从单目图像重建显式物理占用，并引入多层价值图（MVM）统一语义推理与物理约束，在零样本物体目标导航任务中达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本物体目标导航方法在仅使用RGB感知时，由于缺乏深度信息导致严重的物理不确定性和语义-物理错位，要么依赖高层语义推理而缺乏几何基础，要么学习端到端策略但缺少显式物理约束，常产生语义合理但物理不安全的导航行为。

Method: 1) 利用3D基础模型将2D语义实例投影为3D有向边界框，从单目观测重建显式物理占用，构建全局空间语义表示；2) 提出多层价值图（MVM），将语义优先级与重建几何集成到共享代价空间，实现物理基础的几何规划。整体框架对齐感知、规划和控制与真实3D世界。

Result: 在零样本物体目标导航基准上，MVP-Nav显著优于现有深度无关方法，达到SOTA性能，验证了结构化物理先验可以有效补偿主动深度传感器的缺失。

Conclusion: 结构化物理先验可以有效补偿主动深度传感器的缺失，为解决RGB-only导航中物理不确定性问题提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Zero-shot Object Goal Navigation (ZSON) with RGB-only perception poses a fundamental challenge for embodied agents, as the absence of explicit depth information introduces severe physical uncertainty and semantic-physical misalignment. Existing approaches either rely on high-level semantic reasoning without geometric grounding or learn end-to-end policies that lack explicit physical constraints, often resulting in semantically plausible but physically unsafe behaviors. In this paper, we propose MVP-Nav, a physical-aware RGB-only navigation framework that aligns perception, planning, and control with the real 3D world. MVP-Nav reconstructs explicit physical occupancy from monocular observations by leveraging 3D foundation models to project 2D semantic instances into 3D oriented bounding boxes, forming a global spatial semantic representation. To unify high-level semantic reasoning and low-level physical constraints, we introduce a Multi-layer Value Map (MVM) that integrates semantic priorities and reconstructed geometry into a shared cost space, enabling physically grounded geometric planning. Extensive experiments on zero-shot object navigation benchmarks demonstrate that MVP-Nav significantly outperforms existing depth-free methods, achieving state-of-the-art performance and validating that structured physical priors can effectively compensate for the absence of active depth sensors.

</details>


### [98] [LeCropFollow: Latent Space Planning for Navigation in Unstructured Crop Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31941)
*Felipe Tommaselli,Francisco Affonso,Arthur Pompeu,Gianluca Capezzuto,Arun Narenthiran Sivakumar,Girish Chowdhary,Marcelo Becker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LeCropFollow提出了一种规避显式几何建模的视觉导航框架，通过自监督语义热图提取器结合TD-MPC2模型强化学习规划器，在潜在流形中直接优化轨迹，实现了从简化仿真到真实世界的零样本迁移，在非结构化农田场景中相比基于关键点的方法语义失败率降低2.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有几何方法将高维视觉数据压缩为确定性空间参考，丢弃了导航非结构化地形所需的不确定性和语义上下文，导致在不规则种植或间断等非结构化场景中频繁失效。

Method: 采用自监督语义热图提取器提取非压缩的热图信号，保留语义上下文；结合TD-MPC2模型强化学习规划器在潜在流形中直接优化轨迹，避免几何降维的信息损失。

Result: 在结构化行间与基线性能持平，在种植间隙场景中语义失败率相比基于关键点的方法降低2.4倍，且实现从简化仿真到物理世界的零样本迁移。

Conclusion: 潜在空间规划策略在异质性农业环境中可作为几何估计方法的鲁棒替代方案。

Abstract: Unstructured navigational features, such as irregular planting or discontinuities, remain the primary failure mode for under-canopy agricultural robots. Existing geometric approaches often fail in these scenarios because they compress high-dimensional visual data into deterministic spatial references, effectively discarding the uncertainty and semantic context required to navigate ambiguous terrain. To address this, we present LeCropFollow, a visual navigation framework that bypasses explicit geometric modeling in favor of a learned latent representation. By integrating a self-supervised semantic heatmap extractor with TD-MPC2, a Model-Based Reinforcement Learning (MBRL) planner, our system optimizes trajectories directly within a latent manifold. The framework operates over the uncompressed heatmap signal, preserving the semantic context that geometric reductions discard. We demonstrate that this representational shift enables zero-shot transfer from simplified simulation to the physical world without fine-tuning. Extensive field experiments in late-stage corn fields show that LeCropFollow matches state-of-the-art baselines in unstructured rows but significantly outperforms them in plantation gaps, achieving a 2.4x reduction in semantic failures compared to keypoint-based methods. These results suggest that latent planning offers a robust alternative to geometric estimation for operations in heterogeneous agricultural environments. Code, models, and data available: https://felipe-tommaselli.github.io/lecropfollow .

</details>


### [99] [RRT-Rope: A deterministic shortening approach for fast near-optimal path planning in large-scale uncluttered 3D environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31948)
*Louis Petit,Alexis Lussier Desbiens*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RRT-Rope算法，通过改进RRT-Connect快速生成可行路径，再结合确定性捷径技术进行后处理，在大型3D无杂波环境（如地下采矿巷道）中显著降低路径代价和计算时间，比次优算法快70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路径规划算法（如Informed RRT*, RRT#）在大型3D无杂波环境中收敛时间过长，而RRT的捷径化虽已被证明有效但仍有改进空间，需要一种能在极短时间内找到近优解的方法。

Method: 采用改进版RRT-Connect快速计算可行路径，随后利用树中各分支添加的中间节点进行确定性捷径后处理（RRT-Rope）。

Result: 在所有仿真环境中，RRT-Rope在路径代价和计算时间上均优于其他流行的RRT变体和缩短技术，在代表性采场中比次优算法快70%。

Conclusion: RRT-Rope通过快速可行路径生成加确定性捷径后处理的组合策略，在大型3D无杂波环境中实现了近优解的高效求解，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Many path planning algorithms have been introduced so far, but most are costly, in path cost and in processing time, in large-scale uncluttered 3D environments such as underground mining stopes explored by an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV). Rapidly-exploring Random Tree (RRT) algorithms are popular because of their probabilistic completeness and rapidity in finding a feasible path in single-query problems. Many of the algorithms (e.g. Informed RRT*, RRT#) developed to improve RRT need considerable time to converge in large environments. Shortcutting an RRT is an old idea that has been proven to outperform RRT variants. This paper introduces a new method, RRT-Rope, that aims at finding a near-optimal solution in a drastically shorter amount of time. The proposed approach benefits from fast computation of a feasible path with an altered version of RRT-connect, and post-processes it quickly with a deterministic shortcutting technique, taking advantage of intermediate nodes added to each branch of the tree. This paper presents simulations and statistics carried out to show the efficiency of RRT-Rope, which gives better results in terms of path cost and computation time than other popular RRT variations and shortening techniques in all our simulation environments, and is up to 70% faster than the next best algorithm in a representative stope.

</details>


### [100] [Adapting Generalist Robot Policies with Semantic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31958)
*Jagdeep Singh Bhatia,Andrew Wagenmaker,William Chen,Sergey Levine*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出语义动作强化学习（SARL），通过优化语言提示空间而非直接优化机器人动作，使通用策略模型能够通过在线交互学习解决复杂长程任务，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略虽然通过大规模预训练具备了丰富的技能，但在下游任务中直接使用标准强化学习方法优化动作空间效果不佳，因为对于复杂或长程任务，初始策略的动作分布与最优策略相差甚远。

Method: 提出SARL框架，将通用策略视为可控技能先验，通过在线交互学习优化语言提示空间（而非动作空间），利用自然语言提示来组合和调用策略中已有的技能，从而实现结构化且有语义意义的探索。

Result: 在真实世界设置和模拟基准测试中，SARL能够使VLA策略适应解决复杂的、长程的任务，显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 对于足够表达能力强的通用策略，语言提示是学习解决复杂任务的有效替代空间；通过语义动作强化学习，可以解锁通用机器人策略的新能力，使其超越零样本能力范围。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies learn a diverse repertoire of behaviors from large-scale pretraining. In principle, this makes them excellent priors for downstream adaptation via reinforcement learning (RL). In practice, however, standard RL methods leveraging this prior optimize directly over robot actions, requiring the base policy's action distribution to be close to that of a performant policy from the start. This assumption breaks down for complex or long-horizon tasks that fall outside the pretraining distribution. Our key insight is that, for sufficiently expressive generalist policies, language prompts are an effective alternative space for learning to solve such tasks: modulating language inputs elicits skills already within the policy's repertoire, which can be composed to solve tasks beyond its zero-shot capabilities. We propose Semantic Action Reinforcement Learning (SARL), which learns to optimize this prompt space through online interaction, treating the generalist policy as a controllable skill prior. Importantly, leveraging pretrained skills rather than learning new ones from scratch yields structured, semantically meaningful exploration and highly efficient online improvement, and learning to modulate prompts through experience grounds them in induced real-world behaviors for robust task-solving. Across real-world settings and simulated benchmarks, we show SARL unlocks fundamentally new capabilities -- adapting VLA behavior to solve complex, long-horizon tasks -- and significantly outperforms existing approaches for improving robot behavior in deployment.

</details>


### [101] [OopsieVerse: A Safety Benchmark with Damage-Aware Simulation for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31993)
*Arnav Balaji,Arpit Bahety,Sriniket Ambatipudi,Daniel Lam,Junhong Xu,Roberto Martín-Martín*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OOPSIEVERSE是一个统一的仿真框架和基准测试平台，用于损伤感知的家居操作。它通过DAMAGESIM组件在仿真中显式检测和量化机械、热和流体损伤，支持多种物理引擎，可应用于安全演示收集、策略学习、VLA策略安全基准测试和Sim-to-Real迁移，为安全机器人操作提供开源基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人操作仿真器缺乏通用的机制来检测、量化和表示损伤，导致物理安全问题成为部署家用机器人的主要障碍。仿真虽可避免真实训练的高成本和危险，但现有工具无法提供损伤反馈信号。

Method: 提出OOPSIEVERSE框架，核心包含：(1) DAMAGESIM——一个与仿真器无关的损伤检测与量化框架，将接触力、温度变化和液体交互等物理信号转化为机械、热或流体损伤；(2) 一套面向常见损伤模式的家务任务基准。该框架在OmniGibson（Nvidia Omniverse）和RoboCasa（MuJoCo）两种不同物理后端上实例化验证。

Result: 展示了框架在多个用例中的有效性：(1) 通过实时损伤反馈引导更安全的演示收集；(2) 通过损伤条件模仿学习和强化学习学习更安全的操作策略；(3) 对最先进的视觉-语言-动作(VLA)策略进行安全性基准测试；(4) 提高Sim-to-Real迁移策略的真实世界安全性。

Conclusion: OOPSIEVERSE可作为系统化、可扩展的安全机器人操作研究的开源基础，为仿真中损伤感知能力的标准化提供统一框架。

Abstract: While robotic manipulation capabilities have advanced rapidly, physical safety remains a major barrier to deploying household robots: task success is insufficient if the robot damages itself or its surroundings. Simulation offers a harm-free alternative to costly and dangerous real-world training and evaluation, yet existing simulators lack general mechanisms to detect, quantify, and represent damage. To address this gap, we introduce OOPSIEVERSE, a unified simulation framework and benchmark for damage-aware household manipulation. OOPSIEVERSE provides damage as an explicit, physically-grounded, and taskagnostic signal by converting sources such as contact forces, temperature changes, and liquid interactions into corresponding mechanical, thermal or fluid damage. OOPSIEVERSE comprises two core elements: (1) DAMAGESIM, a simulator-agnostic framework for detecting and quantifying damage during navigation and manipulation, and (2) a suite of household tasks designed to evaluate common damage modes and distinguish between task completion and safe execution. We demonstrate the generality of our framework by instantiating DAMAGESIM in two simulators with different physics backends, OmniGibson (Nvidia Omniverse) and RoboCasa (MuJoCo). We further showcase the utility of OOPSIEVERSE across multiple use cases, including (1) guiding safer demonstration collection via real-time damage feedback, (2) learning safer manipulation policies through damage-conditioned imitation learning and reinforcement learning, (3) benchmarking the safety of state-of-the-art Vision Language Action policies, and (4) improving real-world safety of sim-to-real transferred policies. Together, our results highlight the potential of OOPSIEVERSE as an open-source foundation for systematic, scalable research on safe robot manipulation. For code and more information, please refer to https://robin-lab.cs.utexas.edu/oopsieverse/

</details>


### [102] [Human-as-Humanoid: Enabling Zero-Shot Humanoid Learning from Ego-Exo Human Videos with Human-Aligned Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32009)
*Xiaopeng Lin,Ruoqi Yang,Shijie Lian,Zhaolong Shen,Bin Yu,Changti Wu,Haibao Liu,Yuxiang Zhang,Hong Li,Qiyuan Su,Haochen Liu,Xuguo He,Yukun Shi,Cong Huang,Zhirui Zhang,Bojun Cheng,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Human-as-Humanoid框架，通过同步ego-exo视频、运动重定向、分段逆运动学和正运动学感知监督，将人类演示视频转化为可用于高自由度人形机器人VLA模型训练的观测-动作监督，实现4.8-7.2倍数据采集效率提升，且无需目标任务机器人演示即可泛化到真实部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型依赖高质量观测-动作监督数据，但为高自由度人形机器人扩展此类数据极其困难；遥操作效率低，而人类第一人称视频虽包含丰富双人操作信息却不直接提供可执行动作。

Method: 1) 使用PrimeU（60-DoF上半身人形机器人）；2) 同步ego-exo视频配对部署对齐的自我中心观测与外中心运动恢复；3) 通过分段逆运动学将恢复的人类运动重定向为控制器对齐的60-DoF动作块；4) 使用正运动学感知监督训练VLA模型以保留腕部和指尖任务空间几何结构。

Result: 与人类遥操作相比，原始演示吞吐量提升4.8-7.2倍；在多个下游任务中，仅使用转换后的人类标签进行后训练的策略无需目标任务的机器人演示即可泛化到真实机器人部署。

Conclusion: Human-as-Humanoid成功将大规模人类演示从视觉观察转化为目标人形机器人可执行的观测-动作监督数据，有效解决了高自由度人形机器人训练数据稀缺问题。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models across robot embodiments require high-quality observation--action supervision to learn deployable action distributions, yet scaling such robot data remains difficult, especially for high-DoF humanoids. Teleoperation provides controller-aligned supervision, while human egocentric videos capture diverse bimanual manipulation but do not directly provide executable robot actions. We introduce Human-as-Humanoid, a human-to-humanoid supervision framework that enables near-real-time human-centric action generation, making human demonstrations usable for high-DoF humanoid VLA training by jointly aligning the robot embodiment, the sensing setup, and the action-label interface. Built on PrimeU, a human-aligned 60-DoF upper-body humanoid, Human-as-Humanoid uses synchronized ego-exo videos to pair deployment-aligned egocentric observations with exocentric motion recovery, retargets the recovered human motion through staged Inverse Kinematics (IK) into controller-aligned 60-DoF action chunks, and trains the VLA model with Forward Kinematics (FK)-aware supervision to preserve wrist and fingertip task-space geometry. This converts large-scale human demonstrations from visual observations into executable observation--action supervision for the target humanoid. Experiments validate the conversion chain at the motion-recovery, robot-action-space, and real-robot deployment levels. Human-as-Humanoid yields a 4.8--7.2x raw demonstration-throughput gain over humanoid teleoperation in our data-collection analysis, and on several downstream tasks, policies post-trained only with the converted human labels generalize to real-robot deployment without target-task robot demonstrations. The official project website is available at https://zgc-embodyai.github.io/Human-as-Humanoid.

</details>


### [103] [Freeform Preference Learning for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32027)
*Marcel Torne,Anubha Mahajan,Abhijnya Bhat,Chelsea Finn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FPL是一种从自由形式人类偏好中学习机器人策略的方法，通过自然语言定义偏好轴（如速度、安全性等）并收集沿各轴的成对偏好，学习语言条件奖励模型，最终训练出可沿多维优化且支持测试时行为引导的奖励条件策略。在多项长时程操作任务中，FPL比稀疏奖励和二元偏好方法提升38个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励设计是自主机器人策略提升的核心瓶颈。长时程操作任务中，稀疏成功标签信号过弱，二元偏好则将多种竞争性的质量概念压缩为一个模糊信号，无法捕捉不同维度的细微差异。

Method: FPL允许标注者定义自然语言偏好轴（如速度、安全性、放置质量、细心程度），并为每个轴提供成对偏好。利用这些标注学习语言条件奖励模型，将轨迹和偏好标签映射为轴特定奖励。进而训练奖励条件策略，沿多个人类指定的维度进行优化。

Result: 在四个真实世界和两个模拟的长时程操作任务中，FPL相比稀疏奖励和二元偏好方法提升38个百分点。FPL无需显式子任务分割即可学习密集进度信号，展现出数据中未出现的行为组合性，并允许用户在测试时引导策略走向不同行为而无需重新训练。

Conclusion: FPL通过自由形式偏好学习有效解决了长时程操作任务中奖励设计的瓶颈，在性能、信号密度、行为组合性和用户引导性方面均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Reward design remains a central bottleneck for autonomous robot policy improvement, especially in long-horizon manipulation tasks where sparse success labels provide too little signal and binary preferences collapse many competing notions of quality into one ambiguous signal. We introduce Freeform Preference Learning (FPL), a method for learning robot policies from freeform human preferences. Rather than asking annotators which of two trajectories is better overall, FPL lets them define natural-language preference axes, such as speed, safety, quality of placement, or carefulness, and provide pairwise preferences along each axis. These annotations are used to learn a language-conditioned reward model that maps a trajectory and preference label to an axis-specific reward. We use this model to train a reward-conditioned policy that optimizes across the multiple human-specified dimensions. Across four real-world and two simulated long-horizon manipulation tasks, FPL improves over sparse-reward and binary-preference methods by 38 percentage points. Beyond improved performance, FPL learns dense progress signals without explicit subtask segmentation, shows compositionality of behavior not present in the data, and allows users to steer the policy towards different behaviors at test time without retraining. Blog post with videos available at https://freeform-pl.github.io/fpl.website/

</details>


### [104] [DVG-WM: Disentangled Video Generation Enables Efficient Embodied World Model for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32028)
*Ziyu Shan,Zhenyu Wu,Xiaofeng Wang,Zheng Zhu,Ziwei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DVG-WM，一种解耦视频生成世界模型，将世界建模显式分解为动力学学习和视觉合成两个阶段，在LIBERO和真实平台实验中实现了最高3.97倍的加速，同时保持高质量视频生成，可用于机器人操作中的高效规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视频的具身世界模型存在根本性纠缠：精确建模动力学需要低层次时序推理，而生成高分辨率帧需要高层次语义指导的视觉合成。这种纠缠导致推理速度慢（迭代规划）或预测过于粗糙（无法保留接触-rich细节），难以兼顾效率与质量。

Method: DVG-WM框架将世界模型解耦为两个阶段：首先基于初始观察和语言指令生成中间视觉状态的合理序列以预览物理交互，然后细化得到高保真视频。关键机制包括：(1) 使用流匹配(flow matching)直接将动力学映射到视频潜空间；(2) 引入潜空间退化机制(latent degradation mechanism)以重新生成接触-rich细节；(3) 高效的级联机制(cascading mechanism)实现加速。

Result: 在LIBERO和真实机器人平台上的实验表明，DVG-WM在提升视频质量的同时，实现了最高3.97倍的推理加速，验证了解耦视频生成作为高效具身世界模型在机器人操作中的有效性。

Conclusion: 解耦视频生成（将动力学学习与视觉合成分离）可以成为一种高效且高质量的具身世界模型范式，有效解决了传统世界模型中精确动力学建模与高分辨率视觉生成之间的效率-质量权衡问题。

Abstract: Video-based embodied world models provide an appealing substrate for robotic manipulation by predicting future states, yet current approaches remain limited by a fundamental entanglement: accurately modeling dynamics typically requires low-level temporal reasoning, while producing high-resolution frames demands expansive visual synthesis according to high-level semantics. This entanglement results in slow inference speed for iterative planning or too coarse predictions to retain contact-rich details. To solve this dilemma, we present Disentangled Video Generation World Model (DVG-WM), an efficient framework that explicitly decomposes world modeling into dynamics learning and visual synthesis. Conditioned on an initial observation and a language instruction, our model first generates a plausible sequence of intermediate visual states to preview the physical interaction and refines them to obtain high-fidelity videos. Furthermore, an efficient cascading mechanism is proposed, where DVG-WM uses flow matching to directly map the dynamics to video latents, and introduces a latent degradation mechanism to regenerate contact-rich details. Experiments on LIBERO and real-world platforms demonstrate improved video quality with up to 3.97 times acceleration, validating that disentangled video generation can be an efficient embodied world model for robotic manipulation.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [105] [Information Terra: A Narrative-Anchored Semantic-First Projection of Document Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30824)
*Brian Keith-Norambuena,Fausto German,Chris North*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出Information Terra，一种基于叙事的语义优先投影方法，将文档语料库映射到类地球体上，纬度编码叙事进展，经度编码主题偏离，并通过核密度估计恢复文档密度地形，构建叙事轨迹来呈现可读的故事线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档可视化方法缺乏对叙事结构和语义进展的直观表达，用户难以从大规模语料库中快速理解叙事脉络与主题变化。

Method: 1) 将文档嵌入到超球面上，用户选择两个端点文档作为北极和南极，它们之间的大圆路径作为本初子午线；2) 纬度编码叙事进展，经度编码主题偏离；3) 通过核密度估计从文档密度恢复陆地特征并按主题标记；4) 基于叙事连贯图构建单调的叙事轨迹。

Result: 在540篇文章的古巴抗议语料库上展示了方法有效性，呈现出从奥巴马2016年访问到2021年抗议期间国际援助的故事线。

Conclusion: 该方法通过地球隐喻实现了语义轴的可解释性，支持旋转和对跖阅读，为语料库探索提供了直观的叙事可视化工具。

Abstract: We introduce Information Terra, a narrative-anchored semantic-first projection that places a document corpus on an Earth-like globe whose poles are two user-chosen endpoint documents and whose prime meridian is the great-circle geodesic between them on the embedding hypersphere -- so latitude encodes narrative progress and longitude thematic deviation. Land features are recovered from document density via kernel density estimation and labeled by theme. A narrative trail built from the underlying narrative coherence graph, and constrained to be monotone in geodesic progress, provides a readable storyline. The projection's axes are semantically grounded in the user's chosen narrative endpoints, and the globe metaphor affords rotation and antipodal reading. We demonstrate the method on a 540-article Cuban Protests corpus, showing a storyline from Obama's 2016 visit to the 2021 International Aid during the protests.

</details>


### [106] [Drawing Out Legal Risks: Co-Designing with Lawyers to Predict and Manage Legal Uncertainties of Medical AI Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30828)
*Gennie Mansi,Julia Kim,Michael Rosenbloom,Mark Riedl*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过与美国律师的共同设计过程，探讨如何预测和管理医疗AI工具的法律风险，提出了可视化工具和策略，帮助人们和组织理解和应对这些风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管医疗AI工具具有跨用户和跨环境的适应能力，但这些新能力使得现有法律法规难以预测和管理相关风险。律师虽擅长识别法律结果，但缺乏AI技术知识，难以将专业见解有效传达给AI工具的创建者和使用者。

Method: 通过两年的跨学科努力，与美国律师进行共同设计（co-design）过程，开发可视化工具，识别和转化预测及管理医疗AI工具法律风险的方法。

Result: 提出了一系列可视化工具以及关于法律风险如何被确定的发现，并给出了帮助个人和组织预测及管理这些风险的策略。

Conclusion: 利用律师的专业知识可以有效理解、预测和管理医疗AI工具的法律风险；跨学科合作是弥合法律与技术之间鸿沟的关键。

Abstract: While there's optimism around medical AI tools due to their abilities to adapt from user-to-user and across environments, these new abilities complicate how people and organizations are able to predict and manage risk based on existing laws and regulations. Lawyers are trained to identify potential legal outcomes, but they lack technical AI knowledge, making it difficult to translate their expertise to creators and users of AI tools. We contribute insights from our co-design process with U.S. lawyers to identify and translate ways to predict and manage risks of medical AI tools. We present the visualizations we developed through two years of cross-disciplinary efforts and thereby illustrate our findings about how legal risks are determined and our strategies for people and organizations to predict and manage these risks. We offer insights about leveraging lawyers' expertise to understand, predict, and manage legal risks.

</details>


### [107] [Neural Signatures of Programming Expertise: Classifying Programmer Skill Levels Using EEG Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30879)
*Maurice Rekrut,Mahima Mahabaleshwar Acharya,Taisiia Ulianova,Norman Peitek,Annabelle Bergum,Mariya Toneva,Sven Apel,Antonio Krüger*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究利用EEG和机器学习分析37名程序员（1-30年经验）在代码理解时的脑电数据，发现EEG熵与编程技能水平最强相关，专家表现为局部化的中央-额叶激活，使用随机森林分类器在二分类（专家vs新手）中达91.83%准确率，多分类（专家、中级、新手）达78.15%，表明EEG特征可有效捕捉编程技能的神经关联，有望补充传统技能评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统编程技能评估方法（如编码测试、面试）难以全面捕捉编程专长背后的认知能力，因此探索使用EEG和机器学习来研究编程技能的神经关联，以提供更客观、多维度的技能评估手段。

Method: 分析已有的EEG数据集（37名程序员，1-30年经验），在代码理解任务中提取EEG特征（如熵、频带功率等）；使用随机森林分类器进行二分类（专家vs新手）和多分类（专家、中级、新手）实验；采用分层10折交叉验证和留一被试交叉验证评估性能；分析不同频带和脑区对分类的贡献。
