We construct tractable and informative features for the model by leveraging measures of product segment similarity from the geospatial literature. We find that such a model provides plausible estimates of the marketplace returns to additional supply and strong out of sample performance.

</details>


### [142] [Offline Reinforcement Learning for Fluid Controls: Data-based Multi-observational Policy Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31025)
*Deepak Akhare,Luning Sun,Xin-Yang Liu,Xiantao Fan,Timo Bremer,Ben Zhu,Jian-Xun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于离线强化学习的主动流动控制框架，通过传感器位置条件化架构和Point Attention层，实现了无需重新训练即可适应多种传感器配置的策略网络，并在K-S方程和Navier-Stokes方程两个代表性问题上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统在线强化学习方法在主动流动控制中存在两大问题：需要大量实时环境交互导致计算成本过高，且传感器配置变化时需要重新训练整个策略。这严重限制了该方法在实际工程中的应用。

Method: 提出离线强化学习框架，通过数据驱动进行策略提取；设计了传感器位置条件化架构，使单一策略网络可适应多种传感器排列；引入Point Attention层建模空间关系，确保对不同传感器位置配置的泛化能力。

Result: 在Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程的混沌抑制问题和Navier-Stokes方程控制的翼型流动控制问题上进行了验证，结果表明从数据集中提取的策略为传感器位置优化提供了前所未有的灵活性。

Conclusion: 该方法代表了向自适应、智能流动控制系统迈出的重要一步。

Abstract: Active flow control is a fundamental application in engineering. Recent advances in deep reinforcement learning have made progress in this field. However, the classical online RL approaches require extensive real-time interactions with the high fidelity environment, while each sensor configuration change necessitates whole policy retraining. All these factors result in prohibitive computational costs for real-world applications. In this work, we propose a novel offline RL framework that addresses both challenges through data-driven policy extraction. We develop a sensor position-conditioned architecture that enables a single policy network to adapt seamlessly to multiple sensor arrangements. The position-conditioned approach incorporated spatial relationship modeling through Point Attention layers to ensure the generalizability to varying sensor placements. We demonstrate the framework on two representative problems, mitigating chaoticity in the Kuramoto-Sivashinsky equation and flow control over airfoils governed by the Navier-Stokes equation. The result demonstrates that the policy extraction from the dataset provides unprecedented flexibility for sensor placement optimization. This approach represents a significant step towards adaptive, intelligent flow control systems.

</details>


### [143] [OTCache: Optimal Transport for Geometry-Aware Caching in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31026)
*Huanlin Gao,Fang Zhao,Qiang Hui,Fuyuan Shi,Shaoan Zhao,Yantao Li,Chao Tan,Ting Lu,Yuren You,Kai Wang,Shiguo Lian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OTCache 是一种无需训练的扩散采样加速框架，通过最优传输（OT）启发下的缓存调度预测，在低NFE场景下克服了传统图方法的加性独立假设问题。通过参考调度、极端低预算锚点搜索和分位数插值三阶段，在FLUX.1等模型上实现3.66x-4.7x加速，同时提升生成保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的缓存加速方法依赖加性独立假设（additive independence assumption），该假设在低NFE（函数评估次数）场景下常常失效，导致缓存调度质量下降。本文旨在提出一种无需额外训练、且能跨推理预算鲁棒工作的缓存调度方法。

Method: 三阶段框架：(1) 在保守预算下用图方法获取高保真参考调度；(2) 在极端低预算下用Optuna优化和端到端感知目标进行轻量锚点搜索；(3) 通过连续扭曲表示的分位数插值，在参考策略与锚点策略之间预测目标预算的调度策略。

Result: 在FLUX.1 [dev]上实现4.5x加速，Qwen-Image上4.7x加速，HunyuanVideo上3.66x加速，在生成保真度上持续优于最先进的缓存基线方法。

Conclusion: OTCache通过最优传输启发的调度建模，为加速扩散模型提供了新视角，在无需训练的条件下实现了显著的加速比和保真度提升。

Abstract: We propose OTCache, a training-free framework for accelerating diffusion sampling via caching schedule prediction. Existing graph-based caching methods reduce redundant computation by optimizing shortest-path objectives, but rely on an additive independence assumption, which often breaks down in the low NFE regime. To address this issue, OTCache models caching schedules across inference budgets as a smooth evolution in policy space, inspired by Optimal Transport (OT). The framework consists of three stages: (1) obtaining a high-fidelity \textbf{reference schedule} using a graph-based caching method under a conservative budget; (2) performing a lightweight anchor search under an extreme low-budget setting via Optuna optimization with an end-to-end perceptual objective; and (3) predicting schedules for target budgets via quantile interpolation between the reference and anchor policies using continuous warping representations. Experiments on FLUX.1 [dev], Qwen-Image, and HunyuanVideo show that OTCache achieves 4.5x, 4.7x, and 3.66x acceleration, respectively, while consistently improving generation fidelity over state-of-the-art caching baselines. This work provides a new perspective on accelerating diffusion models through Optimal-Transport-inspired schedule modeling. Code:https://github.com/UnicomAI/OTCache

</details>


### [144] [Teaching LLMs to Recommend and Defer in Underrepresented Epilepsy Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31036)
*Shreyas Rajesh,Kartik Sharma,Tonmoy Monsoor,Mehmet Yigit Turali,Richard Idro,Juliana Kayaga,Robert Sebunya,Tracy Tushabe Namata,Jessica Nichole Pasqua,Vwani Roychowdhury,Rajarshi Mazumder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MANANA框架，通过非参数化提示学习从少量患者数据中学习本地处方指南，结合贝叶斯提示平均实现选择性预测，在乌干达儿科癫痫诊疗中显著提升抗癫痫药物处方预测准确率，并能将高置信度病例自动处理（95%精确度覆盖半数病例），低置信度病例转交专家审核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限环境中，专科癫痫诊疗专家稀缺，使得基于LLM的决策支持系统对一线临床医生管理长期治疗具有吸引力；这类系统必须适应当地处方实践并知道何时应当转交专家处理。

Method: 提出MANANA，一种非参数化提示学习框架；从少量患者训练集中学习本地处方指南，将观察到的处方错误转化为可审计的提示记忆；支持单智能体和多智能体变体；进一步提出贝叶斯提示平均，将学习到的提示轨迹转化为处方似然和基于不确定性的转交信号。

Result: 在独立收集的保留队列上，MANANA的访视级top-3处方准确率相比提示优化基线提升4-8个百分点；系统能以95%精确度自动处理最置信的半数病例，以99%精确度处理最置信的四分之一病例，同时将低置信度病例转交专家审核。

Conclusion: MANANA框架通过非参数化提示学习和贝叶斯提示平均，有效解决了LLM在本地化处方实践中的分布校准问题，实现了高精度自动处方与不确定性驱动的选择性转交机制，适用于资源受限的临床场景。

Abstract: Specialist epilepsy expertise is scarce in resource-constrained settings, making LLM-based decision support attractive for frontline clinicians managing longitudinal treatment. Such systems must adapt to local prescribing practice and know when to defer. We study this problem in Ugandan pediatric epilepsy care, predicting anti-seizure medication regimens from longitudinal unstructured clinic notes. Standard prompting achieves non-trivial agreement with physician prescriptions, but neurologist review shows that many errors reflect distribution-miscalibrated prescribing defaults rather than failures to parse the local record. We introduce MANANA, a non-parametric prompt-learning framework that learns local prescribing guidance from a small patient-level training set. MANANA converts observed prescription errors into auditable prompt memories, instantiated in single-agent and multi-agent variants, and improves over classical ML models, direct LLM prompting, and prompt-optimization baselines across two independently collected Ugandan cohorts. We further propose Bayesian prompt averaging, which converts the learned prompt trajectory into prescription likelihoods and an uncertainty-based deferral signal. On the independently collected held-out cohort, this improves visit-level top-3 prescription accuracy by 4-8 percentage points over prompt-optimization baselines and enables selective prediction: the system can auto-handle the most confident half of cases at 95% precision, or the most confident quarter at 99% precision, while deferring lower-confidence cases for specialist review.

</details>


### [145] [Warp RL: Reshaping Base Policy Distributions for Dynamics Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31043)
*Ethan Hirschowitz,Fabio Ramos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Warp RL，用可逆的状态条件变换替代残差强化学习中的加法修正，以解决基策略动作分布在动态变化下因方差、置信度及几何形态不匹配导致的性能退化问题。在ManiSkill3仿真和真实机器人插销任务中，Warp RL在需要分布重塑时显著优于传统残差修正，且在sim-to-real管线中实现30%更快完成任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 残差强化学习中加法修正只能平移基策略的动作分布，无法改变其方差、置信度或状态依赖的几何形状（即"wrong variance, miscalibrated confidence, non-uniform correction"）。这些局限在动力学发生变化时会导致残差修正甚至不如未适应的基策略，因此需要一种能灵活重塑动作分布的适应方法。

Method: Warp RL将残差修正替换为一个状态条件的可逆变换，具体采用单调有理二次样条流（Monotonic Rational-Quadratic Spline Flows）来实现。该方法保留了恒等初始化，严格推广了加法残差修正，并提供了一个结构化的适应空间，可兼容策略梯度和无梯度优化。

Result: 在ManiSkill3多种操作任务及受控动力学变化下，Warp RL在仅需平移时匹配残差修正性能，在需要分布重塑时显著超越后者；在真实机器人插销任务的off-policy sim-to-real管线中，达到相同成功率的条件下任务完成速度提升30%。

Conclusion: Warp RL通过可逆分布变换替代加法残差，为机器人策略适应提供了一种更通用的框架，能够处理超出简单平移的动力学变化，在仿真和真实实验中均验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Residual reinforcement learning adapts a pretrained robot policy by learning an additive correction to its actions. While effective when adaptation amounts to shifting the base policy's action distribution, additive corrections cannot change the distribution's shape, scale, or state-dependent geometry -- limitations we formalize as wrong variance, miscalibrated confidence, and non-uniform correction. We show that these matter under dynamics shift: when the base distribution is geometrically mismatched to the shifted system, residual correction can underperform even the unadapted policy. We propose \textbf{Warp RL}, a policy adaptation method that replaces additive residuals with an invertible, state-conditioned transformation of the base policy's action distribution. Instantiated with monotonic rational-quadratic spline flows [arXiv:0706.1234v1], Warp RL preserves identity initialization, strictly generalizes additive residual correction, and exposes a structured adaptation space suitable for both policy-gradient and gradient-free optimization. Across a variety of ManiSkill3 manipulation tasks with controlled dynamics shifts, Warp RL matches residual correction when translation is sufficient and substantially outperforms it when adaptation requires distributional reshaping. We further demonstrate that warping can replace additive correction in an off-policy sim-to-real pipeline, achieving comparable success rate with 30% faster task completion on a real-robot peg-insertion task.

</details>


### [146] [Knowledge Distillation from Large Reasoning Models to Compact Student Models: A Case Study on the John O Bryan Mathematics Competition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31048)
*Gaurab Baral,Aaditya Khanal,Yangyang Tao,Junxiu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过双智能体框架从DeepSeek-R1大推理模型蒸馏知识到Qwen2.5-7B小模型，使用约翰·奥布莱恩数学竞赛历史题目构建思维链训练语料，在Apple Silicon上用LoRA微调。经过5轮200次迭代训练，学生模型在竞赛集上准确率从64.67%提升至69.43%，在MATH-500上达73.1%。研究还发现响应长度是影响数学推理质量的关键因素，准确率随长度减少从69.43%降至41.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索将大型推理模型（DeepSeek-R1）的知识通过知识蒸馏迁移到紧凑型学生模型（Qwen2.5-7B）中，以提升小模型在数学推理任务上的表现，同时研究响应长度对推理质量的影响。

Method: 采用双智能体框架构建思维链（CoT）训练语料，数据来源为北肯塔基大学约翰·奥布莱恩数学竞赛2011-2025年的历史题目。使用LoRA（低秩适配）方法在Apple Silicon硬件上通过MLX框架对学生模型进行微调。先进行1000次迭代训练发现过拟合（验证损失在200次迭代后上升），随后进行5次200次迭代的独立训练。同时设置6个推理层级（R1-R6）研究响应长度对准确率的影响。

Result: 基座Qwen2.5-7B准确率64.67%，教师模型DeepSeek-R1达91.40%。5次200迭代训练后，学生模型在竞赛集上平均准确率69.43%（标准差0.17%），提升4.76个百分点；在MATH-500上泛化至73.1%（标准差0.18%）。响应长度实验中，R1（平均220词）准确率69.43%，R6（平均31.2词）降至41.9%，双人速度部分对token缩减最敏感。

Conclusion: 思维链知识蒸馏能有效提升紧凑学生模型的数学推理能力（提升约4.8个百分点），且模型具有良好的结果稳定性（标准差仅0.17%）。响应长度是影响数学推理质量的关键因素，过短的推理链会显著损害准确性，其中需要多步推理的题目对长度缩减最为敏感。

Abstract: This paper investigates knowledge distillation from a large reasoning model (DeepSeek-R1) to a compact student model (Qwen2.5-7B). Using historical problems from the John O'Bryan Mathematics Competition at Northern Kentucky University (2011-2025), we build a Chain-of-Thought (CoT) training corpus through a dual-agent framework. The dataset is used to fine-tune the student model with Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) on Apple Silicon hardware using the MLX framework. The base Qwen2.5-7B model achieves 64.67% accuracy on competition problems, while the DeepSeek-R1 teacher achieves 91.40%. An initial 1,000-iteration training run revealed severe overfitting, with validation loss reaching a minimum at iteration 200 before rising steadily. Based on this finding, we ran five independent training runs each limited to 200 iterations with varied random seeds to assess result stability. Across these five runs, the fine-tuned student model achieves a mean accuracy of 69.43% (std dev 0.17%) on the competition dataset, a 4.76 percentage-point improvement over the base model, and generalizes to 73.1% (std dev 0.18%) on the MATH-500 benchmark. We further study how response length affects answer quality across six reasoning levels (R1-R6): accuracy declines consistently from 69.43% at R1 (mean 220 words) to 41.9% at R6 (mean 31.2 words), with the two-person speed section most sensitive to token reduction. These results demonstrate that CoT distillation improves compact student models and that response length is a critical factor in mathematical reasoning quality.

</details>


### [147] [Fora: From Weight-Space to Function-Space Protection in Capability-Preserving Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31092)
*Rui Zhou,Tianci Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FORA是一种基于函数空间正交残差适配的方法，通过估计每层输入激活协方差的主方向来构建投影矩阵，保护大语言模型在微调中已有能力不被破坏，相比传统的权重空间投影方法在保留能力上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全参数微调会使大语言模型丢失已有能力，现有方法通过参数距离、重要性惩罚、输出匹配或权重奇异方向等代理手段进行保护，但未能直接关注能力所依赖的激活方向。作者认为能力更应通过其诱导的激活子空间来刻画，而非权重矩阵的奇异几何。

Method: FORA从无标签校准数据中估计每层输入激活协方差的主方向Q，构建右投影器P_Q = I - QQ^T；结合权重SVD得到的左投影器P_U，将更新量分解为ΔW = P_U M P_Q + U_2 D_δ V_2^T，即一个被禁止读取能力相关函数方向的高容量分支，加一个窄谱通道用于受控可塑性。该构造可扩展为参数高效微调（M → (α/r)BA）。

Result: 在Qwen3-1.7B上三个场景（COGS和GSM8K学习同时保留翻译、翻译学习同时保留数学）的实验中，FORA在保留能力上一致优于权重空间投影和标准正则化方法，仅在数学保留场景中有小幅的新任务性能折损。消融实验表明优势来源于投影到能力导出方向而非权重导出方向。

Conclusion: FORA通过函数空间正交投影有效保护大语言模型在微调中的已有能力，其核心贡献在于证明了激活子空间比权重奇异几何更能忠实刻画模型能力，且能力导出方向的投影优于权重导出方向的投影。

Abstract: Full fine-tuning adapts large language models to new tasks but can erode capabilities they already possess. Existing remedies protect through proxies such as parameter distances, importance penalties, output matching, or dominant singular directions of the weights, but none directly asks which activation directions the preserved capability relies on. We argue that a capability is characterized more faithfully by the activation subspace it induces than by the singular geometry of the weight matrix, and develop function-space protection, instantiated as FORA (Function-space Orthogonal Residual Adaptation). From label-free calibration inputs, FORA estimates, per layer, the principal directions $Q$ of the input-activation covariance and forms a right projector $P_Q = I - QQ^T$. Paired with a left projector $P_U$ from the weight SVD, the update is $ΔW = P_U M P_Q + U_2 D_δ V_2^T$: a high-capacity branch structurally barred from reading capability-relevant function directions, plus a narrow spectral channel for controlled plasticity. The construction extends to parameter-efficient adaptation via $M \to (α/r) BA$. Across three settings on Qwen3-1.7B, including COGS and GSM8K learned while preserving translation and translation learned while preserving math, FORA consistently improves preservation over weight-space projection and standard regularization, with only a small new-task trade-off in the math-preservation setting. A controlled ablation isolating the projection source shows that the advantage comes not from projection itself, but from projecting onto capability-derived rather than weight-derived directions. Code is available at https://github.com/zrui239/FORA.

</details>


### [148] [Explaining Machine Learning and Memorization with Statistical Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31110)
*Robin Theriault*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文旨在通过统计力学工具，从理论上理解神经网络和机器学习中对抗性攻击和隐式低维学习的本质，并以密集联想记忆（DAM）和受限玻尔兹曼机（RBM）两类模型为研究对象，分析不同程度的学习与记忆拟合方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络和机器学习算法缺乏完善的理论理解，导致难以充分发挥其潜力并克服弱点：训练过程中权重沿低维子空间移动的特性未被充分利用，且训练后的网络容易受到对抗性攻击的干扰，其理论基础尚不清晰。

Method: 采用统计力学的数学工具，研究不同类型神经网络及其拟合数据的方式，重点分析两类模型——密集联想记忆（DAM）和受限玻尔兹曼机（RBM），并探讨这些模型不同版本之间的关联以提高分析效率。

Result: 论文通过统计力学方法揭示了神经网络在学习与记忆之间的权衡机制，建立了不同模型版本之间的理论联系，为理解隐式低维学习和对抗性攻击提供了数学框架。

Conclusion: 该论文从理论层面增进了对神经网络和机器学习的理解，特别是在对抗性攻击和隐式低维学习方面，为优化训练过程和提升模型鲁棒性奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Artificial neural networks (NNs) and machine learning (ML) algorithms are poorly understood from a theoretical perspective, which makes it difficult to fully realize their potential and overcome their weaknesses. For instance, ML algorithms train NN weights by moving them along a low-dimensional subspace of their allowed values, but this implicitly low-dimensional learning structure is not properly exploited to improve training because its nature is not well understood. Moreover, trained NNs are easily confused by pervasive adversarial attacks whose theoretical underpinnings are still unclear. This thesis aims to improve our theoretical understanding of NNs and ML, with a particular focus on adversarial attacks and implicitly low-dimensional learning. For this purpose, we use mathematical tools from statistical mechanics to study different types of NNs and ways in which they can fit the data. In particular, we study two classes of models that fit the data with various degrees of learning and memorization: dense associative memory (DAM) and restricted Boltzmann machines (RBM). In the process, we investigate connections between different versions of these models that are useful to make analytical investigations more efficient.

</details>


### [149] [Probing Memorization of Tabular In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31208)
*Francesco Capano,Jonas Böhler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了大型表格模型（LTMs）在上下文学习（ICL）中的参数记忆化现象，提出了ICLMEM探测框架，发现记忆化信号在低基数/二分类任务中存在，但在现实训练条件下基本消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLMs）已知会无意中记忆训练数据，但大型表格模型（LTMs）的记忆化动态尚未被探索。理解LTMs是否以及如何记忆训练数据对于保护敏感表格数据的安全至关重要。

Method: 提出ICLMEM探测框架，通过零信息多项选择上下文剥离有效上下文模式，强制模型依赖参数记忆；采用受控微调设置建立成员关系地面真值，并解决分布偏移、特征污染、基率谬误和样本难度校准等常见陷阱；使用预训练基模型作为参考校准样本难度。

Result: 在领先的真实世界训练LTMs上，10个任务中有8个检测到中等记忆化信号（AUC最高0.67，1% FPR下的TPR > 0.1），低基数和二分类任务中信号最强；但在现实训练条件下记忆化信号基本消失。

Conclusion: LTMs在特定条件下（单任务微调、固定样本多轮训练、小查询量）存在参数记忆化信号，但现实训练条件下风险有限；需针对敏感数据采取适当保护措施。

Abstract: Large tabular models (LTMs), i.e., tabular foundation models leveraging in-context learning (ICL), achieve state-of-the-art performance on tabular tasks. While LLMs are known to unintentionally memorize training data, the memorization dynamics of LTMs remain largely unexplored. We investigate the potential for parametric memorization in tabular ICL. We introduce ICLMEM, a probing framework designed to separate context-based predictions from parametric memorization. Our zero-information multiple-choice context strips away valid contextual patterns to force the model to fall back on its parametric memory. Our controlled fine-tuning setup establishes membership ground truth and accounts for common pitfalls, e.g., distribution shift, feature contamination, base-rate fallacy, and the pre-trained base model acts as reference to calibrate for sample difficulty. Our controlled evaluation on a leading real-world-trained LTM detects moderate memorization signals in 8 out of 10 tasks ($\text{AUC}$ up to $0.67$ and TPR at $1\%$ FPR $>0.1$). Notably, memorization signals are strongest for low-cardinality and binary tasks. However, they largely vanish under realistic training conditions. Our findings show LTM memorization signals under specific circumstances (single-task fine-tuning with fixed samples across many epochs and small query size). To protect sensitive data, appropriate measures must be taken, which we discuss.

</details>


### [150] [Visualizing High-Dimensional Graph Embeddings via Informed Multi-View Projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31119)
*Ya Ji,Xuefeng Li,Timo Brand,Jacob Miller,Peng Zhang,Stephen Kobourov,Yifan Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出在高维空间嵌入图，通过可微分的边交叉替代函数搜索最优二维视角，并开发交互式系统DataFly，实验证明该方法在美学和可读性指标上优于传统二维布局，能揭示隐藏的结构模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图在二维空间可视化中常扭曲高维结构信息，人类虽擅长理解二维空间关系，但二维布局难以保真地反映图的高维拓扑特性。

Method: 将图嵌入高维空间，设计可微分的边交叉替代函数，结合美学和可读性指标（如边交叉、角分辨率）优化搜索信息丰富的二维视点。实现交互系统DataFly支持多候选视点无缝导航。

Result: 数值实验表明该方法在优化指标上持续优于标准二维布局，甚至超越专门优化这些指标的方法；用户研究证明能揭示传统二维可视化中隐藏的结构模式。

Conclusion: 高维嵌入结合视角优化是一种有效的图可视化范式，既能保持结构的保真度，又能通过交互探索发现深层模式。

Abstract: Graphs are commonly visualized in 2D, where humans readily interpret spatial relationships, yet such layouts often distort higher-dimensional structure. We propose to embed graphs in high-dimensional space and search for informative 2D viewpoints that optimize aesthetic and readability metrics (e.g., edge crossings and angular resolution), enabled by a novel differentiable surrogate for edge crossings. Numerical experiments show that these viewpoints consistently outperform standard 2D layouts, and can even surpass methods explicitly designed to optimize these metrics. We further introduce DataFly, an interactive system for exploring multiple candidate viewpoints through seamless navigation. A usability study demonstrates that our approach reveals structural patterns that remain hidden in conventional 2D visualizations.

</details>


### [151] [Can Tabular In-Context Learners Generalize to Biomolecular Property Prediction?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31126)
*Davy Guan,Lu Zhang,Asiri Wijesinghe,Allen Zhu,He Zhao,Helen Power,F. Hafna Ahmed,Andrew Warden,Cheng Soon Ong,Daniel M. Steinberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现表格基础模型（如TabPFN3、TabICL）虽基于与生物分子数据无直接关联的合成因果图预训练，但在蛋白质适应度回归和小分子分类任务中表现竞争力强，其性能高度依赖输入表示方式（如ESMC、ECFP/RDKit）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物分子性质预测面临标注数据稀缺的瓶颈，虽然预训练编码器已能提供丰富表示，但如何构建数据高效的小样本预测器仍是核心挑战。

Method: 将表格基础模型作为预测器-表示对，在蛋白质域（固定ESMC表示）和小分子域（ECFP/RDKit描述符）上分别评估其回归与分类性能，对比不同表示组合下的表现。

Result: 蛋白质适应度回归中，表格上下文学习在ProteinGym和酯酶数据集上表现一致竞争力；小分子分类中，无单一表示-预测器组合占优，表示选择成为主要决定因素。

Conclusion: 表格基础模型在生物分子预测任务上表现强劲，但性能强烈依赖于所使用的序列或分子表示方式。

Abstract: Predicting biomolecular properties from limited labeled data is a central bottleneck in protein engineering and small-molecule design. As strong pretrained encoders now supply rich fixed-length representations, the difficulty has shifted from representation learning to building a data-efficient predictor for the few-shot regime. Tabular foundation models such as TabPFN3 and TabICL are unlikely candidates for this role: they are in-context learners pretrained on synthetic tables drawn from random causal graphs, a generative prior with no obvious correspondence to the processes that produce protein sequences or molecular graphs. That this tabular, causal inductive bias should transfer to biomolecular data at all is unintuitive, yet we find it does. Treating each method as a predictor-representation pair, we evaluate across two domains. Over a fixed ESMC representation, tabular in-context learning is consistently competitive for protein fitness regression on ProteinGym and a diverse esterase dataset. For small-molecule classification with ECFP/RDKit descriptors, no single pairing dominates across TDC ADMET, MoleculeNet, FS-Mol, and DrugOOD; representation choice becomes a primary determinant, as expected when the predictor's own prior is indifferent to molecular structure. We conclude that tabular foundation models are strong performers on biomolecular prediction tasks, but that their performance depends strongly on the sequence or molecular representation used.

</details>


### [152] [A Bayesian Filtering Approach for Learning Lagrangian Dynamics from Noisy Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31137)
*Kundan Kumar,Shreya Das,Simo Särkkä*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于贝叶斯滤波的方法，从含噪声的部分测量中学习物理系统的动力学，将系统动力学建模为拉格朗日力学形式，用神经网络参数化动能和势能，将未知外力建模为高斯白噪声，通过欧拉-拉格朗日方程得到连续时间随机状态空间模型，并利用高斯近似贝叶斯滤波器以最大似然方法联合学习网络参数和系统状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拉格朗日神经网络（LNN）方法在处理含噪声和不完整测量数据时存在局限性，需要一种能在部分观测和噪声环境下有效学习物理系统动力学的方法。

Method: 1) 用神经网络参数化拉格朗日力学中的动能和势能；2) 将未知外力建模为高斯白噪声；3) 通过欧拉-拉格朗日方程导出连续时间随机状态空间模型；4) 采用高斯近似贝叶斯滤波器（如扩展卡尔曼滤波器、无迹卡尔曼滤波器等）以最大似然方法联合学习神经网络参数和系统状态。

Result: 在摆锤和Duffing振子两个经典物理系统上验证了有效性，并与传统LNN以及使用已知系统模型的近似贝叶斯滤波器进行了性能对比。

Conclusion: 所提出的贝叶斯滤波框架能有效从部分含噪测量中学习物理系统动力学，在不确定性环境下优于传统LNN方法，为物理信息神经网络与贝叶斯滤波的结合提供了新思路。

Abstract: This paper proposes a Bayesian filtering-based approach for learning the dynamics of a physical system from partial, noisy measurements. We model the system dynamics using a Lagrangian mechanics formulation. As in Lagrangian neural networks (LNNs), we parameterize the kinetic and potential energies with neural networks. The unknown external forces in the Lagrangian formulation are modeled as white Gaussian noise. The corresponding Euler--Lagrange equations then yield a continuous-time stochastic state-space model (SSM) that describes the system dynamics. The neural network parameters and system states are then jointly learned via a maximum-likelihood method using Gaussian-approximation-based Bayesian filters. The effectiveness of the proposed method is demonstrated on pendulum and Duffing oscillator examples, and its performance is compared with conventional LNNs and with approximate Bayesian filters using known system models.

</details>


### [153] [PPT-Eval: A Benchmark for Computer-Use Agents on PowerPoint Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31154)
*Apurva Gandhi,Vishwas Suryanarayanan,Raja Hasnain Anwar,Firoz Shaik,Shubhang Desai,Thong Q. Nguyen,Muhammad Taqi Raza,Vishal Chowdhary,Graham Neubig*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了PPT-Eval，一个包含120个PowerPoint任务的基准测试，并设计了基于评分细则的部分分数评估框架（Kendall's τ-b=0.77），发现当前最强的Claude-4.5-Opus模型也仅达到45%的成功率和57%的平均部分分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 幻灯片创建与编辑是一项多模态、多步骤的复杂活动，缺乏系统性的评估基准来衡量AI智能体在真实PowerPoint任务中的表现，且现有二元成功指标无法捕捉智能体仅完成部分任务的进展。

Method: 构建了包含12个文件、120个PowerPoint任务的基准测试，任务涵盖内容创建和演示编辑场景并按难度分级；设计了一个基于评分细则的评估框架，对中间步骤给予部分分数、惩罚不必要修改和不良美学，并提供自然语言反馈。

Result: 该评估框架与人类判断的Kendall's τ-b相关性达到0.77；现有前沿智能体表现不佳，Claude-4.5-Opus仅45%成功率、平均部分分数57%。

Conclusion: PowerPoint任务对当前AI智能体仍是巨大挑战，所提出的评估框架能有效捕捉部分进展并提供细粒度反馈，为未来计算机使用智能体的研究提供了可靠的评估工具和基准。

Abstract: Creating and editing slides is a rich, multimodal activity that is ubiquitous in professional and educational settings, making it an ideal testbed for real-world computer-use agents. Microsoft PowerPoint is among the most widely adopted and feature-rich environments for presentation creation. We introduce PPT-Eval, a benchmark of 120 PowerPoint tasks across 12 files that cover both content creation and presentation editing scenarios, organized by difficulty. A central challenge in this domain is evaluation: tasks are complex, multimodal, and often admit many valid solutions. Moreover, today's agents frequently make only partial progress, which binary success metrics fail to capture. To address this, we design a robust evaluation framework to help create task-specific rubrics for PowerPoint tasks, taking inspiration from and building on past works for rubric-based evaluation. These rubrics award partial credit for intermediate steps, penalize unnecessary changes and poor aesthetics, and provide natural language feedback. This nuanced approach proves highly effective, achieving a Kendall's τ-b correlation of 0.77 with human judgments. We find that existing frontier agents still struggle with solving PowerPoint tasks, with strong models like Claude-4.5-Opus achieving only a 45% success rate and an average partial score of 57%. The benchmark is located at: https://microsoft.github.io/ppteval.

</details>


### [154] [ComplianceGate: Classifier-Gated Multi-Tier LLM Routing for Inference in Regulated Industries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31163)
*Abhishek Dey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分类器门控路由架构，通过在LLM推理前对查询进行复杂度和数据敏感性分类，将含有PII的查询路由到本地端点、简单查询路由到小型快速模型，从而同时实现合规性和成本效率。实验表明，该架构在600个查询上实现了39%的中位延迟降低、33-52%的成本节省，分类器准确率达99.2%，PII召回率接近完美，推理开销仅7ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在受监管行业中部署的大语言模型面临合规执行和成本效率的双重约束：用户查询中的PII可能在系统判定数据是否应离开管辖边界前就已到达模型端点；而单一大型模型服务所有查询不仅消耗全部GPU容量，也无法提供地理路由机制。现有MoE架构也无法解决此问题，因为路由发生在数据到达端点后的模型内部专家层之间。

Method: 提出分类器门控路由架构：在解码器推理前放置一个训练好的编码器分类器，评估每个查询的复杂度和数据敏感性，然后将其路由到适当规模（密集模型）和适当地理位置。含PII的查询在LLM计算开始前就被路由到本地端点，简单查询则路由到小型快速模型。

Result: 在600个查询上评估：中位延迟降低39%，成本节省33-52%（取决于查询分布），生成吞吐量122-200 tokens/秒（基线为50-64 tokens/秒）。编码器分类器准确率99.2%，PII召回率接近完美，推理开销仅7ms。

Conclusion: 预推理分类是实现合规性设计的LLM部署的实用路径，能够从根本上消除数据驻留违规的可能性，同时显著降低延迟和成本。

Abstract: Large language models deployed in regulated industries operate under two constraints: compliance enforcement and cost efficiency. Personally identifiable information (PII) in user queries can reach model endpoints before the system determines whether that data should leave its jurisdictional boundary. Serving all queries through a single large model consumes full GPU capacity regardless of query complexity while offering no mechanism for geographic routing. Mixture-of-Experts architectures do not address this routing occurs between expert layers within the model after data has already arrived at the endpoint, with all experts loaded in memory regardless of query complexity. We propose a classifier-gated routing architecture that enforces compliance by design. A trained encoder classifier sits before any decoder inference, evaluating each query for complexity and data sensitivity, then routing it to an appropriately sized dense model in the appropriate geographic location. PII-containing queries route to local endpoints before any LLM computation begins, making data residency violations structurally impossible. Simple queries reach small, fast models at a fraction of the cost. Our evaluation on 600 queries demonstrates 39% median latency reduction, 33-52% cost savings depending on query distribution, and generation throughput of 122-200 tokens/second versus 50-64 for the baseline. The encoder classifier achieves 99.2% accuracy with near-perfect PII recall at 7ms inference overhead, establishing pre-inference classification as a practical path to compliance-by-design LLM deployment.

</details>


### [155] [AETDICE: Unified Framework and Offline Optimization for Nonlinear Multi-Objective RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31178)
*Woosung Kim,Youngjun Suh,Jinho Lee,Jongmin Lee,Byung-Jun Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了聚合-期望-变换（AET）框架，统一了多目标强化学习中非线性偏好的两种范式（标量化期望回报SER和期望标量化回报ESR），并基于此设计了AETDICE算法，利用密度比估计在增广状态空间中实现离线样本优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标强化学习中的非线性偏好（如风险规避、公平性）优化至关重要，但现有SER和ESR两种范式因优化策略碎片化（SER需全局优化，ESR需非马尔可夫策略）而长期割裂，缺乏统一的理论框架。

Method: 通过标量化的三部分分解（聚合-期望-变换）统一SER与ESR，提出AET框架；基于此设计AETDICE算法，在增广状态空间中利用DICE风格的密度比估计实现离线强化学习。

Result: AET框架从理论上弥合了SER与ESR的范式鸿沟；AETDICE算法能从静态数据集进行基于样本的非线性MORL优化，解决了现有方法无法处理的权衡问题。

Conclusion: AET框架为通用非线性多目标强化学习提供了原则性基础，AETDICE证明了该框架在离线场景下的可计算性，突破了长期存在的优化障碍。

Abstract: Optimizing nonlinear preferences in multi-objective reinforcement learning (MORL) is essential for capturing complex trade-offs like risk aversion or fairness. However, such non-linearity has historically bifurcated nonlinear MORL objectives into two distinct paradigms: Scalarized Expected Return (SER) and Expected Scalarized Return (ESR). While SER requires global-level optimization and ESR requires non-Markovian policies, leading to fragmented optimization strategies, we bridge this divide through the Aggregation-Expectation-Transformation (AET) framework. By unifying both criteria through a tripartite decomposition of scalarization, AET provides a principled foundation for general nonlinear MORL. Building on this framework, we propose AETDICE, a tractable offline RL algorithm for AET objectives. By utilizing DICE-style density-ratio estimation in an augmented state space, AETDICE enables sample-based optimization from static datasets. Our framework resolves long-standing barriers and captures respective trade-offs induced by AET framework, which existing methods fail to address.

</details>


### [156] [Transformers as Bayesian In-Context Experimenters: Smoothness-Adaptive Efficient ATE Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31184)
*Jiachun Li,David Simchi-Levi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出贝叶斯上下文实验者——一种通过上下文学习模仿贝叶斯后验Neyman分配规则的Transformer策略，实现自适应实验的平均处理效应（ATE）高效推断，并理论证明了其可学习性与有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应实验需要在随机化分配中平衡统计推断的有效性与效率，而最优的oracle设计依赖于未知的臂条件结果方差。现有方法需在线估计方差并动态调整分配，计算负担重。作者希望探索能否通过上下文学习（in-context learning）来摊销这一序贯方差估计与分配过程。

Method: 提出贝叶斯上下文实验者（Bayesian in-context experimenters）：将Transformer策略训练为模仿贝叶斯后验Neyman教师（teacher）。教师利用实验历史更新潜在结果的非参数信念，并据此分配后验Neyman治疗概率。Transformer通过注意力机制构建充分统计量，并利用投影梯度下降近似实现贝叶斯更新（基于高斯级数先验）。针对未知结果平滑度，进一步提出混合专家Transformer（mixture-of-experts transformer），其门控机制充当平滑度类上的分层后验，聚焦于接近oracle的专家。通过约束Transformer类的复杂度，证明该摊销策略可通过监督预训练的经验风险最小化来学习。

Result: 实验验证了Transformer能够准确模仿教师策略，实现自适应分配，并在ATE精度上优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了Transformer可以通过上下文学习有效实现自适应实验设计中的序贯方差估计与最优分配，为因果推断中的摊销优化提供了理论保证与实用方案。

Abstract: Adaptive experiments for average treatment effects (ATE) require randomized allocations balancing valid inference with statistical efficiency. The oracle design is a covariate-dependent Neyman rule governed by unknown arm-conditional outcome variances. We investigate whether this sequential variance-estimation and allocation process can be amortized via in-context learning. We introduce Bayesian in-context experimenters: transformer policies trained to imitate a Bayesian posterior Neyman teacher. The teacher updates nonparametric beliefs over potential outcomes using experimental history to assign posterior Neyman treatment probabilities. This design converges to the oracle rule, supporting efficient ATE inference. Transformers constructively implement this mapping through attention-based sufficient statistics and projected gradient descent, imitating Bayesian updating for Gaussian-series priors. To address unknown outcome smoothness, we combine smoothness-indexed experimenters using a mixture-of-experts transformer. The gate acts as a hierarchical posterior over smoothness classes, concentrating on near-oracle experts. By bounding the complexity of the transformer class, we prove this amortized policy can be learned via empirical risk minimization using supervised pretraining. Experiments confirm accurate teacher imitation, adaptive allocation, and improved ATE precision over baselines.

</details>


### [157] [ISM:Self-Improving Strategy Memory for Continual Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31191)
*Prakhar Dixit,Tim Oates*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出智能模式记忆（ISM），一种自我演化的记忆增强系统，通过维护紧凑、自精炼的策略模式库，在严格的情节隔离下提升冻结大语言模型的数学推理能力，无需更新模型参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在持续学习且严格情节重置（hard episodic resets）条件下，冻结的大语言模型无法通过参数更新来适应新任务，需要一种外部记忆机制来积累和复用策略知识，同时避免灾难性遗忘。

Method: ISM维护一个紧凑、自精炼（self-refined）的策略模式（strategy schemas）库，从成功和失败的经验中学习，并利用符号工具检查中间步骤和验证答案。

Result: 在MATH-Hard和OlympiadBench上，ISM优于被动基线、检索基线和反思基线，且分别比最强的被动基线少使用64%和86%的模式。

Conclusion: 小型、主动维护且经过验证的策略记忆能够在严格情节隔离下支持可靠的持续数学推理。

Abstract: We propose Intelligent Schema Memory (ISM), a self-evolving memory-augmented system that improves mathematical reasoning for a frozen LLM under continual learning with hard episodic resets. ISM maintains a compact, self-refined bank of strategy schemas learned from both successful and failed episodes, with symbolic tools that check intermediate steps and certify answers.Without updating model parameters, ISM outperforms passive, retrieval, and reflection baselines on MATH-Hard and OlympiadBench, using 64% and 86% fewer schemas respectively than the strongest passive baseline. These results show that small, actively maintained, and verified strategy memories can support reliable continual mathematical reasoning under strict episodic isolation.

</details>


### [158] [Learning Gaussian Graphical Models from a Glauber Trajectory Without Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31230)
*Eric Shen,Tony Wu,Mahbod Majid,Ankur Moitra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究从单条Glauber动力学轨迹中学习d-稀疏高斯图模型的结构，提出了一个多项式时间算法，其轨迹长度保证不依赖于混合时间，通过条件方差估计、局部边测试和鲁棒中值估计三个组件实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际应用中常出现时间相关观测（非独立同分布样本），且经典i.i.d.设置下虽然存在次线性样本保证，但多项式时间内实现仍开放。本文旨在弥合这一差距。

Method: 算法包含三个组件：1) 估计条件方差并重新缩放轨迹以归约到单位对角情况；2) 设计局部边测试，通过隔离成对影响从短更新窗口中提取邻接信息；3) 使用鲁棒中值估计聚合局部统计量，克服单条轨迹的时间依赖性。

Result: 提出了一个多项式时间算法，能从单条Glauber动力学轨迹中恢复条件独立图，轨迹长度保证不依赖于混合时间。

Conclusion: 在时间相关观测（单条Glauber轨迹）下实现了图结构学习，克服了i.i.d.设置下多项式时间的困难，且无需混合时间依赖的保证。

Abstract: We study the task of learning the structure of a $d$-sparse Gaussian graphical model on $n$ variables from a single trajectory of Glauber dynamics. Beyond algorithmic considerations, many applications present temporally correlated observations rather than i.i.d.\ samples. In the classical i.i.d.\ setting, under comparably general sparsity and minimum edge-strength assumptions, sublinear-in-$n$ sample guarantees are known, but achieving them in polynomial-time remains open. Motivated in part by this gap, we give a polynomial-time algorithm that recovers the conditional-independence graph from a single Glauber trajectory, with a trajectory-length guarantee that does not depend on the mixing time.
  Technically, our algorithm has three components. First, we estimate the conditional variances and rescale the trajectory to reduce to the unit-diagonal case, without changing the underlying graph. Second, we design a local edge test that extracts adjacency information from short update windows by isolating pairwise influence. Third, we aggregate these local statistics using a robust median-based estimator, and prove accuracy despite temporal dependence arising from a single trajectory.

</details>


### [159] [TDGT: A Tabular Data Generation Toolkit supporting adaptive GPU-accelerated Bayesian mixture models, diffusion-based models, and latent-space generative modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31268)
*Vasileios C. Pezoulas,Nikolaos S. Tachos,Eleni Georga,Kostas Marias,Manolis Tsiknakis,Dimitrios I. Fotiadis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TDGT是一个支持自适应贝叶斯混合合成器(ABMS)及其VAE增强变体(VAE-ABMS)的Web工具包，用于表格数据合成与保真度评估，提供11项统计保真度指标和隐私风险指标，支持GPU加速和实时交互式可视化，在医疗、社会经济和网络安全数据集上验证了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成数据解决方案缺乏自适应生成策略、多指标评估体系以及端到端可访问的统一Web工具包。

Method: 提出ABMS算法（通过迭代聚类质量优化自动确定混合分量数量）和VAE-ABMS混合架构（VAE潜在空间学习结合自适应贝叶斯混合合成）；提供GPU加速版本（CUDA k-means聚类+高斯混合拟合）；设计11项统计保真度指标和隐私风险指标；构建基于Plotly交互式可视化的实时流式Web界面。

Result: 在医疗、社会经济建模和网络安全等多个领域的数据集上，TDGT在不同特征类型和数据规模下均表现出一致的生成保真度和统计一致性。

Conclusion: TDGT通过集成自适应生成、多维评估和Web可用性，为隐私保护数据共享提供了统一的表格数据合成解决方案。

Abstract: The growing demand for privacy-preserving data sharing has positioned synthetic data generation as a critical component of responsible AI workflows. Despite notable advances in generative modeling, existing solutions often lack integration of adaptive generation strategies, multi-metric evaluation, and accessible end-to-end generators within a unified web-based toolkit. In this work, we introduce TDGT (Tabular Data Generation Toolkit), a web-based toolkit for synthetic tabular data generation and fidelity assessment. TDGT introduces the Adaptive Bayesian Mixture Synthesizer (ABMS), a novel algorithm that autonomously determines the optimal number of mixture components through iterative cluster quality optimization, eliminating the need for manual hyperparameter configuration. Building upon ABMS, we further propose VAE-ABMS, a hybrid architecture that couples Variational Autoencoder-based latent space learning with adaptive Bayesian mixture synthesis, enabling high-fidelity generation of complex, nonlinear tabular distributions. For large-scale scenarios, TDGT provides a GPU-accelerated variant of ABMS leveraging CUDA-based k-means clustering and Gaussian mixture fitting. Synthetic data fidelity is assessed through eleven statistical fidelity metrics spanning distributional divergence, structural correlation, and sample-level similarity, complemented by privacy risk indicators including k-anonymity scoring and disclosure rate estimation. The web-based toolkit supports a real-time streaming interface with interactive Plotly-based visualizations. TDGT is assessed across datasets from healthcare, socioeconomic modeling, and cybersecurity domains, demonstrating consistent generation fidelity and statistical coherence across heterogeneous feature types and data scales.

</details>


### [160] [The Calibration Turn in AI-Assisted Research: A Conceptual and Methodological Framework for Evidence-Licensed Claims](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31273)
*Hongmin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向AI辅助研究的证据许可主张（evidence-licensed claims）的概念与方法框架，将AI辅助研究建模为五个算子（假设生成、模型中介的推论推导、外部验证、信念更新、主张校准），核心观点是校准是一种管理科学断言权的机制，并区分了四种语义类型，定义了主张-证据差距与认知债务，提出了最小结构重构作为向上的主张校准方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助研究的核心问题已从能否生成假设、运行实验或撰写论文，转变为科学主张是否与支撑证据相校准。现有研究缺乏一个系统的概念与方法论框架来界定和评估AI辅助研究中科学主张的证据授权问题。

Method: 采用Perspective-style论述方式，首先通过六个代表性路线（专用科学基础模型、LLM研究助手、多智能体协同科学家、AI科学家pipeline、数学发现智能体、自驱动实验室）提炼出五个算子框架，进而区分四种语义（语言学的、基于推论的、干预性的、证据许可的），定义主张-证据差距和认知债务，最后提出校准原则。

Result: 提出了三条核心原则：（1）无证据许可则无主张；（2）验证不能决定主张等级；（3）自动化放大了校准需求。并设计了AISim-Cal作为示意性合成动力学练习。最终将可靠的AI辅助研究定义为：生成假设→推导可检验推论→接受独立裁决→更新信念→仅输出证据许可的主张的循环。

Conclusion: AI辅助研究的可靠性取决于能否形成一个封闭的校准循环，其中主张的输出必须受到证据的许可约束。校准不是谨慎措辞，而是科学断言权的管理机制。该框架为评估和改进AI辅助研究的可信度提供了理论基础。

Abstract: AI-assisted research has entered a stage in which the central question is not only whether systems can generate hypotheses, run experiments, or produce manuscripts, but whether their scientific claims are calibrated to the evidence that supports them. This Perspective-style paper develops a conceptual and methodological framework for evidence-licensed claims in AI-assisted research. Motivated by representative routes including specialized scientific foundation models, LLM research assistants, multi-agent co-scientists, AI Scientist pipelines, mathematical discovery agents, and self-driving laboratories, it represents AI-assisted research as five operators: hypothesis generation, model-mediated consequence derivation, external validation, belief update, and claim calibration. The central claim is that calibration is not merely cautious wording but a mechanism for managing scientific assertion rights: evidence licenses some forms of speech and withholds others. The paper distinguishes linguistic, consequence-based, interventional, and evidence-licensed semantics; defines the claim-evidence gap and epistemic debt; and treats minimal structural reconstruction across heterogeneous outputs as an upward form of claim calibration. AISim-Cal is included as an illustrative synthetic dynamics exercise, not as an empirical forecast or benchmark. The resulting principles are: no claim without license, validation does not determine claim level, and automation amplifies the need for calibration. Reliable AI-assisted research is therefore evaluated as a loop that generates hypotheses, derives testable consequences, accepts independent adjudication, updates beliefs, and outputs only evidence-licensed claims.

</details>


### [161] [Revisiting the Volume Hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31282)
*Ari Pakman,Lior Kreimer,Yakir Berchenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了深度神经网络中梯度学习与随机采样训练在泛化性能上的差异，发现随着训练数据量增加，梯度学习的泛化优势逐渐减弱，解决了关于体积假说的矛盾实验结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前关于体积假说的实验呈现矛盾结果：一组实验发现随机采样权重至零训练误差导致泛化差，而分子动力学密度估计支持体积假说。作者观察到这些实验在不同数据集规模下进行，因此需要探索中间数据规模区间以解决这一矛盾。

Method: 使用Replica Exchange Wang-Landau算法估计二值网络中训练和测试准确率的联合态密度，在多种架构和数据集上比较梯度学习与随机采样训练的泛化性能随训练数据大小的变化。

Result: 梯度学习相对于随机采样训练的泛化优势随着训练数据量增加而逐渐减弱。

Conclusion: 该结果解释了此前关于体积假说的矛盾实验结果，表明数据规模是关键因素。

Abstract: Modern deep neural networks often contain far more parameters than needed to fit their training data, yet they achieve impressive generalization. A common explanation for this success is the implicit bias of stochastic gradient descent (SGD). An alternative volume hypothesis posits that, within low training-loss regions, loss-landscape basins leading to strong generalization occupy much larger regions of weight space than basins that generalize poorly, and therefore SGD is simply more likely to land in the former. Recent experimental explorations of this idea present seemingly contradictory results. While in one set of experiments randomly sampling the network weights until achieving zero training error yielded poor generalization, molecular dynamics density estimates supported the volume hypothesis. We observe that these experiments were performed at different dataset size regimes, and explore an intermediate regime using the Replica Exchange Wang-Landau algorithm to estimate the joint density of states over training and test accuracies in binary networks. Across several architectures and datasets, we show that the generalization advantage of gradient learning over random sampling training generally diminishes as the training data size grows, suggesting a resolution of the paradox.

</details>


### [162] [Sequential sparse Gaussian process quantile regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31284)
*Hugo Nicolas,Olivier Le Maître*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于稀疏高斯过程和拉普拉斯近演的贝叶斯分位数回归框架，通过将预测不确定性分解为条件先验和后验诱导方差，驱动诱导点填充与数据采集两种自适应机制，实现模型复杂度与计算资源的动态调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯高斯过程分位数回归因非对称拉普拉斯似然的非共轭性导致后验推断计算成本高昂，亟需一种兼顾计算效率与不确定性量化的方法。

Method: 采用稀疏高斯过程通过少量诱导变量表示分位函数，利用拉普拉斯近似进行后验推断；将预测不确定性分解为条件先验方差和后验诱导方差，据此设计诱导点填充与数据采集两种互补自适应机制，并通过序贯算法动态分配计算资源。

Result: 在基准问题上验证了拉普拉斯近似的准确性、基于方差的诱导点放置的优越性，以及所提序贯策略相比预定义数据采集策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的自适应稀疏高斯过程框架能够高效处理贝叶斯分位数回归中的计算挑战，通过不确定性分解驱动的自适应机制实现了模型复杂度的可控增长与计算资源的最优分配。

Abstract: Quantile regression aims to estimate the conditional quantiles of a response variable from observed data. In a Bayesian setting, Gaussian process quantile regression provides uncertainty quantification but faces significant computational challenges due to the nonconjugacy of the asymmetric Laplace likelihood and the cost of posterior inference. We develop a sparse Gaussian process framework in which the quantile function is represented through a reduced set of inducing variables and posterior inference is performed using a Laplace approximation. A decomposition of the predictive uncertainty into conditional-prior and posterior-induced variance components is then exploited to drive two complementary adaptive mechanisms: inducing-input infilling and data acquisition. These mechanisms are combined within a sequential algorithm that allocates computational effort toward the dominant source of predictive uncertainty and adaptively controls model complexity. Numerical experiments on benchmark problems demonstrate the accuracy of the Laplace approximation, the benefits of variance-based inducing-input placement, and the effectiveness of the proposed sequential enrichment strategy compared with predefined data-acquisition strategies.

</details>


### [163] [Probabilistic Inversion with Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31288)
*Baldur Paulwitz,Stefan Buske*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文展示了将生成式人工智能中的Flow Matching技术应用于地球物理概率反演（如全波形反演），并通过2D速度模型和OpenFWI数据集验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将生成式AI中的Flow Matching理论适配到地球物理概率反演场景，解决传统反演方法在不确定性量化方面的局限性。

Method: 将Flow Matching的数学理论从生成式AI适配到概率反演框架，通过两个案例（简单2D速度模型和复杂OpenFWI数据集）进行验证。

Result: Flow Matching方法成功应用于概率反演，在简单和复杂速度模型上均展现出良好的反演性能。

Conclusion: Flow Matching作为一种新兴的概率反演工具，能够有效地对地球物理参数进行不确定性量化，适用于从简单到复杂的地震速度模型反演任务。

Abstract: We demonstrate the application of Flow Matching, a technique originating from generative Artificial Intelligence, to probabilistic inversion in geophysical settings, such as seismic Full-Waveform inversion. We adapt the well-established mathematical theory of Flow Matching from generative Artificial Intelligence to the context of probabilistic inversion. We evaluate the approach with two case studies: a simple 2D velocity model to illustrate the general features of the method, and the OpenFWI dataset to show its capabilities for probabilistic inversion of more complex seismic velocity models.

</details>


### [164] [Patch-PODiff-ViT: Structured Latent Diffusion with Patchwise POD for Super-Resolution and Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31290)
*Onkar Jadhav,Tim French,Matthew Rayson,Nicole L. Jones*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Patch-PODiff-ViT框架，通过分块本征正交分解（POD）构建固定线性正交基作为潜空间，替代非线性自编码器学习潜空间，实现高效扩散和可解释不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素空间扩散模型计算成本高，而学习的潜空间缺乏可解释的不确定性量化能力。

Method: 利用分块POD定义固定线性正交基作为潜空间，提取低维、方差排序的token，结合Vision Transformer进行扩散；解码器固定线性正交，潜系数不确定性可解析传播至物理空间。

Result: 在海表温度、医学成像和自然图像上，该方法用更少参数和更低内存实现强重建性能，产生与经验集成高度一致的校准良好的空间不确定性。

Conclusion: 固定线性正交潜空间无需蒙特卡洛估计即可解析传播预测方差，兼顾效率与可解释不确定性量化。

Abstract: Diffusion models enable probabilistic super-resolution and conditional generation, but pixel-space methods are computationally expensive and learned latent spaces often lack interpretable uncertainty quantification. We introduce Patch-PODiff-ViT, a structured latent diffusion framework in which the latent space is defined by patchwise Proper Orthogonal Decomposition (POD), a fixed linear orthonormal basis over local patches, rather than learned by a nonlinear autoencoder. This yields low-dimensional, variance-ordered tokens that preserve spatial structure and enable efficient diffusion in a structured low-dimensional latent space with a Vision Transformer. Because the decoder is fixed, linear, and orthonormal, latent coefficient uncertainty can be propagated directly to physical-space predictive variance, enabling analytic propagation of predictive variance through the linear decoder without Monte Carlo estimation in pixel space. Across sea surface temperature, medical imaging, and natural images, the method achieves strong reconstruction with fewer parameters and lower memory, while producing well-calibrated spatial uncertainty that closely matches empirical ensembles.

</details>


### [165] [Deep Reinforcement Learning for Spacecraft Attitude Control During Atmospheric Re-Entry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31291)
*Alexander Fabisch,Melvin Laux,Mariela De Lucas Álvarez,Edoardo Caroselli,Julian Theis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探索了深度强化学习在航天器再入姿态控制中的应用，通过动力学随机化提升泛化能力，混合控制器在操作包线内比传统PID控制器具有更好的攻角跟踪性能和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统姿态控制方法在处理非线性动力学、不确定性和故障情况方面存在局限，深度强化学习有望更自适应、精确和鲁棒地解决这些问题。

Method: 将姿态控制形式化为连续、离策略的强化学习问题，以带增益调度的PID控制器为基线，分别训练无模型RL控制器和RL+PID混合控制器，并采用动力学随机化在训练中引入任务变化以强制泛化。

Result: 最优RL控制器在操作包线内的应用特定指标评估中表现优于传统控制器：混合控制器能更好地跟踪攻角，并且在质量、惯性张量和襟翼执行器带宽变化下具有更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 强化学习在航天器再入姿态控制中具有潜力，混合控制器结合了传统方法的稳定性和RL的自适应性，在操作包线内实现了更优性能和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning has the potential to solve attitude control problems more adaptively, precisely, and robustly by handling nonlinear dynamics, uncertainties, and failure cases more effectively than traditional attitude control approaches. We explore reinforcement learning (RL) for attitude control in spacecraft re-entry. An industry-standard proportional-integral-derivative controller with gain scheduling serves as a strong baseline for model-free RL and hybrid controllers that combine these two approaches. We formalize the application in the RL framework to apply continuous, off-policy RL. State-of-the-art RL achieves comparable performance to traditional control approaches in this domain. However, its out-of-distribution generalization is not sufficient. Hence, we use dynamics randomization to introduce challenging task variations during training and enforce generalization in a predefined operational envelope. Finally, we assess the best obtained RL-based controllers with application-specific metrics to show superior performance in comparison to traditional controllers in the operational envelope, that is, hybrid controllers are able to track the angle of attack better and are more robust under variations of mass, inertia tensor, and flap actuator bandwidth.

</details>


### [166] [Safe Online Learning via Smooth Safety-Structured Policy Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31320)
*Hongpeng Cao,Liqun Zhao,Yuliang Gu,Naira Hovakimyan,Lui Sha,Marco Caccamo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AutoSafe是一种将安全监控与干预直接集成到动作生成过程中的策略架构，实现了性能驱动与安全保持行为之间的平滑、风险依赖转换，在连续控制基准和物理车杆系统上验证了其在不牺牲学习平滑性的前提下提供强安全保证的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全在线强化学习方法存在两难困境：严格安全执行（动作干预）导致系统交互和学习不连续，而软安全约束虽保持学习平滑但安全保证有限。需要一种既能保证安全又不破坏学习连续性的方法。

Method: 提出AutoSafe策略架构，将结构化安全监控和干预机制直接集成到动作生成过程中，实现性能驱动行为与安全保持行为之间的平滑、风险依赖转换，从而维持连续的在线交互和学习动态。

Result: 在多个连续控制基准任务上，AutoSafe在保持学习平滑性的同时实现了强安全执行；在物理车杆系统上的实验进一步验证了其在真实世界安全在线学习中的实际有效性。

Conclusion: AutoSafe成功解决了安全强化学习中安全保证与学习平滑性之间的权衡问题，通过架构级集成安全机制实现了两者的兼顾，并在物理系统上证明了其实用性。

Abstract: Safe online reinforcement learning requires policies to respect safety constraints while maintaining smooth optimization dynamics. Existing approaches typically rely on either strict safety enforcement via action interventions, which introduce discontinuities in system interaction and learning, or soft safety constraint formulations, which preserve smooth learning but provide limited safety assurance. We propose AutoSafe, a safety-aware policy architecture that integrates structured safety monitoring and intervention directly into the action generation process. This design enables smooth, risk-dependent transitions between performance-driven and safety-preserving behaviors, resulting in continuous online interaction and learning dynamics. Empirical results across a suite of continuous-control benchmarks demonstrate strong safety enforcement without sacrificing learning smoothness. We further validate AutoSafe on a physical cart-pole system, highlighting its practical effectiveness for safe online learning in the real world.

</details>


### [167] [Expected Gain-based Escalation in Vertical Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31331)
*Mohamad Mestoukirdi,Vincent Corlay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于垂直联邦学习（VFL）的两轮推理协议中的选择性升级策略。第一轮低成本推理使用客户端后验概率进行预测，仅当预期能显著提升最终决策时才触发第二轮嵌入融合。通过基于校准池化后验和类别级可靠性的分析性分数进行路由，无需单独训练路由网络。实验表明，该方法在通信-准确率权衡上优于多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在垂直联邦学习（VFL）中，对每个样本都执行协同融合会带来不必要的通信和计算开销。作者希望实现一个选择性升级机制，仅在能提升最终决策时触发昂贵的第二轮融合，以优化通信-准确率权衡。

Method: 提出两轮VFL推理协议：第一轮基于客户端后验概率低成本预测；第二轮通过嵌入融合提升精度。路由决策基于期望增益评分估计——将校准池化后验与VFL模型的类别级可靠性估计结合，生成分析性评分来决定是否升级。该方法仅需少量校准数据，无需额外训练路由网络。

Result: 在多视图分类基准测试及受控的测试时视图退化场景下，所提出的路由器在通信-准确率权衡上优于基于置信度、学习增益和延迟策略的基线方法。

Conclusion: 提出了一种无需额外训练网络的可解释路由方法，能有效在VFL中权衡通信开销与预测准确性，适用于资源受限的分布式推理场景。

Abstract: Collaborative inference can improve predictive performance by integrating complementary information across agents, but applying collaborative fusion to every sample can incur unnecessary communication and computational overhead. This trade-off is particularly relevant in vertical federated learning (VFL), where clients observe different views of the same sample and fusion typically requires transmitting intermediate representations to a server. We study selective escalation in a two-round VFL inference protocol, in which a low-cost first round produces a prediction from client posteriors and a second embedding-fusion round is invoked only when it is expected to improve the final decision. We formulate routing as expected-gain score estimation: a sample is escalated when a predicted improvement in correctness justifies the additional communication. The proposed analytical score combines a calibrated pooled posterior with classwise reliability estimates of the VFL model, both obtained from held-out calibration data, yielding an interpretable router that requires no separately trained routing network. Experiments on multi-view classification benchmarks, including controlled test--time view degradation settings, show that the proposed router improves the communication-accuracy trade-off over confidence-, learned-gain-, and deferral-based baselines.

</details>


### [168] [Dualformer: Efficient Feature Extractor for Complex-valued Blind Communication Signal Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31352)
*Yurui Zhao,Xiang Wang,Jingreng Lei,Wanlong Zhang,Yik-Chung Wu,Zhitao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 DualNN 框架及其 Transformer 实现 Dualformer，通过 IQ 通道参数共享高效处理复值信号，理论上降低泛化误差并保持表达能力，在自动调制识别、信号方案识别和信号结构解析任务上优于现有方法，并可泛化至盲源分离和低信噪比频谱感知等任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为盲信号分析任务（如自动调制识别、信号方案识别、信号结构解析）设计高效的特征提取器至关重要，现有实数或复数模型存在局限性，亟需一种既能高效利用复值信号又能保证泛化性能的框架。

Method: 提出 DualNN 框架，通过 IQ 通道间参数共享处理复值信号的实部和虚部；并基于 Transformer 实现 Dualformer，将输入信号分割为 patch 级 token 并捕获多粒度特征。

Result: 在 AMR、SSR 和 SSP 任务上与三种基于 Transformer 的基线方法和四种传统深度学习方法相比，Dualformer 均取得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: DualNN 的模块化设计使其能良好泛化至盲源分离、低信噪比频谱感知等盲信号处理任务，为无监督和弱监督复值信号分析场景开辟更广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Designing effective feature extractors is critical for blind signal analysis tasks such as automatic modulation recognition (AMR), signal scheme recognition (SSR), and \color{black} signal structure parsing (SSP). In this work, we propose dual-channel neural network (DualNN) that efficiently exploits complex-valued signals through parameter sharing across IQ channels. Unlike traditional real-valued or complex-valued models, DualNN is a groundbreaking framework which shares the network parameters for processing the real and imaginary parts of the complex-valued signals, and is theoretically shown to reduce generalization error while preserving expressive capacity. Specifically, we propose a novel Transformer-based architecture to implement DualNN, called Dualformer. The Dualformer segments input signals into patch-level tokens and captures multi-granularity features, enabling robust performance across diverse signal analysis tasks. Furthermore, we conduct extensive experiments comparing Dualformer with three Transformer-based baselines and four conventional DL-based approaches. Results demonstrate consistent performance improvements on AMR, SSR, and SSP tasks. Besides, the modular design of DualNN allows it to generalize well to blind signal processing tasks such as blind source separation and low-SNR spectrum sensing. This work paves the way for a broader application of DualNN architectures in unsupervised and weakly supervised complex-valued signal analysis scenarios.

</details>


### [169] [Calibrating the Evaluator: Does Probability Calibration Mitigate Preference Coupling in LLM Agent Feedback Loops?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31371)
*Zewen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次研究了评估器概率校准作为缓解LLM Agent中评估器偏好耦合（EPC）现象的方法，通过对比标准二元TTRL与置信度校准TTRL，发现校准能将耦合系数降低20-49%，JS散度降低45-67%，并发布了校准后的TTRL协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已发现评估器偏好耦合现象及其诊断框架（EPC），但尚未探索校准技术能否缓解该问题，本文填补了这一空白。

Method: 采用被试内实验设计（N=5），对比标准二元TTRL（胜/负）与置信度校准TTRL（概率加权更新），使用DeepSeek-V4-Pro作为执行器、GLM5.2作为评估器，并引入对称LR控制实验排除更新不对称性的干扰。

Result: 校准将耦合系数γ降低20-49%，Jensen-Shannon散度降低45-67%，且对称LR控制证实效果并非源于更新不对称性的降低。

Conclusion: 概率校准是一种有效的轻量级缓解方法，建议在LLM-as-judge部署流程中采用校准后的TTRL协议。

Abstract: When large language model (LLM) agents adapt their behavior through evaluator feedback, systematic evaluator biases propagate into the agent's learned strategy distribution - a phenomenon termed evaluator preference coupling. Prior work has documented this coupling and established a diagnostic framework (EPC) to measure it, but has not investigated whether calibration techniques can mitigate the effect. We present the first study of evaluator calibration as mitigation: applying probability calibration to the evaluator's pairwise judgments to reduce spurious preference propagation. In a controlled within-subjects experiment (N=5) comparing standard binary TTRL (win/loss) with confidence-calibrated TTRL (probability-weighted updates) using DeepSeek-V4-Pro as executor and GLM5.2 as evaluator, we find that calibration reduces the coupling coefficient gamma by 20-49% and Jensen-Shannon divergence by 45-67%. A symmetric-LR control confirms the effect is not due to reduced update asymmetry. We release the calibrated TTRL protocol and recommend it as a lightweight mitigation for LLM-as-judge deployment pipelines.

</details>


### [170] [Resolving superposition in AI for interpretability and cross-modal alignment in patient-neuronal images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31394)
*Jisung Park,Seohyeon Kang,Daeun Yoo,Eunsu Lee,Seoin Cho,Wooyeop Choi,Ian Choi,James R. Evan,Daesoo Kim,Sonia Gandhi,Minee L. Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用稀疏自编码器（SAE）解决高维生物数据中的维度瓶颈与叠加（superposition）问题，证明叠加会污染潜在表征的几何度量空间，而SAE可恢复几何保真度。进而将纯化后的表征作为单细胞状态向量，将scRNA-seq分析方法迁移至图像域，并引入基于Gromov-Wasserstein最优传输的GW-map方法，实现图像表征与真实scRNA-seq数据的无参考对齐，重建层次化的神经元病理通路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维生物数据中，神经网络在维度瓶颈下迫使不同概念挤压至低维空间产生叠加（superposition），虽然该现象已被广泛认为阻碍可解释性，但其对潜在空间几何度量的污染效应却长期被忽视。本文旨在揭示并解决这一问题。

Method: 1) 在超10万张帕金森病与健康神经元的多重成像数据上训练稀疏自编码器（SAE）以解析叠加；2) 理论证明并实验验证叠加污染表征度量空间，SAE可恢复几何保真度；3) 将几何纯化后的表征作为单细胞状态向量，将scRNA-seq数据分析方法直接迁移至图像域；4) 提出GW-map，利用Gromov-Wasserstein最优传输实现图像表征与真实scRNA-seq数据的无监督对齐。

Result: SAE成功恢复被叠加污染的几何保真度；GW-map无需参考空间转录组即可重建层次化神经元病理通路（如Calcium-AIS支架），为空间生物学建立可扩展基础。

Conclusion: 叠加现象对潜在空间几何的污染可通过SAE有效缓解，几何纯化后的表征能够桥接图像与单细胞转录组数据，为跨模态的空间生物学分析提供了新范式。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is transforming our capability to solve biological challenges. In dimensionality bottleneck regimes exacerbated by high-dimensional biological data, Neural networks force distinct concepts into the lower dimensions known as superposition. Although this superposition is widely known to hinder interpretability, its impact on corrupting the geometry of latent spaces remains critically overlooked. Here, we utilized sparse autoencoders (SAEs) trained on over 100,000 multiplexed images of patient-derived Parkinson's disease and healthy neurons to resolve superposition. This approach bypasses the mathematical non-uniqueness of feature attribution by shifting to interpretable latent representation analysis. We theoretically and empirically demonstrate that superposition contaminates representational metric spaces, and thereby SAEs successfully recover geometric fidelity. By treating these geometrically purified representations as single-cell state vectors, we adapted single-cell RNA sequencing (scRNA-seq) data analysis methodologies directly to the image domain. Finally, we introduce GW-map, utilizing Gromov-Wasserstein optimal transport to align these image representations with authentic scRNA-seq data \emph{de novo}. This coupling reconstructs hierarchical neuronal pathology pathways such as Calcium-AIS scaffold, without reference spatial transcriptomics, establishing a scalable foundation for spatial biology. Code is available at https://github.com/jijihihi/Bio_superposition

</details>


### [171] [Mixture-of-Control: State-Aware Fine-Tuning for Transformer-based Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31397)
*Duc Anh Nguyen,Tien Ngoc Luu,Tung Pham,Toan Tran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Mixture-of-Control (MoC)框架，通过将块级控制状态视为稀疏混合专家模型中的专家，实现跨Transformer块的高效通信，在保持内存和计算效率的同时，优于现有基于状态的微调方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于状态的微调方法通常仅进行逐块控制更新，限制了跨块信息交换和表征适应性；而先前的跨块通信机制引入了大量计算开销，降低了高效微调的实用性。

Method: 提出Mixture-of-Control (MoC)框架，将块级控制状态视为稀疏混合专家模型中的专家，自适应地整合局部和全局控制信号，实现跨Transformer块的高效通信。

Result: 在多种基于Transformer的基准任务上，MoC优于现有基于状态的微调方法，同时保持了可比较的内存和计算效率。

Conclusion: MoC通过稀疏混合专家机制实现了高效的跨块通信，在不增加显著计算开销的前提下提升了表征学习能力，是一种实用且高效的状态基微调框架。

Abstract: State-based fine-tuning has emerged as a compelling alternative to weight-based adaptation for transformers, updating lightweight controls into states rather than model weights, offering substantial memory savings while retaining parameter efficiency. However, most existing state-based methods typically apply only per-block control updates, which limits inter-block information exchange and restricts representational adaptation. Meanwhile, prior mechanisms that enable cross-block communication often introduce considerable computational overhead, reducing their practicality for efficient fine-tuning. We introduce Mixture-of-Control (MoC), a lightweight fine-tuning framework that adaptively integrates local and global control signals to enhance representation learning. MoC treats block-wise control states as experts in a sparse mixture-of-experts process, enabling efficient communication across transformer blocks. Empirical results across diverse transformer-based benchmarks demonstrate that MoC outperforms state-based methods while maintaining a comparable memory and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [172] [Contextual Slate GLM Bandits with Limited Adaptivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31449)
*Tanmay Goyal,Sukruta Prakash Midigeshi,Gaurav Sinha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限自适应下的上下文带状石板赌博机问题，提出B-SlateGLinCB（分批设置，仅需O(log log T)个批次）和RS-SlateGLinCB（罕见切换设置，仅O(Nd log T)次参数更新）两种算法。在多样性假设下，分别达到O(Nd^{3/2}√T)和O(Nd√T)的遗憾界，且不依赖于GLM的固定非线性参数κ。算法计算高效，每轮仅需poly(N)时间。实验表明算法在有限自适应下优于基线，并在语言模型上下文示例选择任务中表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有石板赌博机算法通常需要每轮更新策略，但在许多实际场景中（如在线广告推荐、语言模型示例选择），频繁更新不可行或代价高昂。因此，研究如何在有限自适应（分批或罕见切换）条件下，仍能实现低遗憾的上下文石板赌博机算法。

Method: 针对分批设置提出B-SlateGLinCB，将时间划分为O(log log T)个批次，每批策略仅依赖之前批次数据；针对罕见切换设置提出RS-SlateGLinCB，仅进行O(Nd log T)次参数自适应更新。两种算法均基于广义线性模型（GLM），并利用多样性假设来消除GLM中非线性参数κ对遗憾界的影响。算法通过有效枚举和计算实现每轮poly(N)的时间复杂度。

Result: B-SlateGLinCB达到O(Nd^{3/2}√T)遗憾界，RS-SlateGLinCB达到O(Nd√T)遗憾界，两者均不依赖于GLM的非线性参数κ。在模拟实验中，两种算法在有限自适应设置下优于现有基线，且与全自适应最优算法Slate-GLM-OFU竞争力相当。修改后的B-SlateGLinCB在经验上与全自适应基线持平。在语言模型上下文示例选择任务中表现良好。

Conclusion: 本文成功将有限自适应技术引入石板赌博机问题的广义线性奖励场景，证明了即使大幅限制自适应更新频率，仍能获得与全自适应算法相当的遗憾性能，且计算高效。结果对在线推荐系统和语言模型的实际部署具有重要价值。

Abstract: We investigate the contextual slate bandit problem with generalized linear rewards under limited adaptivity. At each round, the learner is presented with $N$ sets of items, where each item is represented by a $d$-dimensional feature vector. The learner then constructs a slate by selecting one item per set; the resulting slate yields a scalar reward sampled from a Generalized Linear Model (GLM). We propose algorithms under two limited-adaptivity settings: (a) Batched and (b) Rarely-Switching. For the batched setting, we introduce B-SlateGLinCB, which partitions the time horizon into $\mathcal{O}(\log\log T)$ batches such that each batch's policy relies only on data from previous batches. For the rarely-switching setting, we propose RS-SlateGLinCB, which adaptively performs only $\mathcal{O}(Nd\log T)$ parameter updates. Under a diversity assumption on the item sequences, we prove that B-SlateGLinCB and RS-SlateGLinCB achieve regret bounds of $\mathcal{O}(Nd^{3/2}\sqrt{T})$ and $\mathcal{O}(Nd\sqrt{T})$, respectively. Notably, both bounds are independent of the non-linearity parameter $κ$ that is typically found to scale the regret of GLM bandit algorithms. Our algorithms are computationally efficient, requiring only $\text{poly}(N)$ time per round despite $2^{Ω(N)}$ possible slates. Simulations show our algorithms outperform existing baselines with limited adaptivity and remain competitive with Slate-GLM-OFU, a fully adaptive state-of-the-art algorithm. Notably, a slightly modified B-SlateGLinCB empirically matches this baseline. Finally, we demonstrate strong performance in a practical in-context example selection task for language models.

</details>


### [173] [Zero-Shot Quantization for Object Detectors using Off-the-Shelf Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31456)
*Hyunho Lee,Kyomin Hwang,Hyeonjin Kim,Suyoung Kim,Sunghyun Wee,Nojun Kwak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GoodQ提出了一种利用现成生成模型构建训练集的零样本量化感知训练管道，通过信息密集提示、内在分布感知选择和教师引导的自适应降噪策略，解决了低比特区域目标检测量化中的三个关键挑战，在W4A4和W3A3等极端比特宽度上取得了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本量化感知训练（ZSQ-AT）方法通过噪声优化合成训练集，但在低比特区域难以维持性能。本文旨在解决当原始训练数据不可访问时，如何有效对目标检测模型进行低比特量化的问题。

Method: GoodQ提出三种策略：1）信息密集提示（Information-Dense Prompting）生成多实例图像；2）内在分布感知选择（Intrinsic Distribution-Aware Selection）匹配预训练类别分布；3）教师引导自适应降噪（Teacher-guided Adaptive Noise Reduction）缓解量化感知训练中的噪声信号。

Result: 在低比特零样本量化（W4A4）上达到最先进性能，并扩展到了极端比特宽度（W3A3）。

Conclusion: GoodQ有效解决了生成模型引入零样本目标检测量化任务时的三个关键挑战，并通过广泛分析揭示了其有效性的潜在因素。

Abstract: With an increasing number of Object Detection (OD) models being deployed on edge devices, Zero-Shot Quantization for OD (ZSQ-OD) aims to quantize these models when access to the original training data is prohibited. Existing research on Zero-Shot Quantization-Aware Training (QAT) for OD synthesizes training sets through noise optimization. However, this approach struggles to maintain performance in low-bit regions. In this paper, we introduce GoodQ (Generative off-the-shelf models for object detector Quantization), a QAT pipeline that utilizes off-the-shelf generative models to construct a training set. We first identify three challenges that arise when introducing a generative model to the ZSQ-OD task: 1) each image contains dense information with multiple instances, 2) the class-wise distribution in the original dataset is imbalanced, and 3) the pseudo-labels assigned to the generated images can potentially act as noisy signals during QAT. GoodQ addresses these challenges by 1) introducing an Information-Dense Prompting strategy to generate multi-instance images, 2) applying Intrinsic Distribution-Aware Selection to match the pretrained class distribution, and 3) employing Teacher-guided Adaptive Noise Reduction to mitigate noise arising from the QAT process. Our framework achieves state-of-the-art performance in low-bit ZSQ (W4A4) and extends quantization to extreme bit-widths (W3A3). Furthermore, we conduct an extensive analysis to uncover the underlying factors contributing to the efficacy of GoodQ.

</details>


### [174] [TabPATE: Differentially Private Tabular In-Context Learning Without Public Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31474)
*Dariush Wahdany,Matthew Jagielski,Jesse C. Cresswell,Adam Dziedzic,Franziska Boenisch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TabPATE，一种无需公开数据的差分隐私PATE式防御方法，用于保护表格基础模型在上下文学习中的成员隐私，通过分区私有上下文、私有聚合教师标签并生成学生上下文，在保持实用性的同时将成员推断攻击成功率降至接近随机水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型在上下文学习中能够利用少量标注数据进行准确预测，但上下文中的私有记录可能通过模型预测泄露隐私。现有工作表明即使基本的成员推断攻击也能成功攻击表格ICL，因此需要形式化的隐私保护机制。

Method: TabPATE方法将私有上下文分区到多个教师模型中，对合成的表格查询进行私有聚合教师标签，然后释放带有标签的查询作为学生上下文。由于表格特征是有界且相对低维的，可以从特征范围或轻度私有化的边际分布生成有效的查询。

Result: 在多个表格基准测试上，TabPATE在保持竞争力的实用性的同时，将成员推断攻击成功率降低到接近随机水平。

Conclusion: TabPATE提供了一种无需公开数据即可实现私有表格上下文学习的实用路径。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models enable accurate in-context learning (ICL) from small labeled datasets, but the private records placed in context can leak through model predictions. We first show that even basic membership inference attacks succeed against tabular ICL, motivating formal privacy protection. We then introduce TabPATE, a differentially private PATE-style defense for tabular ICL that does not require public in-distribution data. TabPATE partitions the private context across teacher models, privately aggregates their labels on synthetic tabular queries, and releases the resulting labeled queries as a student context. Because tabular features are bounded and relatively low-dimensional, useful queries can be generated from feature ranges alone or from lightly privatized marginals. Across tabular benchmarks, TabPATE preserves competitive utility while reducing membership inference to near-random success, providing a practical path to private tabular ICL without public data.

</details>


### [175] [Constrained Online Convex Optimization without Slater's Condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31480)
*Kihyun Yu,Junehee Lee,Dabeen Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自适应正则化的原始-对偶框架，用于求解带随机或对抗约束的在线凸优化问题，无需Slater条件即可实现O(√T)的遗憾界和O(√T log T)的累积约束违反界，并扩展至强凸损失和高概率保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对随机约束的在线凸优化算法通常依赖Slater条件等正则性假设，而对抗约束算法则通过限制性的逐轮可行比较器来避免这些假设。本文旨在弥合这一差距，设计一种无需Slater条件即可同时处理随机和对抗约束的统一框架。

Method: 提出了一种随时（anytime）原始-对偶框架，在对偶更新中引入自适应正则化项。该正则化器能够稳定对偶过程，而不依赖Slater条件带来的负漂移效应。对随机约束，算法在凸损失下达到O(√T)期望遗憾和O(√T log T)期望累积约束违反；强凸损失下遗憾界改进为O(log T)。还给出了高概率保证。通过微小修改，框架也适用于对抗约束，并提供硬约束违反保证。

Result: 随机约束且凸损失：期望遗憾O(√T)，期望累积约束违反O(√T log T)；强凸损失：遗憾O(log T)，约束违反同阶；可扩展至高概率保证；对抗约束下可提供硬约束违反保证。

Conclusion: 本文提出的自适应正则化原始-对偶框架有效桥接了随机约束与对抗约束在线优化之间的差距，无需Slater条件即可实现接近最优的遗憾和约束违反界，具有较好的通用性和理论保证。

Abstract: We study constrained online convex optimization with adversarial losses and stochastic or adversarial constraints. For stochastic constraints, existing algorithms that achieve nearly optimal regret and constraint violation bounds typically rely on regularity assumptions such as Slater's condition, while adversarial-constraint algorithms avoid these assumptions by using a rather restrictive round-wise feasible comparator. We bridge this gap with an anytime primal-dual framework that incorporates an adaptive regularizer into the dual update. The regularizer stabilizes the dual process without relying on the negative drift induced by Slater's condition. For stochastic constraints and convex losses, our algorithm achieves $O(\sqrt{T})$ expected regret and $O(\sqrt{T}\log T)$ expected cumulative constraint violation. Furthermore, we show that our algorithm also admits high-probability bounds of the same order on regret and constraint violation. For strongly convex losses, the regret bound improves to $O(\log T)$ with a violation bound of the same order. With a minor modification, the framework also applies to adversarial constraints and provides guarantees for hard constraint violation.

</details>


### [176] [Fork-Think with Confidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31484)
*Zena Al-Khalili,Rafi Hakim,Dietrich Klakow,Ji-Ung Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Fork-think方法，通过在单个推理路径中基于模型置信度识别分叉点，再触发多路径采样与聚合，相比传统并行推理方法可减少最多30%的token消耗和57%的运行时间，同时保持或提升推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有并行推理方法遵循"先思考后决策"范式，即先采样多条推理路径再剪枝，导致过度生成和资源浪费。"先决策后思考"范式（先识别有潜力的点再触发推理）尚未被充分探索。

Method: 在单个播种路径（seeding path）中，利用模型置信度识别分叉点（forking points），在这些点触发多路径采样（multiple continuations），并对采样结果进行聚合得到最终响应。

Result: 在3个模型和3个推理基准上，Fork-think减少最多30%的token消耗和57%的运行时间，性能与并行推理相当或更优。结合早停和加权投票等机制可达到与现有SOTA方法相当的水平，且无需预热或离线训练。

Conclusion: 预确定分叉点（pre-determined forking）是高效LLM推理的一个有前景的研究方向。

Abstract: Parallel thinking has enjoyed great success for boosting LLM performance on reasoning tasks without the need for any re-training. However, existing methods follow a think-first-then-decide paradigm, i.e., they first sample multiple reasoning paths, which inevitably leads to overgeneration, then prune or stop unnecessary paths to compensate. In contrast, decide-first-then-think, i.e., first identifying points that are likely to lead to desirable generations, has been underexplored so far. Following this paradigm, we propose Fork-think with confidence, that first identifies forking points using model confidence in a single seeding path, then triggers thinking, sampling multiple continuations and aggregating them for the final response. Our experiments across three models and three reasoning benchmarks show that Fork-think reduces the token consumption by up to 30% and run-time by up to 57%, while performing comparable to or better than parallel thinking. Our analysis reveals that Fork-think is able to identify forking points that are meaningful with respect to the downstream task and that sampling at later positions can lead to substantially better generations. Finally, we demonstrate how combining Fork-think with existing mechanisms such as early stopping and weighted voting can further boost the performance and perform comparably to existing state-of-the-art methods, without requiring any warm-up or offline training. Our results establish pre-determined forking as a promising research direction for efficient LLM reasoning.

</details>


### [177] [RaBitQCache: Rotated Binary Quantization for KVCache in Long Context LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31519)
*Wenhao Li,Jinhao Dong,Hailin Zhang,Wenhang Shi,Wei Lu,Xiaoyong Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RaBitQCache提出了一种基于随机旋转二值量化和二进制-INT4算术的新型稀疏注意力框架，通过无偏估计和自适应Top-p检索动态调整token预算，并实现了硬件感知的异步流水线和延迟更新来降低开销，在加速推理和减少内存I/O的同时保持生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文大语言模型推理中，巨大的键值缓存（KV cache）成为严重瓶颈，而现有稀疏注意力方法要么使用静态固定预算（Top-k）检索，要么依赖计算成本高且有偏的代理分数。

Method: 利用随机旋转二值量化（RaBitQ）和二进制-INT4算术高效估计注意力权重，设计具有理论误差边界的无偏估计代理分数，实现自适应Top-p检索动态调整token预算，并采用异步流水线和延迟更新等硬件感知优化。

Result: 与现有最先进基线相比，RaBitQCache显著加速了推理速度并减少了内存I/O，同时保持了生成质量。

Conclusion: 随机旋转二值量化结合自适应Top-p检索为长上下文LLM的稀疏注意力提供了一种高效且高质量的解决方案。

Abstract: Long-context Large Language Model inference is severely bottlenecked by the massive Key-Value (KV) cache, yet existing sparse attention methods often suffer from static fixed-budget (Top-k) retrieval or rely on proxy scores that are computationally expensive and biased. To address these limitations, we propose RaBitQCache, a novel sparse attention framework that utilizes randomized rotated binary quantization and high-throughput binary-INT4 arithmetic to efficiently estimate attention weights. Our proxy score serves as an unbiased estimator with a proven error bound, enabling adaptive Top-p retrieval that dynamically adjusts the token budget based on actual attention sparsity. We further implement a hardware-aware system with asynchronous pipelining and lazy updates to mask overhead. Evaluations demonstrate that RaBitQCache significantly accelerates inference and reduces memory I/O while preserving generation quality compared to state-of-the-art baselines. Code is available at https://github.com/Sakuraaa0/RaBitQCache.git.

</details>


### [178] [On the Convergence of Self-Improving Online LLM Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31524)
*Xudong Wu,Pangpang Liu,Vaneet Aggarwal,Jiayu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SAIL-RevKL算法，通过加入反向KL散度正则化项解决原始SAIL目标函数非强凹的问题，证明了该正则化目标在有限参数空间内满足PL条件，并建立全局收敛保证（近线性样本复杂度），在MuJoCo和LLM对齐任务上优于原始SAIL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始SAIL算法虽在实践中表现良好，但其目标函数的Hessian矩阵具有不利性质，无法保证强凹性，导致缺乏严格的收敛性理论分析。作者旨在通过正则化手段修复这一理论缺陷。

Method: 提出SAIL-RevKL算法，在原始SAIL目标函数中引入反向KL散度惩罚项以改善优化景观；通过理论分析证明该正则化目标在有限参数空间内满足Polyak-Lojasiewicz (PL)条件；建立全局收敛性证明并给出近线性样本复杂度上界。

Result: 理论层面：证明了SAIL-RevKL满足PL条件，具有全局收敛保证和近线性样本复杂度。实验层面：在MuJoCo基准和LLM对齐任务上均优于原始SAIL算法，验证了有效性和稳定性。

Conclusion: 反向KL正则化有效解决了原始SAIL的非强凹问题，使得算法兼具理论收敛保证和实际性能优势，为自改进对齐算法的理论分析提供了重要进展。

Abstract: The Self-Improving Alignment (SAIL) algorithm addresses distribution shift by reducing a bilevel formulation of the problem to an efficient, single-level method. Empirically, SAIL has demonstrated strong performance on this task. However, a formal analysis of its convergence properties has been lacking. We identify a key theoretical challenge: the standard SAIL objective function is not guaranteed to be strongly concave due to unfavorable properties of its Hessian. To address this limitation, we propose a regularized objective, SAIL-RevKL, which incorporates a reverse Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence penalty to improve the optimization landscape. Our central theoretical contribution is to prove that this regularized objective satisfies the Polyak-Lojasiewicz (PL) condition within a bounded parameter space. We establish global convergence guarantees, achieving a near-linear sample complexity. We further validate the effectiveness and stability of SAIL-RevKL through empirical evaluations, demonstrating that it outperforms the vanilla SAIL on both MuJoCo benchmarks and LLM alignment tasks.

</details>


### [179] [Beyond the Expressivity-Trainability Paradox: A Dynamical Lie Algebra Perspective on Navigating Barren Plateaus in Quantum Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31536)
*Kung-Ming Lan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了量子机器学习中一个反直觉现象：非结构化的参数化量子电路（PQC）因希尔伯特空间容量过大而导致"表达力-可训练性悖论"——巨大的状态空间虽被认为是量子优势的来源，却直接引发梯度消失的贫瘠高原（Barren Plateaus）问题。通过动态李代数（DLA）和几何QML框架，作者证明限制DLA代数维度的多项式增长（即加入群论几何先验）可作为结构性正则化器，牺牲记忆容量换取可扩展的梯度丰富训练景观，提出"可训练性由设计保证"的量子神经网络架构路线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决QML从理论走向实践时面临的架构瓶颈：非结构化PQC在增加模型容量时不仅不会过拟合（如经典学习理论所预期），反而陷入严重的量子欠拟合状态，其根源在于表达力与可训练性之间的根本矛盾。

Method: 综合动态李代数（DLA）与几何QML的最新进展，建立电路生成元代数维度与优化动力学之间的理论联系；并在非线性二分类任务上进行实证验证，对比非结构化架构与嵌入群论几何先验的对称保持架构的性能差异。

Result: 非结构化架构通过不可扩展的参数化实现近乎完美的训练准确率（量子过拟合），但面临贫瘠高原；而限制DLA多项式生长的对称保持方法作为结构性正则化器，虽牺牲了原始记忆容量，却保证了可扩展且梯度丰富的训练景观。

Conclusion: 提出"Trainability-by-Design"（可训练性由设计保证）的量子神经网络范式，强调在可扩展的QML中，通过几何先验限制架构的表达力是克服贫瘠高原、实现实际可行的量子机器学习的必要路径。

Abstract: As Quantum Machine Learning (QML) transitions toward practical implementation, the field faces a critical architectural bottleneck that challenges the fundamental assumptions of classical statistical learning theory. In classical deep learning, increasing model capacity typically risks overfitting.
